Chapter I N100 Grammar and Exact Work

The source-derived N100 Hamiltonian was solved exactly by two routes.
H14F returned its complete retained-role density of states in
3.828137395 seconds; conditional-component enumeration and exact
convolution returned the same coefficients in 0.500951441 seconds. Both
account for all 2¹⁰⁰ assignments. The source census fixes the object,
and the factorization explains the smaller amount of local work.

The historical constructions in this chapter retain the fixed 8
September 2026 SAMA source stage. Later results are identified by their
own source records.

Overview

N100 is an exact frustrated-spin computation whose favorable
computational structure comes from its source grammar. The mathematical
object is the unchanged 100-spin, 260-edge Hamiltonian with

2¹⁰⁰ = 1, 267, 650, 600, 228, 229, 401, 496, 703, 205, 376

binary assignments. The first complete H14F route returns the full exact
density-of-states tensor in 3.828137395 seconds. A later structure-first
route detects eight conditional components, executes 165,920 local
assignments and convolves them to the coefficientwise-identical tensor
in 0.500951441 seconds. The reduced workload is an exact evaluation
strategy; it does not reduce the state space, change the Hamiltonian,
sample the assignments or request only a ground state.

The 100-row census is itself source-bound. The visible QP lane contains
105 records. Exactly one of the 21 complete five-role packet
exclusions; the p=9,g=0 packet; produces 100 independently counted rows
with exact native inventory 16,200 and mean 162. The five excluded-count
W9 witnesses remain visible as derived X1+W8 receipts. The A field
remains present as non-row infrastructure, and X1 remains
non-row/non-matter. Those are three different boundaries.

The central computational finding is not merely the observed wall time.
The source grammar contains exact decomposition certificates, and the
compiler can use them while preserving the complete result. Its workload
also returns the ledger quantity L162 through an independent arithmetic
identity. The N105, N81 and N72 controls show exactly which parts depend
on the source grammar and which do not.

Opening question and conceptual picture

The governing question is:

  Can a source-derived 100-row grammar expose enough exact structure to
  compute the complete 2^100 density-of-states tensor without visiting
  every assignment one by one, while returning exactly the same
  mathematical output?

The answer is represented by two equivalent exact routes:

- source-derived N100 Hamiltonian

- complete H14F variable-elimination route

- exact retained-role DOS tensor

- same unchanged Hamiltonian

- detect exact conditional components

- compute each local exact tensor

- exact tensor/polynomial convolution

- coefficientwise-identical retained-role DOS tensor

The grammar does not replace the calculation. It tells the exact solver
how the complete sum can be factorized.

Definitions and cardinality firewall

Exact frustrated spin density of states

For binary spins

σ_(i) ∈ {−1, +1},   i = 1, …, N

and a declared weighted graph, the exact Hamiltonian has the form

H(σ) = ∑_((i, j) ∈ E)J_(ij)σ_(i)σ_(j) + ∑_(i)h_(i)σ_(i)

The density of states is the integer function

g(E) = #{σ ∈ {−1, +1}^(N) : H(σ) = E}

Completeness requires

∑_(E)g(E) = 2^(N)

For N100, N=100. Frustration changes the organization of energy and the
ground sector; it does not change the formal assignment count.

Four quantities that must not be merged

- N100 source rows. Exact value: 100; Type: independent spins of the
  declared Hamiltonian.

- complete configuration space. Exact value: 2^100; Type: every formal
  binary assignment counted by the returned tensor.

- structure-first work. Exact value: 165,920; Type: local assignments
  evaluated before exact convolution.

- L162 native ledger mean. Exact value: 162; Type:
  source-ledger/accounting identity, not a coupling or state count.

The statement “exact 2^100” refers to the tensor’s complete count. It
does not say the runner iterated through 2^100 bit strings. Conversely,
the 165,920 workload is not a claim that the physical or mathematical
problem had only 165,920 states.

The row census exclusions are separately typed

The N100 census is produced after one historical five-row packet is
excluded from independent content. Preserve these three boundaries:

- p=9,g=0 packet: five derived W9 witnesses, visible but non-counted

- A-field row: excluded because A is active non-row infrastructure

- X1: response channel, not a physical/source row

Similar arithmetic around the 163-to-162 ledger does not make the three
objects equivalent.

Source values are not Hamiltonian couplings

The QP rows supply typed identity, topology and role information through
a separately declared compiler. Their native numerical values are not
inserted as J_ij or h_i merely because they occur on the same record.
The tested Hamiltonian is the source-derived 100-spin, 260-edge graph
fixed before the structure-first route.

Deriving the 100 row source census

Begin with the full visible lane

CR120R begins from 105 visible source rows arranged into 21 complete
five-role address packets. The exact source totals are

- source rows 105

- complete five-role packets 21

- address-value sum 2889

- full native inventory 130005/8 = 16250.625

Every source row remains present in the result artifact. The operation
is a counted/not-counted overlay, not deletion.

Test every one packet exclusion

Each of the 21 five-role packets is removed in turn from
independent-content accounting. A valid candidate must leave 100 rows
and close the L162 mean:

$$\frac{M_{native,remaining}}{100}=162$$

Exactly one candidate does so:

(p, g) = (9, 0)

The resulting payload is

- excluded typed packet p=9,g=0, five rows

- payload rows 100

- payload native inventory 16200

- payload mean 162=L

- single-packet L162 hits 1

Thus

$$\frac{16{,}200}{100}=162$$

Verify the derived parent relation

Each same-role p=9,g=0 record closes exactly as the sum of its p=8,g=0
and p=1,g=0 parents in M_native. This is the source-typed W9 route

8 + 1 → 9

expressed on five records. The type controls prevent the scalar 9 from
collapsing W9_CLOSURE_WITNESS, C9_CARRIER_ATOM and P9_BIGRADE_PARTITION
into one object. Weak-vector and hidden-source p9 support rows are also
barred from the selector, and the identity is not extended to g1, g2,
composite routes or qA.

Reconcile the payload

The derived 100 payload IDs and values reconcile one-for-one with the
current Updated Particle Rows.xlsx extraction recorded by CR120R. This
closes the source census used by the N100 computation while retaining
the five witnesses as provenance.

Constructing the exact N100 problem

Freeze the graph and requested output

The declared problem contains:

- 100 independent binary spins;

- 260 source-derived edges;

- induced width 5 under the complete H14F route;

- a retained-role conditional DOS tensor;

- a 16-row scalar closure; and

- exact integer coefficients reconstructed across four CRT primes.

The requested output is the complete tensor, not only the minimum energy
or a small collection of moments. The graph, source roles and tensor
schema are fixed before either timing result. The retained source packet
is p = 1, g = 0, with role order plus, minus, anti-plus and anti-minus.

Why exact routes can be smaller than the state count

The complete sum

g(E) = ∑_(σ ∈ {−1, +1}¹⁰⁰)1{H(σ) = E}

can be rearranged without approximation. Variable elimination sums
internal spins while retaining exact boundary spectra. Disconnected
conditional components can be computed independently and convolved.
Modular NTT lanes can multiply polynomials exactly, and CRT reconstructs
the integer coefficients once the modulus product exceeds the
coefficient range.

All such routes remain exact if they reproduce every coefficient and
close the complete state count.

Complete H14F route from execution to closure

Execute the declared Hamiltonian

The first route applies H14F variable elimination to the full
source-derived graph. It produces:

- spins. Exact result: 100.

- source edges. Exact result: 260.

- represented configurations. Exact result: 2^100.

- occupied energy bins. Exact result: 177.

- ground sector. Exact result: (-260,256).

- distinct port spectra. Exact result: 3.

- singleton root tasks. Exact result: 2,048.

- CRT primes. Exact result: 4.

- observed runner wall. Exact result: 3.828137395 s.

The ordered pair (-260,256) records the ground energy and its
degeneracy. The calculation retains the other 176 occupied bins as well.

Close the port marginals

Four boundary bits produce 16 retained port rows. Each row sums to

2⁹⁶

because fixing the four retained bits leaves 96 independent spins.
Summing all 16 rows gives

16 ⋅ 2⁹⁶ = 2⁴ ⋅ 2⁹⁶ = 2¹⁰⁰

This is both a marginal check and a full-state closure.

The conditional spectra depend on the number of positive retained signs.
Zero or four positive signs give ground energy −260, ground count 128
and 174 occupied bins. One or three give ground energy −250, ground
count 128 and 174 bins. Two positive signs give ground energy −244,
ground count 256 and 171 bins. Representative masks are 0, 1, 3, 7 and
15 for positive counts zero through four. Thus the sixteen boundary rows
resolve three distinct spectra.

Close source symmetries

All 24 source-role permutations reproduce the required tensor relation.
The global spin complement also closes. These checks distinguish a
complete source-role tensor from an accidental scalar DOS total.

The four recorded prime-kernel walls are approximately 0.890328,
0.852483, 0.797375 and 0.799622 seconds, within the 3.828137395-second
runner total. The scalar DOS semantic hash is
6e404623175ba60629110fb17f75ca0f0398e690db9de7b7a97766d32d0c2d30; the
retained tensor semantic hash is
33ec2e3f5cd3a1e66eb892730181e89bb1aff71d93b97fbbba4959bb238bd78d. These
identities are the equality targets for the structure-first route.

Structure first route from components to the same tensor

Detect exact conditional components

The source grammar exposes eight components with sizes

15, 15, 15, 15, 15, 10, 10, 5

They are conditional components: any shared retained port variables
remain explicit, while the interior variables of each component can be
summed locally.

Compute local exact tensors

For component c and retained boundary assignment b, define its exact
energy polynomial

P_(c, b)(x) = ∑_(E)g_(c, b)(E)x^(E)

Exhaustive local work for the eight interiors is

W₁₀₀ = 5 ⋅ 2¹⁵ + 2 ⋅ 2¹⁰ + 2⁵

Evaluate each term:

5 ⋅ 2¹⁵ = 5 ⋅ 32, 768 = 163, 840

2 ⋅ 2¹⁰ = 2, 048,   2⁵ = 32

so

W₁₀₀ = 163, 840 + 2, 048 + 32 = 165, 920

Convolve rather than discard cross component sums

For each retained boundary assignment, multiply the local polynomials:

$$P_b(x)=\prod_{c=1}^{8}P_{c,b}(x)$$

Polynomial multiplication performs the full energy convolution: every
choice of one local state from each component contributes to the
coefficient at the sum of their energies. Summing or assembling over the
retained boundary assignments returns the same retained-role tensor as
the complete route.

No state is sampled or dropped. Factorization evaluates the product
structure of the full sum. When local role coordinates must be assembled
into the global retained role, the same reconstruction is written
$$P_{100}(z,r)=\sum_{r_1\oplus\cdots\oplus r_8=r}\prod_{a=1}^{8}P_a(z,r_a)$$
At z = 1, complete local counts give
P₁₀₀(1) = ∏_(a)2^(n_(a)) = 2^(∑_(a)n_(a)) = 2¹⁰⁰.

Compare complete outputs

The structure-first wall is 0.500951441 seconds. Relative to the
3.828137395 second H14F route, the observed ratio is approximately

$$\frac{3.828137395}{0.500951441}\approx7.642$$

The load-bearing comparison is stronger than timing: the complete
tensors have the same coefficient hash and match coefficient by
coefficient. The Hamiltonian, state space, energy support and output
schema are unchanged.

Grammar to work identity derived line by line

The source ledger supplies

N100_(native) = 100 ⋅ 162 = 16, 200

The exact component work can be regrouped as

165, 920 = 165, 888 + 32 = 162 ⋅ 1, 024 + 32

Therefore

W₁₀₀ = 162 ⋅ 2¹⁰ + 2⁵

Subtract the residual and divide by the ten-bit block:

$$\frac{W_{100}-2^5}{2^{10}}
=\frac{165{,}920-32}{1{,}024}
=\frac{165{,}888}{1{,}024}
=162$$

There is a second exact form. Because

72² = 5, 184 = 32 ⋅ 162

we have

W₁₀₀ = 2⁵(72² + 1) = 32(5, 184 + 1) = 165, 920

The residual 2^5 is the binary assignment space of the surviving
five-role p=9,g=1 packet. It is not X1, Theta18 or a physical closure
object. The identity connects two independently sourced structures; the
row ledger and the exact workload; but the value 16,200 is not inserted
into the Hamiltonian and does not mechanically cause the runtime.

Source native controls and deviations

N105 restored packet control

Restore the historical p=9,g=0 packet. The workload becomes

W₁₀₅ = 166, 912 = 163 ⋅ 2¹⁰

The difference is

ΔW = 166, 912 − 165, 920 = 992 = 2¹⁰ − 2⁵

The restoration also changes the N100/N81 completion-path capacity from
40 to 45 rows. The clean 162 identity is therefore sensitive to the
typed packet boundary; it is not an identity forced by any 100-variable
benchmark.

N81 octahedral control

The N81 source-native control has component sizes

[11, 10, 11, 10, 10, 11, 10, 8]

and exact work

10, 496 = 2⁷(81 + 1)

Its octahedral bridge retains 48 cube embeddings in one full-octahedral
orbit, the completion path 1-8-9-12, reflected C4 gluing and 49,152
incidences. The N100/N81 localization remains 40->40 under all nine
insertions. These are exact geometry/compiler leads; a causal account of
the timing is still a separate research question.

N72 dense contrast

N72 remains an independently dense computation surface. It is not edited
to look like N100. This control prevents the source-structure finding
from being rephrased as a universal rule that nominally smaller N must
be faster or that all graphs admit the same component decomposition.

Unchanged parent control

The N100 graph and requested tensor were frozen before the
structure-first timing route. The earlier H14F result already supplied
the complete tensor. Coefficientwise equality therefore tests an
optimization of representation on an unchanged parent rather than a
retuned benchmark.

Source native compiler boundary

The complete N100 computation and the later N100-to-F81 semantic
compiler are different tasks. G1 audits the latter and records:

- typed N100 QP source keys. Coverage: 100/100.

- Shared72 aggregate connector-incidence eligibility. Coverage: 72/100.

- CR211 address rows. Coverage: 162.

- CR211 rows with QP elementwise assignment. Coverage: 0.

- F81 semantic sites. Coverage: 81.

- frozen F81 contact classes. Coverage: 10.

- N100 rows with complete elementwise F81 map. Coverage: 0.

The missing key is

- QP_CANDIDATE_OR_TEMPLATE_TO_F81_SEMANTIC_SITE_OR_SOURCE_NATIVE_F81_ADDRESS_CLASS

CR211 explicitly leaves address-to-template incidence null, and the F81
target schema has no QP source key. G1 therefore records
PARTIAL_DOMAIN__ELEMENTWISE_F81_JOIN_OPEN; the contact lane is
NOT_EXECUTED__SOURCE_NATIVE_ELEMENTWISE_COMPILER_OPEN. No result or
residual selects a map.

This boundary does not retract the exact N100 Hamiltonian calculation.
It says that a later semantic join from each source row to an F81
site/address class has not been supplied. Aggregate Shared72 eligibility
cannot substitute for 100 elementwise assignments.

Execution architecture and additional controls

The same reconstruction obligation governs component, orbit, cutset,
fiber and rank-aware routes. An admitted structural certificate
specifies how the requested complete tensor is rebuilt. H14F supplies
the fallback when no reduced route closes. The following contracts and
controls extend the N100 example to explicitly different source
structures.

Typed definitions and execution contract

Mathematical object versus execution profile

For a fixed Hamiltonian H, define the retained-role tensor

G(e, r) = #{σ : H(σ) = e, R(σ) = r}

where r is one of the declared port-role states. Its scalar DOS is

g(e) = ∑_(r)G(e, r)

The mathematical object consists of H, the assignment domain, the role
map R, and every requested coefficient G(e, r). H14C and H14F specify
how that object is evaluated. They may change elimination order,
batching, residue storage, worker persistence and cache policy. They may
not change H, delete assignments, reinterpret the roles or replace G
with a smaller target.

H14C and H14F

H14C is the completed fourteen-core profile with complete-prime
calibration and the accepted retained-capacity contract. H14F adds
mixed-width compilation, persistent workers, reduced residue storage and
an ephemeral scale-specific projection cache. Both are exact execution
profiles.

Where an H14F campaign declares fourteen pinned physical workers, that
width is part of the frozen architecture. It cannot silently become
“whatever number of host workers is available,” because batching,
custody and timing receipts are indexed to the declared worker surface.
A new worker mapping would be a successor execution profile, not a
reinterpretation of the old one.

Structural certificate

A structural certificate is exact evidence that the requested object can
be reconstructed from smaller typed objects. Examples include:

- disconnected components after an explicit conditioning boundary;

- a permutation orbit on which energy depends only on marked population;

- a cutset whose fixed assignments reduce admitted elimination width;

- a fee-exempt fiber whose multiplicity can be lifted exactly; and

- a constrained readout with a known independent rank.

The certificate must contain the reconstruction rule. A
low-width-looking plot or favorable runtime is not enough.

Router and fallback

The routing policy is

- if conditional components close:

- evaluate each component exactly and convolve

- elif uniform orbit sectors close:

- count exact marked-population sectors

- elif a declared cutset lowers admitted width:

- branch exactly on every cutset assignment

- elif the output is a constrained fiber/readout:

- evaluate independent rank and reconstruct the ambient view

- else:

- execute the exact H14F variable-elimination fallback

Every branch returns the complete requested tensor. Failure to certify
one branch is a routing outcome, not permission to approximate.

Mixed connectivity controls

The installation tournament adds two controlled deviations:

- a one-edge partial join, width 5, ground sector ((-261,128)); and

- a connected ring, width 5, ground sector ((-268,2)).

Both complete sixteen-row tensors equal an independent
original-component bridge contraction coefficient for coefficient. The
partial join retains 128 component-orientation choices; the connected
cyclic skeleton leaves the two global orientations. These tests show
that a changed coupling topology can be handled exactly when its
reconstruction rule is explicit.

Dense N96 fallback

The full N96 graph remains width 24. A frozen five-site cutset creates

2⁵ = 32

exact branches, each admitted to H14F at width 22. This is neither a
component convolution nor an approximate shortcut. Every cutset
assignment is retained, and H14F performs the exact branch work. The
regression ran 4,096 fresh tasks and retained the frozen N96 numerical
authority.

Routing qualification

The installation gates execute this logic rather than merely state it.
They record 12/12 route checks, 18/18 mixed arithmetic checks, 9/9
N18/N54 checks, 16/16 N72 checks and 20/20 N96 checks: 75/75 combined
executed checks. Their admission was for a separate owner-authorized
promotion decision; the gate campaign itself did not silently rewrite a
current pointer.

Recorded authority and delegated base status

Historical H14F, structure-first, Exact Write, W9P/Theta, support/Gram,
tensor-orbit and bounded application artifacts remain frozen within the
SLCV21R arithmetic/application lineage. Recorded authority is SLCQ2-RZ,
the source-recorded default hybrid ordinal selection engine with 70
exact source-visible features. It delegates byte-identical
arithmetic/application services to the frozen SLCV21R base, retaining
the exact H14F fallback and structure-first routing lineage.

SLCV21R is noncurrent as a revision, but its declared services remain
the delegated base. Q1 is the immutable fallback. Q2 does not install
physical calibration, Binding, ATOM3D or RH merely by consuming base
arithmetic. H14F therefore remains load-bearing without becoming the
current selection engine.

Established result boundary and forward handoff

The complete N100 result counts all 2¹⁰⁰ assignments of the unchanged
100-spin, 260-edge Hamiltonian and returns the full exact retained-role
tensor. The structure-first route evaluates 165,920 local assignments
and reconstructs that same tensor coefficientwise. The execution-work
reduction is established for this source grammar; it is not asserted for
every arbitrary 100-spin graph.

The router’s general result is procedural and exact: detect the source
certificate, preserve the requested object, reconstruct independently,
and retain H14F when no reduced route closes. N105, N81, N72,
mixed-graph and N96 controls define where each branch applies.

The forward handoff goes to SAMA-D000041, where Starbreaker’s own
grammar is routed without substituting the N100 graph. It also supports
the current-Q2 ordinal chapter SAMA-D000055, where exact base
computation and source-visible selection must remain different types.

Connections and forward handoff

- SAMA-D000027. Connection: Supplies the finite source grammar and
  row-count distinctions..

- SAMA-D000033. Connection: Supplies M126/N144/L162 types without
  merging them with N100..

- SAMA-D000035. Connection: Makes the grammar, type system, program
  custody and exact semantic path explicit..

- SAMA-D000036. Connection: Receives the exact DOS definition,
  coefficient closure and route families..

- SAMA-D000037. Connection: Receives the structure-first router and
  exact fallback policy..

- SAMA-P000006. Connection: Connects N100 computation back to the
  particle, Starbreaker and binding parent route..

The immediate handoff to D35 is a language requirement: source identity,
operator type, program tape, state and history must be serialized so the
grammar certificates can select an exact route without hidden UI or
wrapper semantics.

Chapter II SAM Language and Exact Program Semantics

SAM Language makes source identity, operator type, state and history
executable parts of one program contract. The retained formal simulator
implements parsing, resolution, checking, evaluation and deterministic
receipts; its G01-G16 records test those semantic and custody surfaces.
This chapter explains how that finite kernel preserves program meaning
through execution and reconstruction.

The historical constructions in this chapter retain the fixed 8
September 2026 SAMA source stage. Later results are identified by their
own source records.

Overview

SAM Language is the semantic boundary between a mathematical grammar and
an execution. It owns the parser, resolver, checker, type system,
operator definitions, program registry and evaluator. A UI, wrapper or
orchestration layer may transport a program, but it cannot acquire
authority to recognize a phrase and run hidden scientific logic. Exact
program meaning belongs to one declared language path.

That path carries more than a final state. It carries immutable source
and program custody, the typed current state, a separately typed
accumulated history, deterministic receipts and predecessor hashes. Two
programs may end at the same exact state while retaining different route
histories. A rejected operation must be atomic, and a tampered receipt
must fail custody rather than be silently reinterpreted.

The evidence has three time layers that must remain distinct. SAM
Language v0.6 is the preserved formal-simulator campaign whose G01-G16
records test the semantic and custody surfaces in detail. SAM Language
v0.7 is the later typed 12-site reflective-tape candidate. Neither is
the SLC version or the current SLC release. The live repository
authority is SLCQ2-RZ; historical language and SLC labels remain source
lineage, not current-pointer aliases.

Opening question and conceptual picture

The governing question is:

  How does a source grammar become one exact, reproducible computation
  without allowing interfaces, profiles, mutable tapes or hidden
  dispatch paths to change its scientific meaning?

The required architecture is

- source text or immutable instruction tape

- parser

- resolver

- checker and type system

- operator/program registry

- evaluator

- typed current state + typed history + custody receipt

The forbidden alternative is semantic branching outside the language:

- UI or wrapper recognizes a phrase

- runs an unrelated calculation

- returns a value as if the language produced it

The first architecture makes source, meaning and result traceable. The
second creates two semantic authorities and breaks provenance even if
the returned number happens to match.

Version and authority firewall

v0 6 preserved formal simulator evidence

The v0.6 campaign supplies the focused evidence in this document. It
tests one semantic path, formal-profile isolation, the complete 12-site
basis, frozen B and X1 operators, ordered-pair schedules, native
decompositions, state/history separation, deterministic interfaces,
atomic rejection, packaging and 23 wrong controls.

Its source statuses are PASS across G01-G16. Those statuses belong to
the sealed v0.6 test surface. They are not an authorized SAMA result
classification and do not make v0.6 the current language or SLC
revision.

v0 7 typed reflective tape candidate

The Volume III source spine records v0.7 as a later typed, source-linked
reflective SLC C1 tape candidate over twelve sites and 4,096 basis
addresses. Its first-class types include

- SLCInstructionTape

- SLCTape12

- SLCTapeInspection

Six declared operations create an immutable zero-origin tape, append
B/request/response records, execute it and inspect custody. The executor
validates the zero-origin hash chain, dispatches through inherited exact
state transitions and returns the same SLCState12 dossier as the
corresponding direct program.

The candidate remains bounded to a straight-line 12-site tape. It has no
self-hosted parser, recursive source generation or general
SAM-Language-to-Dense-Exact adapter. Tape custody and executed state
remain different types.

Source recorded SLC SLCQ2 RZ

The immutable Volume III spine contains historical SLC-current wording
from its creation time. At the retained source stage, live authority
controlled: SLCQ2-RZ is the sole frozen/default SLC, with its delegated
arithmetic/application base separately typed. SAM Language, the SAM UI
and SLC each have their own version lineage.

Accordingly:

- SAM Language v0.6 = preserved formal-simulator evidence

- SAM Language v0.7 = later typed reflective-tape candidate

- SLCQ2-RZ = sole source-recorded default SLC authority

No row in this document promotes one identity into another.

Definitions and type boundaries

Source tape state and history

- source text. Mutability: fixed for a declared execution; Semantic
  role: human-readable program origin.

- instruction tape. Mutability: immutable append/custody object;
  Semantic role: ordered typed program records and predecessor chain.

- current state. Mutability: transformed by accepted operators; Semantic
  role: exact endpoint dossier.

- accumulated history. Mutability: append-only typed receipt; Semantic
  role: ordered route, including distinctions erased at the endpoint.

- inspection. Mutability: read-only; Semantic role: custody and semantic
  trace readback.

Endpoint equality means

s_(final)⁽¹⁾ = s_(final)⁽²⁾

It does not imply

h⁽¹⁾ = h⁽²⁾

That distinction later supports Complete Exact Write and reciprocal
history.

The twelve site address domain

Let a basis state be a twelve-bit vector

b = (b₀, …, b₁₁),   b_(i) ∈ {0, 1}

Its canonical integer address is

$$a(b)=\sum_{i=0}^{11}b_i2^i$$

so

0 ≤ a < 2¹² = 4, 096

The finite address surface permits exhaustive basis, arity, range and
site-ID checks.

Operators and profiles

An operator is resolved by identifier through the registry, checked
against a typed signature, and executed only after the profile admits
it. The formal evidence profile is FORMAL_CANDIDATE; normal and research
profiles are rejected in G03. This prevents an application mode from
injecting semantics into a formal campaign.

Exact receipt

A receipt binds at least the declared program, predecessor lineage,
exact state, history and operator trace. The relevant invariants are
byte-stable serialization, deterministic ordering and exact
coefficients. Recomputing a hash for a mutated object does not authorize
it; predecessor custody is part of the chain.

From source custody to one semantic path

G01 seal the parent and candidate delta

Before executing semantics, G01 inventories:

- parent files 92

- frozen-source files 150

- candidate executable files 97

Every declared hash matches, no mismatch is reported and the parent
remains unchanged. The candidate delta is therefore distinguishable from
inherited source. Filename equality or an unsealed working tree would
not provide the same custody.

G02 traverse the only authority path

G02 checks that parser, checker, evaluator, type system and registry
exports resolve to one authority. A representative mixed program emits
the trace

- RESOLVE

- QP_ORDERED_PAIR

- SLC_ZERO_REGISTER

- SLC_PREPARE_REQUEST

Operator digits resolve through the registry. No legacy sidecar or
source-text dispatch is present. The trace matters because a matching
endpoint produced through a hidden path would not be the same semantic
result.

G03 isolate the formal profile

The same path is then invoked under three profiles. FORMAL_CANDIDATE is
accepted; normal and research profiles are rejected. Thus the formal
evidence does not borrow undeclared application or exploratory behavior.

Build and exhaust the formal state surface

G04 complete basis construction

G04 enumerates all 2^12=4,096 addresses. It verifies the exact
dimension, site-ID order and binary range, then rejects wrong arity,
out-of-range values, wrong register size and unknown/reordered site
identifiers.

This establishes a finite formal address space. It does not identify the
twelve sites with physical hardware or assign coupling magnitudes.

G05 exact canonical state invariant

The evaluator returns an exact sparse state. G05 checks:

- canonical sparse-key order;

- integer coefficients;

- the engine contract hash; and

- exact norm.

Reordering terms before hashing, converting coefficients to floats or
using an undeclared norm would change the contract even if a display
looked similar.

G06 freeze B request and X1 response

G06 installs B prepare/request and X1 response as differently typed
operators. For every declared basis column it verifies frozen hashes,
matrix shapes, prepare columns, response mapping and involution. B
contains a binary flip but is not identical to the flip; request and
response cannot be exchanged.

This is the formal premise used by D39. It defines executable operators
inside the candidate without assigning a physical B-to-X1 causal weld.

Complete reciprocal and decomposition schedules

G07 ordered pair lift

Twelve sites produce

12 ⋅ 11 = 132

ordered non-self placements. Each placement acts on all 4,096 basis
states in both directions:

132 ⋅ 4, 096 = 540, 672

forward cases and the same number in reverse. Therefore

2 ⋅ 132 ⋅ 4, 096 = 1, 081, 344

G07 closes all 1,081,344 classicalized schedule cases. Using unordered
pairs, one direction or a sample would be a different schedule.

G08 request sign and gauge diagnostics

The four frozen real request candidates REQ02, REQ08, REQ09 and REQ15
are each tested on all 132 placements:

4 ⋅ 132 = 528

All 528 placement cases pass, leaving four surviving gauges under the
installed positive-first-row convention. The diagnostic therefore
preserves ambiguity among the four real gauges instead of selecting one
from a desired downstream result.

G09 every declared native decomposition

The grammar generates five factor-shape families with exact assignment
counts:

- 1×12. Assignments: 1.

- 3×4. Assignments: 5,775.

- 4×3. Assignments: 15,400.

- 6×2. Assignments: 10,395.

- 12×1. Assignments: 1.

Their sum is

1 + 5, 775 + 15, 400 + 10, 395 + 1 = 31, 572

All 66 rooted-forest parent cases also pass, and the two induction modes
agree. The decomposition is native to the declared grammar rather than
an external factorization imported after execution.

G10 peel and restore the extra edge

G10 executes 204 exact controls:

- 1×12 : 132 cases

- 3×4 : 36 cases

- 4×3 : 24 cases

- 6×2 : 12 cases

- total: 204 cases

For each case, the declared extra edge is peeled, the exposed native
decomposition is evaluated, the same edge is restored, and the original
terminal state returns exactly. Restoring a different edge or accepting
graph isomorphism without exact state equality would weaken the control.

G11 frozen classical limit boundary

G11 checks all eight forward/reverse two-bit rows in the frozen
classical limit. Every row matches. The result is deliberately bounded:
a complete classical table is not evidence that complex-phase or general
quantum semantics have been installed.

State history and interface custody

G12 same endpoint different route

Chain and star programs produce the same exact coefficients and the same
terminal-state hash, but their history hashes differ. In symbols,

s_(chain) = s_(star),   hash (h_(chain)) ≠ hash (h_(star))

The component partition is not used to erase the route. This is the
formal reason an endpoint-returning computation can retain a nontrivial
receipt.

G13 deterministic API and CLI receipts

The same declared program is executed repeatedly through API and CLI.
Both interfaces are byte-deterministic, their receipts match each other,
the CLI exits successfully and no traceback occurs. Interface adapters
therefore do not introduce independent semantics or serialization
defaults.

G14 atomic rejection and tamper detection

G14 mutates coefficient, exponent, operator argument, predecessor hash
and receipt content. Every mutation is detected. Invalid operations
reject atomically, leaving no accepted prefix or partially changed
state.

This is a semantic property as well as a custody property: malformed
input does not acquire meaning merely because its first tokens were
valid.

G15 inherited and new replay on a clean wheel

The candidate suite contains

154 inherited + 29 new = 183 tests

All 183 pass. The clean wheel contains 37 members, and 13 CLI cases pass
with API/CLI parity, no sidecar or UI dependency, no repository-path
dependency and no traceback. This retest establishes that the candidate
package preserves the parent while carrying the new semantics outside
the source checkout.

G16 keep the wrong controls and open boundaries

All 23 declared wrong controls produce their diagnostic outcomes. The
release also retains five open domains:

1.  complex-phase semantics;

2.  measurement and publication semantics;

3.  physical connectivity;

4.  coupling magnitude and cost; and

5.  hardware realization.

A passing formal candidate remains scoped because these boundaries
remain in the artifact rather than being erased after the regression
run.

The v0 7 reflective tape semantics

The v0.7 candidate makes the instruction sequence itself typed data. A
typical execution can be understood as:

1.  create a zero-origin SLCTape12 and record its origin hash;

2.  append a typed B/request record whose predecessor is the current
    tape tip;

3.  append a typed X1/response record under the same custody law;

4.  seal the straight-line tape;

5.  validate the complete predecessor chain;

6.  dispatch each record through the inherited exact transition;

7.  return SLCState12 and a separate SLCTapeInspection.

If the direct program and tape program contain the same declared
operations, their current-state dossiers match. Their custody still
arrives through different source objects, so the tape is not replaced by
the endpoint.

This reflective layer prepares exact program composition and history
without making the language self-hosting. Recursive source generation, a
general parser inside the tape and a complete typed-IR-to-Dense-Exact
adapter remain open.

Source native N100 to F81 compiler boundary

G1 asks a different question from G01-G16: whether the exact language
and N100 source rows already carry a complete elementwise semantic map
to F81. The audit finds:

- 100/100 typed QP source keys

- 72/100 Shared72 aggregate connector-incidence rows

- 162 CR211 address rows

- 0 CR211 QP elementwise assignments

- 81 F81 semantic sites

- 10 F81 contact classes

- 0 N100 rows with a complete elementwise F81 map

The missing relation is

- QP candidate/template

- F81 semantic site or source-native F81 address class.

No outcome or residual selects a map. The contact lane is not executed
and the status remains PARTIAL_DOMAIN__ELEMENTWISE_F81_JOIN_OPEN.

The boundary shows why formal determinism and semantic completeness are
different. The language can parse, type, execute and receipt a program
while a particular source-to-target scientific join remains absent.

Controls deviations and promotion discipline

The full chain prevents several distinct deviations:

- parent source changes under the candidate. Detecting evidence: G01
  hashes and inventories; Preserved correction or boundary: parent
  remains sealed; candidate delta stays explicit.

- hidden semantic dispatch. Detecting evidence: G02 path and trace;
  Preserved correction or boundary: one
  parser/checker/evaluator/registry path.

- profile leakage. Detecting evidence: G03; Preserved correction or
  boundary: formal candidate accepts only its declared profile.

- malformed address gains meaning. Detecting evidence: G04; Preserved
  correction or boundary: range, arity, size and ID rejection.

- request and response collapse. Detecting evidence: G06; Preserved
  correction or boundary: frozen hashes, shapes and typed mappings.

- sampled schedule called complete. Detecting evidence: G07; Preserved
  correction or boundary: all 1,081,344 cases executed.

- preferred gauge selected after outcome. Detecting evidence: G08;
  Preserved correction or boundary: all four survivors preserved.

- endpoint equality erases history. Detecting evidence: G12; Preserved
  correction or boundary: equal state hash, unequal history hash.

- interface changes semantics. Detecting evidence: G13; Preserved
  correction or boundary: byte-identical API/CLI receipts.

- partial mutation survives rejection. Detecting evidence: G14;
  Preserved correction or boundary: atomic rollback and tamper
  detection.

- source-tree dependency hidden by tests. Detecting evidence: G15;
  Preserved correction or boundary: clean-wheel replay.

- open physical domains disappear after PASS. Detecting evidence: G16;
  Preserved correction or boundary: 23 wrong controls and five
  boundaries retained.

- aggregate incidence becomes elementwise mapping. Detecting evidence:
  G1; Preserved correction or boundary: zero assignments and unexecuted
  contact lane preserved.

Passing validation is not promotion. v0.6 stays a preserved
formal-simulator surface, v0.7 stays a reflective-tape candidate, and
SLC Recorded authority is resolved through the live SLCQ2-RZ pointer
rather than a historical filename.

Established result and current boundaries

The source-bound language result is:

1.  one declared parser/ resolver/ checker/ type/ registry/ evaluator
    path owns exact program semantics;

2.  twelve sites give a complete 4,096-address finite basis;

3.  B request and X1 response are frozen, differently typed operators;

4.  all 1,081,344 ordered-pair schedule cases and all 31,572 declared
    native decompositions close under their respective tests;

5.  current state, immutable source tape and accumulated history remain
    separate;

6.  API and CLI produce deterministic identical receipts;

7.  malformed operations reject atomically and declared tampering is
    detected;

8.  154 inherited plus 29 new tests pass in the clean candidate wheel;

9.  23 wrong controls pass while complex phase, measurement/publication,
    physical connectivity, coupling/cost and hardware realization stay
    open;

10. v0.7 supplies a later typed reflective tape but not general
    Dense-Exact integration; and

11. the N100-to-F81 elementwise semantic join remains open and
    unexecuted.

The focused G artifacts carry source status PASS or the explicit partial
G1 status. The registered atomic records assign no authorized project
result classification to D35, so this revision does not create one.
