Sources and evidential scope

This chapter is assembled from the following fixed source manuscripts.
Source-reported campaign outcomes retain their original scope; no new
campaign was executed for this edition.

- Sam language and exact program semantics.

Chapter III Exact State Spaces and Thermodynamics

Exact density-of-states methods count every assignment by energy and
retain the integer coefficients needed for later thermodynamic
calculations. This chapter develops enumeration, elimination and
polynomial routes to that complete object, then derives the partition
function and thermal moments from the frozen N96 density. Physical units
enter through a separately specified energy scale.

The historical constructions in this chapter retain the fixed 8
September 2026 SAMA source stage. Later results are identified by their
own source records.

Overview

An exact state-space computation answers a stronger question than an
optimizer: not merely which assignment has the lowest energy, but how
many assignments occupy every possible energy. For a declared binary
Hamiltonian this complete answer is the density of states, or DOS. Its
coefficients are integers, their sum is the complete formal
configuration count, and their arrangement retains the energy
organization created by the graph, couplings and fields.

The computation can be exact without visiting every assignment one by
one. Gray enumeration, variable elimination, component convolution,
modular polynomial arithmetic, orbit sectors and rank-aware readout are
different routes to the same requested object. Their right to be called
exact comes from reconstruction and equality obligations:
coefficientwise agreement, state- count closure, moments, symmetries,
port marginals, hashes and independent readers. Runtime is useful
evidence about an execution route; it is not the definition of
exactness.

This distinction has a second consequence. Exact mathematics and
executable custody are separate obligations. SLCX023 retained exact
exhaustive and NTT/variable-elimination authorities but failed its
frozen same-run custody gates. That failure remains part of the record.
SLCX023A corrected the custody mechanism in a separate successor,
reproduced the unchanged coefficient authorities and left the failed
parent immutable. The chain demonstrates the Courtroom method at its
best: retain what failed, identify the type of failure, change only the
defective layer, replay the original question and preserve both
receipts.

The chapter ends by deriving exact thermodynamic readers from the frozen
N96 DOS. Those readers are exact and dimensionless while the physical
energy scale remains symbolic. A polynomial can determine partition
functions and moments without thereby choosing joules, kelvin, a contact
orientation or a physical binding map.

Opening question and conceptual picture

The governing question is:

  How can a finite computational system account for every formal
  assignment, preserve the exact integer answer under different
  execution routes, and carry that answer into thermodynamics without
  importing an undeclared physical scale?

The full chain is

- declared typed grammar and Hamiltonian

- exact integer DOS g(E)

- independent route equality and state-count closure

- custody-qualified frozen artifact

- exact polynomial P(x)

- exact partition, moment and response readers

- symbolic-scale physical boundary

Every arrow changes the representation or the question, not the source
object. A component factorization may reduce work but may not reduce the
state space being counted. CRT may store residues but must reconstruct
the original integers. A thermodynamic reader may turn coefficients into
rational functions or numerical display values but may not retroactively
change the DOS. A physical unit conversion may occur only after a
source-authorized scale exists.

Typed definitions and domains

Source grammar assignment and Hamiltonian

Let V = {1, …, N} be the declared independent binary sites, let
E_(G) ⊆ V × V be the declared interaction edges, and let

σ = (σ₁, …, σ_(N)) ∈ {−1, +1}^(N)

For integer edge weights J_(ij) and integer local fields h_(i), the
Ising-type Hamiltonian used here is

H(σ) = ∑_((i, j) ∈ E_(G))J_(ij)σ_(i)σ_(j) + ∑_(i ∈ V)h_(i)σ_(i)

The types are load-bearing:

- N counts independent binary coordinates;

- E_(G) is topology, not an energy histogram;

- J_(ij) and h_(i) are declared Hamiltonian coefficients;

- a native grammar-row value does not become J_(ij) merely because it is
  numerical; and

- a constrained occurrence ledger is not automatically an independent
  N-spin state space.

This is why the N100 compiler keeps source-row selection separate from
Hamiltonian coefficients and why later M126/N144/L162 readouts retain
their rank and sidecar types.

Density of states

For each attainable integer energy e, define

g(e) = #{σ ∈ {−1, +1}^(N) : H(σ) = e}

The function g is an exact integer ledger. Summing over all energies
partitions the complete configuration space:

∑_(e)g(e) = ∑_(e)∑_(σ)1[H(σ) = e] = ∑_(σ)1 = 2^(N)

This derivation is elementary but decisive. Every assignment has exactly
one energy, so it contributes once. Frustration changes which energies
occur and how assignments are distributed among them. It does not change
the cardinality 2^(N) of the formal assignment domain.

A ground-state solver keeps only

e₀ = min_(σ)H(σ),   g(e₀)

whereas a DOS retains every pair ((e,g(e))). Interior bins, odd moments,
response sectors and finite-temperature behavior would be lost if the
output were reduced to an optimum.

Exactness execution and custody

Three types must not be collapsed:

- Mathematical exactness. Question answered: Are the requested
  coefficients the exact integers?; Required evidence: equality,
  closure, moments, symmetries, reconstruction.

- Execution policy. Question answered: How was the exact object
  evaluated?; Required evidence: route declaration, worker/batch
  receipts, performance data.

- Artifact custody. Question answered: Did the sealed code, inputs,
  receipts and outputs retain their declared identities?; Required
  evidence: hashes, domains, manifests, independent verification.

An exact tensor with a failed receipt is not a successful custody
packet. A fast route with no equality proof is not established as exact.
A hash-correct artifact does not become the right mathematical object
unless the declared calculation also closes.

Deriving the exact route family

Gray exhaustive authority

The direct definition suggests enumerating all 2^(N) assignments. A Gray
code orders them so adjacent assignments differ in one spin. If spin k
changes sign, only terms incident to k change, giving the incremental
update

ΔH_(k) = −2σ_(k)(h_(k) + ∑_(j : (k, j) ∈ E_(G))J_(kj)σ_(j))

Thus one full energy need not be recomputed from scratch at every state.
The route is still exponential in the number of visited assignments, but
for small N it is transparent and supplies an independent authority
against more structured routes. In SLCX023, GRAY_EXHAUSTIVE covers the
complete address space through N = 24.

Variable elimination

Write a local factor for every edge and field contribution. Eliminating
a spin means summing its two values while retaining an exact table over
the still-live neighboring boundary. If B_(k) is that boundary and z
marks energy, a schematic elimination step is

F_(k + 1)(σ_(B_(k)); z) = ∑_(σ_(k) = ±1)z^(ΔH_(k)(σ_(k), σ_(B_(k))))F_(k)(σ_(k), σ_(B_(k)); z)

The coefficients remain integers. Cost follows the largest induced
boundary width w, roughly through tables of size 2^(w), rather than
nominal arity alone. Different elimination orders therefore change work
while leaving the Hamiltonian and the final polynomial fixed.

SLCX023 deliberately used two distinct exact versions: a reference
NTT/VE route with a frozen min-degree order and a typed NTT/VE route
with an independently frozen cell-aware min-fill order. Their boundary
tables were retained so equality could be checked below the final scalar
output.

Exact component convolution

Suppose the declared graph splits into independent components
G₁, …, G_(m) after conditioning on an explicit boundary. If component a
has DOS polynomial

P_(a)(z) = ∑_(e)g_(a)(e)z^(e)

then additivity of energy gives

$$P_G(z)=\prod_{a=1}^mP_a(z)$$

and therefore

$$g_G(e)=\sum_{e_1+\cdots+e_m=e}
        \prod_{a=1}^m g_a(e_a)$$

This is not a sample or truncation. Every combination of component
assignments appears once in the convolution. At z = 1, closure is
inherited:

P_(G)(1) = ∏_(a)P_(a)(1) = ∏_(a)2^(N_(a)) = 2^(∑_(a)N_(a))

The N100 structure-first calculation in SAMA-D000037 uses this exact
identity. The same logic also explains why a reduced work count can
still return a complete 2^(N) census.

NTT lanes and CRT reconstruction

Large integer polynomial products can be evaluated modulo primes. For a
suitable prime p_(e)ll, an NTT transforms coefficients, multiplies
pointwise and applies the inverse transform, all in exact modular
arithmetic. One lane returns

g(e) mod  p_(ℓ)

With pairwise-coprime primes p₁, …, p_(L), CRT returns the unique
integer coefficient modulo

$$M=\prod_{\ell=1}^Lp_\ell$$

If the declared coefficient bound is less than M (or less than M/2 for a
signed reconstruction), the original integer is unique. The modular
lanes are therefore an exact representation of the coefficient only when
the prime product, reconstruction convention and bound all close. A
displayed floating-point polynomial is downstream and cannot replace
these obligations.

Orbit sectors and rank aware readout

When a dense graph is uniform under a declared permutation action,
assignments can be grouped by marked population. A sector with k marked
sites has multiplicity

$$\binom{N}{k}$$

provided the Hamiltonian is constant on that orbit or the remaining
typed coordinates are retained. This replaces assignment traversal with
an exact orbit census.

Likewise, an ambient readout with m slots may have only r < m
independent coordinates. The exact route evaluates the 2^(r) independent
domain and applies the declared readout map. It must not report the
ambient 2^(m) ceiling as independent state count. This distinction
becomes critical for N144 independent state and L162 constrained
occurrence views.

Route acceptance checklist

Two routes may differ internally and still compute the same object.
Their equivalence is established through a layered checklist:

1.  identical occupied-energy support;

2.  identical integer coefficient in every bin;

3.  ∑_(e)g(e) = 2^(N);

4.  matching exact raw moments ∑_(e)e^(k)g(e);

5.  declared parity, complement and role-permutation symmetries;

6.  matching retained-port marginals and conditional tensors;

7.  stable semantic hashes; and

8.  an independent reconstruction or reader.

A runtime comparison is interpreted only after these checks establish
same-object, same-output work.

Deviation chain SLCX023 to SLCX023A

The frozen question

SLCX023 asked whether a frozen exact-DOS development ladder could close
across six instances of sizes (12,24,36) using independent Gray,
reference NTT/VE and typed NTT/VE authorities while also preserving
execution and receipt custody. The equality standard was exact: same
occupied energies and same integer coefficient in every bin.

The campaign also contained eight deliberately wrong controls and
eighteen metamorphic checks. Wrong controls were not noise to be
deleted. They were expected-invalid transformations used to demonstrate
that the validation surface could distinguish the declared computation
from plausible but wrong alternatives.

Preserved failure

The run completed all six instances, detected all eight wrong controls
and passed all eighteen metamorphic checks. Yet its frozen verdict
remained

- SLCX023_FAIL__EXACT_DOS_MISMATCH_OR_CUSTODY

because the first coefficient, custody, invariant or execution failure
was a stop condition and same-run repair was forbidden. The historical
G02 and G11 custody gates failed. This typed outcome matters: the
available exact lane structure did not authorize rewriting a failed
executable receipt as a pass.

The parent preserved its precommit, executable and execution-ledger
hashes and recorded same_run_repair = false. The failed packet therefore
remained reconstructible.

Correction hypothesis

The appeal did not change the scientific question, Hamiltonians, route
definitions or coefficient authorities. It isolated two implementation
and custody mechanisms:

- execute the official retest from an isolated retained capsule whose
  runner, adapter and engine bytes are checked before and after work;
  and

- distinguish the external lane domain of legacy receipts from the
  boundary-table schema recorded by corrected receipts.

This is a narrow correction. It predicts that the unchanged scientific
outputs will reproduce while the custody gates now close.

Separate retest and cumulative record

SLCX023A executed as a successor rather than modifying SLCX023. It
reproduced all 16 of 16 lane-instance DOS hashes, passed 154 of 154
exactness checks, 64 of 64 moment checks and 18 of 18 metamorphic
controls. Twelve of those metamorphic controls reran transformed solvers
and six diagnosed cell repartitioning. All 432 corrected receipts
verified with their domain and boundary-table schema separated and with
signed links back to the legacy hashes.

The complete chain is therefore

- SLCX023 frozen design

- exact lanes execute

- custody G02/G11 do not close

- parent remains FAIL and immutable

- SLCX023A precommits two custody corrections

- unchanged 16/16 scientific hashes reproduce

- exactness, moment, metamorphic and 432 receipt checks pass

- corrected successor supplements the parent

No hidden N = 48 or N = 60 instance was opened. The appeal did not
install a SAM Language operator or create a physical Ising/gravity
claim.

Frozen N96 worked example

Complete configuration count

For N = 96,

2⁹⁶ = 79, 228, 162, 514, 264, 337, 593, 543, 950, 336

The frozen H14C computation completed four CRT primes and 1,024
root-batch checkpoints. It retained sixteen port rows, each summing to
2⁹², hence

16 ⋅ 2⁹² = 2⁴ ⋅ 2⁹² = 2⁹⁶

The scalar DOS occupies 348 energy bins from −346 through 348, with
ground-state degeneracy 12. State-count, support, parity and raw-moment
checks one through four passed. Both active 512 × 512 fiber factors
remained installed and all four scalar marginals equal the exact N96
result.

The execution completed in 40,307.938817 seconds with the frozen DOS
semantic hash
d74fb93facbebf18fa3cfe3ab2d33a0ec013f27b7ab6f326414bd5cdce4a11cb. The
later H14F profile reproduced that DOS hash under a different execution
policy. That comparison concerns work routing; it does not replace the
N96 Hamiltonian or create a second physical model.

Shifted exact polynomial

The energy spacing is two, so write

E_(k) = −346 + 2k,   0 ≤ k ≤ 347

and define

$$P_{96}(x)=\sum_{k=0}^{347}g(E_k)x^k$$

Then

P₉₆(1) = ∑_(k)g(E_(k)) = 2⁹⁶

with a₀ = 12 and a₃₄₇ = 7. The degree records the complete occupied
energy span after shifting; its coefficients remain the exact
degeneracies.

From DOS to exact thermodynamics

Partition function derivation

Let the dimensionless inverse scale be b = βϵ_(E), where ϵ_(E) remains
symbolic, and set

x = e^(−2b)

The partition function is

Z(b) = ∑_(k)g(E_(k))e^(−bE_(k)) = ∑_(k)g(E_(k))e^(−b(−346 + 2k))

Since e^(346b) = x⁻¹⁷³ and e^(−2bk) = x^(k),

Z₉₆(x) = x⁻¹⁷³P₉₆(x)

This factorization separates the exact coefficient ledger from the
chosen thermodynamic reader.

Euler derivatives and moments

Let

$$\Theta=x\frac{d}{dx}$$

Then

ΘP = ∑_(k)kg(E_(k))x^(k),   Θ²P = ∑_(k)k²g(E_(k))x^(k)

The mean shifted index is ⟨k⟩ = ΘP/P. Because E = −346 + 2k,

$$\langle E\rangle=-346+2\frac{\Theta P}{P}$$

and

$$\operatorname{Var}(E)
=4\left(\frac{\Theta^2P}{P}
-\left(\frac{\Theta P}{P}\right)^2\right)$$

Higher raw moments follow by expanding (−346 + 2k)^(m) and replacing
k^(j) with Θ^(j)P/P. At x = 1, T1 records

⟨E⟩ = 0,  ⟨E²⟩ = 1286,  ⟨E³⟩ = 534,  ⟨E⁴⟩ = 4, 952, 792

The nonzero third moment retains an exact asymmetry hidden by the zero
mean. The microcanonical mode is E = 0 with

g(0) = 1, 761, 659, 846, 545, 153, 896, 312, 195, 809

At the positive low-temperature end, the reader approaches E = −346,
zero variance and entropy log 12. At x = 1, it returns mean zero,
variance 1286 and entropy 96log 2.

Conditional response and the open closed boundary

T2 refines the scalar polynomial into a frozen 4 × 5 × 1181 integer
attachment. Every q marginal reconstructs T1. There are twenty
response-sector polynomials, ten active and ten inactive, and eight
distinct signatures including zero.

The closed trace retains three q signatures and merges q = 1 with q = 3.
The open retained port retains four routing signatures and all five
response values. Thus a closed equality cannot be copied backward as an
open-contact identity. At x = 1, q = 2 has exact energy-response
covariance 8, while q = 1 and q = 3 have zero linear covariance but
nonzero mutual information. The conditional structure survives beyond a
scalar covariance summary.

Wrong controls and type firewalls

The useful wrong controls answer specific failure questions:

- Deterministic count permutation. What it preserves: 2⁹⁶, coefficient
  multiset; What it changes or violates: moment vector and energy
  placement.

- Exact sign reflection. What it preserves: even moments; What it
  changes or violates: signs of odd moments.

- Rational uniform spectrum. What it preserves: support and total
  weight; What it changes or violates: integer state-count semantics and
  moment vector.

- C4-to-C2 quotient. What it preserves: some aggregate response
  structure; What it changes or violates: distinct q = 0 and q = 2
  lanes.

- Factorized response control. What it preserves: response totals and
  scalar marginals; What it changes or violates: actual q = 1, 2, 3
  joint structure.

- Open-port/closed-trace comparison. What it preserves: shared source
  object; What it changes or violates: routing availability versus final
  occupancy.

These controls demonstrate why closure alone is necessary but
insufficient. Many wrong objects can sum to 2^(N). Exactness belongs to
the coefficient ledger, its typed marginals and its independent
reconstruction together.

The physical firewall is equally explicit:

- exact integer DOS != physical energy calibration

- symbolic epsilon_E != measured joules

- dimensionless b != temperature in kelvin

- q_A source support != epsilon_E

- A(r) accumulation lift != Ising coupling J_ij

- open routing signature != closed-trace occupancy

BIPM constants may convert units after a physical scale is supplied;
they do not choose the scale. The current frozen SLCQ2-RZ selection
engine delegates byte-identical arithmetic/application work to the
frozen SLCV21R base. The historical N96, H14C/H14F, Exact Write and
thermodynamic artifacts retain their scoped results, but none replaces
SLCQ2-RZ as the source-recorded default SLC.

Established result boundary and forward handoff

The established mathematical object is the complete exact DOS, not only
a ground sector. Multiple exact routes may evaluate it, but all must
reconstruct the same integer coefficients and close the same typed
marginals. The frozen N96 release accounts for every one of 2⁹⁶
assignments, and its exact polynomial supports exact scalar and
conditional thermodynamic readers.

The preserved deviation result is equally important: SLCX023 remains the
failed custody parent; SLCX023A is the separate correction and retest.
The successor repairs executable custody without rewriting the earlier
run or changing the scientific tensor.

The forward handoff has three branches:

1.  SAMA-D000037 shows how source grammar becomes an exact work
    certificate while preserving the full state space and H14F fallback.

2.  SAMA-D000038 develops the exact write transaction and compiled
    group-law execution surface.

3.  A future direct evidence route must register SLCX023/SLCX023A, N96,
    T1-T6A and current SLCQ2-RZ artifacts before those sources can
    populate a qualified SAMA test crosswalk. Their absence is recorded,
    not filled with invented keys.

Sources and evidential scope

This chapter is assembled from the following fixed source manuscripts.
Source-reported campaign outcomes retain their original scope; no new
campaign was executed for this edition.

- Exact state spaces and slc computation.

Chapter IV Exact Joint Densities at N5000

The completed object

We computed the complete joint density of energy, magnetization and four
retained spins for six specified complete Ising graphs with 5,000 spins.
Every unordered pair interacts, so each graph contains 12,497,500
nonzero pair couplings. The six sources combine three packet
constructions with positive or negative uniform fill. All energies, all
magnetizations and all sixteen assignments of the retained spins are
included.

The primary result contains 1,530,259,992 nonzero records. Each record
gives an energy, a magnetization, a retained-spin assignment and an
exact integer count of configurations realizing those coordinates. A
record is a counted bin, not one individually visited configuration. The
counts together account for all 2⁵⁰⁰⁰ spin assignments for each source.
Holding the four retained spins fixed leaves 2⁴⁹⁹⁶ assignments in each
boundary sector.

The computation also retained a second encoding of the same joint
measure. Six sources times sixteen boundary assignments give 96 primary
tables; two exponent encodings give 192 logical output rows and 96
matched encoding pairs. All pairs agree on the canonical joint digest,
record count and source-derived moments. This is a completed finite
enumeration represented by exact generating functions. Its scientific
value lies in retaining the full correlation among energy, magnetization
and the boundary, so later questions can be answered from the computed
object.

  ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Source        Uniform fill Primary nonzero     Correction Lattice step d
  family                             records        bound B 
  ----------- -------------- --------------- -------------- --------------
  Packet                  +1          79,952              0              1

  Packet                  -1     461,138,212         26,660              2

  Signed                  +1      63,968,000          2,000              2
  packet                                                    

  Signed                  -1     461,223,474         24,660              2
  packet                                                    

  Signed                  +1      76,796,480          2,332              2
  packet                                                    
  chain                                                     

  Signed                  -1     467,053,874         24,994              2
  packet                                                    
  chain                                                     

  Total                        1,530,259,992                
  ------------------------------------------------------------------------

Each row includes all sixteen boundary assignments in one encoding. The
second encoding reproduces the same row totals. The correction bound
measures the total absolute departure from the uniform fill after that
fill has been removed; it does not count the complete graph’s physical
interactions. The small packet/+1 table is the exact uniform
ferromagnetic control. Its counts depend only on magnetization and the
fixed boundary. [N5-6, N5-7]

What the six sources contain

A packet is a finite graph with fifteen spins arranged in three complete
five-spin layers. Perfect matchings connect consecutive layers. One full
packet has forty parent edges: thirty within its layers and ten in the
two matchings. At N=5000, 333 full packets and one terminal five-spin
packet give 13,330 unbridged parent edges. The terminal packet preserves
the actual remainder of the construction.

The packet family assigns positive couplings to these parent edges. The
signed-packet family changes one edge in every five-spin layer to
negative, giving 1,000 negative parent edges and 12,330 positive edges.
The signed-packet chain adds 333 gateway bridges with alternating signs:
167 positive and 166 negative. For each family, all pairs absent from
the parent receive a uniform fill J₀ = +1 or J₀ = −1; parent edges
retain their signs. Thus the graph is physically complete even though
its departure from the uniform fill has a small-component structure.

The retained spins have the ordered labels 𝒫 = (0, 1, 60, 61). A
boundary mask b from 0 to 15 records their signs: bit a is one when spin
𝒫_(a) is positive. Retaining the ordered mask makes the output
conditional on an explicit physical boundary rather than only on its
number of positive spins. Exact duplicate output files can share storage
where the source proves equality; their boundary identities remain
separately recorded. [N5-2, N5-7]

Separating the collective interaction

A spin takes a value s_(i) ∈ {−1, +1}. Energy is the negative signed sum
of pair products, and magnetization is the sum of the spins. With no
local fields in these sources,

E(s) = −∑_(i < j)J_(ij)s_(i)s_(j),   M(s) = ∑_(i)s_(i)

The joint density g(E, M, b) counts the assignments having those three
coordinates. The uniform fill can be handled without materializing every
pair during counting. Squaring the total magnetization gives

M² = N + 2∑_(i < j)s_(i)s_(j)

Writing each pair coupling as J_(ij) = J₀ + ΔJ_(ij) therefore gives the
exact identity

$$E=E_c-\frac{J_0}{2}(M^2-N),\qquad E_c=-\sum_{i<j}\Delta J_{ij}s_i s_j$$

The dense term is a function of one collective coordinate, M. All
remaining configuration dependence is in the correction graph. A source
edge equal to the fill has zero correction. A source edge opposite to it
has correction +2 or -2. This identity changes the representation of the
exact Hamiltonian; it does not discard any pair coupling or approximate
its contribution.

For counting, let k = (M + N)/2 be the number of positive spins and
B = ∑_(i < j)|ΔJ_(ij)| the total absolute correction. The shifted
correction index t = (E_(c) + B)/2 is an integer. The original
coordinates are recovered by

$$M=2k-N,\qquad E=2t-B-\frac{J_0}{2}\bigl[(2k-N)^2-N\bigr]$$

The method must retain k jointly with t. Summing over magnetization too
early would destroy the association needed to restore the dense M² term.
This is why the result is a joint density rather than merely a
correction-energy histogram. [N5-2, N5-5]

Local tables and exact composition

Within a component, each permitted spin assignment contributes a
monomial x^(k)y^(t). Its coefficient counts how often those local
coordinates occur. Local tables retain gateway spins until their
incident bridges are accounted for. Multiplying compatible component
polynomials adds positive-spin counts and correction indices, while
multiplying their exact integer multiplicities. Summing the gateway
assignment joins the compatible cases.

The alternating bridge construction is particularly useful. A bridge
whose sign equals the fill has zero correction, so it disappears from
the correction graph. The remaining correction components contain at
most two complete packets. They are assembled from gateway tables rather
than by enumerating all 2³⁰ assignments of a two-packet component.
Repeated components share the same source-bound polynomial, and repeated
powers and grouped boundary roots remove repeated arithmetic.

This produces one polynomial G_(b)(x, y) for each retained boundary. Its
coefficient at (k, t) is the desired configuration count after the
coordinate decoding above. The correctness argument is constructive:
each local assignment appears once in its local table, disjoint
component products form every compatible global assignment, and gateway
summation closes each interface once. No global configuration is sampled
probabilistically. [N5-2, N5-3]

Two encodings and exact GPU arithmetic

The two-variable polynomial is packed into a one-variable exponent using
its known coordinate bounds. A common lattice step d divides the
correction indices. Put u = t/d and T = B/d. The two maps are

ϕ_(K)(k, u) = k(T + 1) + u,   ϕ_(T)(k, u) = u(N + 1) + k

Both are injective on the admitted coordinate rectangle. Division with
remainder restores the original coordinates, and output decoding rejects
coordinates outside the declared bounds. The two layouts make the same
joint counts take different coefficient positions and multiplication
layouts. Their agreement tests packing, reconstruction and decoding at
the scale of the completed object.

The GPU route multiplies integer polynomials using number-theoretic
transforms modulo suitable primes. It then reconstructs the nonnegative
integer coefficients by the Chinese remainder theorem. The product of
the selected moduli is strictly greater than the full coefficient bound.
Each reconstructed coefficient therefore has a unique value in the
admitted interval. The stored counts are exact integers; floating-point
transforms are not used for them.

N5000 required an enlarged reconstruction buffer. The successor backend
admits eighty 64-bit limbs, or 5,120 bits; the N5000 route uses
seventy-nine limbs. The serialized count field has 626 bytes, followed
with its exact energy and magnetization coordinates to give a 634-byte
raw record. The resource planner admits the transform arrays, host
output arrays, writer concurrency and disk reserve before the full run.
This mattered because exact coefficient growth and output volume remain
real costs even when the source factorization is compact. [N5-1, N5-2,
N5-4, N5-5]

Validation of the completed density

Each boundary table satisfies conditional magnetization closure. If r(b)
is the number of positive retained spins, the count at fixed total
positive-spin number k must be

$$\sum_E g(E,2k-N,b)=\binom{N-4}{k-r(b)}$$

The writer checks this identity for every admitted k. It is stronger
than checking only the total 2⁴⁹⁹⁶ because it constrains every
magnetization marginal. The energy moments are derived directly from the
source couplings and fixed boundary, independently of the polynomial
products. To see their content, write the fixed-boundary energy as a
constant c_(b), linear terms a_(v)s_(v) on free spins, and pair terms
−J_(uv)s_(u)s_(v). Under the uniform distribution of the free spins,
these distinct spin monomials are orthogonal. With A = 2^(N − 4), the
exact checks are

∑_(E, M)g = A,   ∑_(E, M)Eg = Ac_(b)

$$\sum_{E,M}E^2g=A\left(c_b^2+\sum_{v\notin\mathcal P}a_v^2+\sum_{\substack{u<v\\u,v\notin\mathcal P}}J_{uv}^2\right)$$

Here c_(b) = −∑_(u < v ∈ 𝒫)J_(uv)s_(u)s_(v) and
a_(v) = −∑_(p ∈ 𝒫)J_(vp)s_(p). These source identities constrain the
total mass, first energy moment and second energy moment without using
the product coefficients as their answer key.

The host output stage writes the exact coordinates and integer
multiplicities, checks their widths and support, computes canonical
bucket digests, flushes the files, and rereads each complete durable raw
file to confirm its hash. Production compares all 96 pairs of
independently packed encodings on canonical digest, record count and
moments. The preparatory qualification records 75 checks including
CPU-equal coefficients at 3,000-, 4,000- and 5,000-bit widths and small
source/encoding cases. The largest packet/-1 boundary pilot passed in
both encodings before admission of full production.

A cheap portable replay supplies an additional concrete comparison.
Recomputing the packet/+1 control on the CPU reproduced all 32
encoding/boundary rows in 37.768777 seconds, matching the canonical and
raw record hashes, moments and counts. That replay verifies the
analytically simple control through the public route. It does not
replace the full six-source production, whose other cases retain their
own exact checks and paired-encoding records. [N5-1, N5-3, N5-5, N5-8]

The computation and its preserved data

Full production completed 192 logical rows and all 96 encoding pairs in
2,824.215 seconds, about 47 minutes. The recorded machine was an RTX
PRO6000 Blackwell system with 97,887 MiB of device memory, about 27.2
CPU equivalents, 187,999,997,952 bytes of host RAM and a 4 TB container
disk. The largest pilot recorded a process allocation of 83,785 MiB of
GPU memory. These are the conditions of one completed execution, not a
fitted general runtime law.

Before production, the source-bound compiler encountered the generic
4,096-variable admission ceiling. The completed successor normalized and
authenticated the explicit frozen N5000 parent graphs while retaining
the generic solver’s existing admission policy. The failed preparation
remains in provenance. Its relevance is the exact source-binding
correction that enabled the finished run; it is not the result of the
N5000 campaign.

Lossless zstd compression and byte deduplication reduced the completed
files to 89 unique compressed contents. The archive is 609,671,270,157
bytes, compared with 1,045,145,366,546 distinct raw bytes. Compression
took 1,260.611 seconds. Each compressed shard was decompressed and
checked against its production raw hash. The manifest preserves every
logical boundary alias, source, plan, record count, canonical digest and
raw hash.

The frozen source-input restoration is a separate completed achievement
in the chain. When an earlier backup path was absent, all sixteen
original input files were reconstructed byte-identically from the
retained source grammar and original construction receipts. The restored
parent JSON, coupling bitstreams and manifests match their original
SHA256 identities, including their original serialization details. No
N5000 bulk output was lost. The final restoration uses the native GEN3
checkpoint route, and earlier restoration attempts remain preserved.

Later custody records complete the data chain. Batches 001 and 002 have
Drive size and SHA256 verification. The T500 download records verify
batches 003 through 006, with batches 005 and 006 adding 32 shards and
203,896,054,295 bytes in the final stage. All 89 shards and
609,671,270,157 compressed bytes have recorded external custody. The
completed final-download record supersedes the early all-batches
download hold in the original archive README. These statements cite the
retained verification receipts; they do not claim a fresh reread of the
full archive during this manuscript revision. [N5-1, N5-6, N5-7, N5-9,
N5-10, N5-11, N5-12, N5-13, N5-15]

What the result makes available

Once g(E, M, b) is available, a uniform field can be applied by
replacing E with E − hM. The partition function for a fixed retained
boundary is then the exact finite sum

Z_(b)(β, h) = ∑_(E, M)g(E, M, b)exp  [ − β(E − hM)]

Boundary fields, partial boundary closure, fixed-magnetization questions
and added uniform pair couplings likewise operate on retained
coordinates. These operations reuse a completed count rather than
enumerate the spins again. The multiplicities remain exact even when an
exponential or thermodynamic readout is evaluated numerically with its
own precision contract.

The scale increase from N2000 to N5000 is substantive because the larger
output retains the same detailed finite observables across 12,497,500
physical pair interactions. The reason it is tractable is also explicit:
the collective magnetization removes the uniform complete interaction
from the hard local bookkeeping, while repeated bounded correction
components control the remaining factorization. The result establishes
this exact computation for the six specified sources. It is not a
universal exact solver for arbitrary dense frustrated graphs, and the
single production timing does not establish an asymptotic runtime
exponent. [N5-2, N5-7, N5-16]

N5000 thermal ordering and retained boundary memory

A separately defined thermal family

Later N5000 calculations reuse the exact correction polynomials to
determine ordering, fluctuations and boundary response. The thermal
question requires an explicit normalization. In the archived unit-fill
Hamiltonian, the collective term and sparse correction both have their
original integer strengths. Dividing that whole Hamiltonian by N scales
both terms. The later fixed-sparse-strength family instead uses

$$H_{N,\kappa}=-\frac{M^2-N}{2N}+\kappa C_N,\qquad \kappa\ge0,\qquad N=20+30m$$

Here C_(N) = 2∑_(negative edges)s_(i)s_(j) for the signed positive-fill
sources; the packet control has C_(N) = 0. The dense pair coefficient is
1/N, while the sparse correction has fixed strength κ. The choice
κ = 1/N recovers whole scaling of the original positive-fill source.
Holding κ fixed defines the thermal extension used in the transition
theorem. This source distinction makes the later physical results
interpretable without changing the original integer counts. [N5-14]

Let F(y) be the partition function of one repeated correction component
in dimensionless auxiliary field y, including its actual sparse
Boltzmann factor. The signed-packet motif has n = 15 spins and the chain
motif has n = 30; a fixed boundary root and a five-spin tail complete
each finite source. Gaussian decoupling of the collective term gives the
bulk variational pressure

$$\sup_{y\in\mathbb R}\Phi_{\beta,\kappa}(y),\qquad
\Phi_{\beta,\kappa}(y)=-\frac{y^2}{2\beta}+\frac1n\log\frac{F(y)}{F(0)}$$

The inverse temperature β is in the declared model units. Define the
repeated-component magnetization per spin and zero-field susceptibility
by

$$m_0(y)=\frac1n\partial_y\log F(y),\qquad \chi=m_0'(0)$$

A stationary point satisfies y = βm₀(y), and an extremal macroscopic
state has magnetization m_(*) = y_(*)/β. The finite root and tail
contribute only O(1) to log Z on each compact field interval. They
therefore leave the limiting bulk maximization unchanged while remaining
present in the finite conditional weights and boundary-memory
observables. The bound F(y)/F(0) ≤ e^(n|y|) controls the distant
Gaussian tails. [N5-14]

The first global ordering transition

The exact source factors reduce the ordering question to rational
functions of two bounded variables. Put a = tanh (2βκ) and v = tanh y,
and define

P(a, v) = 1 + (−a³ + 2a² − 3a)v² + a²v⁴

Up to positive field-independent constants, the repeated factors are

F_(packet)(y) ∝ cosh¹⁵y

F_(signed)(y) ∝ cosh¹⁵y(1 − av²)³

F_(chain)(y) ∝ cosh³⁰y(1 − av²)⁴P(a, v)

The chain factor has a four-spin path and four negative pairs in its
correction core, together with free correction spins. It is not a
thirty-spin path. The source-factorization identities were checked
against the frozen generating polynomials. The resulting
susceptibilities are

$$\chi_{\mathrm{packet}}=1,\qquad
\chi_{\mathrm{signed}}=1-\frac{2a}{5},\qquad
\chi_{\mathrm{chain}}=\frac{30-14a+4a^2-2a^3}{30}$$

A local threshold alone would leave open a first-order transition caused
by a distant maximum before that threshold. The completed derivation
closes that gap by proving the global response inequality m₀(y) < χy for
every y > 0. Its algebraic certificate uses the polynomial

$$S_4(x)=1+\frac{x}{3}+\frac{x^2}{5}+\frac{x^3}{7}+\frac{x^4}{9}$$

For each signed family, the exact rational response satisfies

$$\chi S_4(v^2)-\frac{m_0(y)}{v}
=\frac{v^2Q_f(a,v^2)}{D_f(a,v)}$$

where the denominators are

D_(signed) = 1575(1 − av²),   D_(chain) = 4725(1 − av²)P(a, v)

Both are positive on 0 ≤ a ≤ 1 and 0 ≤ v < 1. The numerators have
bidegrees (2, 4) and (7, 6) in (a, x), where x = v². Expanding them in
the Bernstein basis on the unit square gives 15 and 56 nonnegative
rational coefficients, respectively. All 71 coefficients and the exact
reconstruction of the power-basis polynomials are retained. Because each
Bernstein basis function is nonnegative on that square, this proves
Q_(f) ≥ 0 throughout the parameter domain. It is an exact finite
certificate of a continuum statement. [N5-14]

The strictly positive remainder in the series for the inverse hyperbolic
tangent gives

$$\frac{\operatorname{atanh}v}{v}>S_4(v^2),\qquad 0<v<1$$

Together with χ ≥ 3/5, the certificate therefore proves m₀(y) < χy.
Consequently, when βχ ≤ 1, the derivative Φ^(′)(y) = m₀(y) − y/β is
strictly negative for y > 0. Evenness makes zero the unique global
maximizer, including at equality. When βχ > 1, the origin has positive
curvature, so a nonzero maximizing pair has larger pressure. Thus the
unique solution of

β_(c)χ(β_(c), κ) = 1

is the first global ordering transition for every fixed finite κ ≥ 0 in
these specified families. Maximizers satisfy |y_(*)| ≤ β; as β decreases
to β_(c), compactness and uniqueness of the critical maximizer force
y_(*) → 0. The negative fourth cumulant supplies a nondegenerate
square-root onset. The stronger route through global concavity of m₀ is
false at exact retained counterexamples; it is not needed for this
successful first-onset proof. The conclusion concerns the first
transition, with no claim to classify every remote stationary point at
larger inverse temperature. [N5-14]

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------
         Fixed correction     Signed-packet β_(c)             Chain β_(c)
               strength κ                         
  ----------------------- ----------------------- -----------------------
                        0          1.000000000000          1.000000000000

                      1/8          1.122873781777          1.136842176652

                      1/4          1.295422047875          1.310642690959

                      1/2          1.581042157548          1.582234699504

                        1          1.663814710634          1.663815941534

                        2          1.666663067473          1.666663067475
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------

The exact susceptibility difference is
χ_(chain) − χ_(signed) = −a(1 − a)²/15. Hence the chain threshold is
strictly higher for finite positive κ, and both approach 5/3 as κ → ∞.
The table reports rounded values from separately retained directed
critical brackets. [N5-14]

Critical fluctuations and a nonzero boundary response

At the exact critical temperature, let c₄ < 0 be the fourth cumulant per
spin of the repeated component. The pressure near zero has a stabilizing
quartic term Φ(y) = −by⁴ + O(y⁶) with b = −c₄/24 > 0. The previously
established global uniqueness controls the complement of a neighborhood
of zero, and the Gaussian bound controls the distant tail. Rescaling
u = N^(1/4)y then gives

$$\sqrt N\,\left\langle\left(\frac MN\right)^2\right\rangle
\longrightarrow
\frac{\sqrt{24/(-c_4)}}{\beta_c^2}
\frac{\Gamma(3/4)}{\Gamma(1/4)}$$

For the chain at κ = 1, the amplitude is 0.546951358570. At the finite
map’s twelve-decimal rational critical centers, the computed N5000 value
of ⟨(M/N)²⟩ is 0.0077033393, compared with 0.0038595576 at N20000. The
exact-critical asymptotic amplitude and the rounded finite input
temperatures have distinct definitions and retained numerical receipts.
Finite systems remain smooth; their curves are compared with the
limiting transition rather than described as finite singularities.
[N5-14]

The fixed retained root supplies a second physical consequence. Let
R_(b)(y) be its exact boundary factor including its correction Boltzmann
weights. Below and at onset, concentration of the common integral at
zero gives

$$\frac{Z_b}{Z_0}\longrightarrow\frac{R_b(0)}{R_0(0)}$$

The common finite tail and normalization cancel. Applying the declared
boundary protocol to these strictly positive limiting weights gives the
memory limit directly, without extrapolating numerical data to enormous
N. At exact chain criticality with κ = 1, the preparation,
between-reading and within-interval differences are respectively

−0.430454602216,   0.000189921648477,   0.000344266087525

These numbers refer to an explicit observation protocol. A heat-bath
generator changes one retained bit at a time with rate
Q_(j, b) = w_(j)/(w_(b) + w_(j)) for weights w_(b) = Z_(b)/Z₀, and its
columns sum to zero. Readings retain the least-significant bit,
postselect even boundary states, and use intervals δ = 1/3. The
preparation difference compares the state-zero start with conditional
equilibration inside its initial coarse class. The between-reading
difference measures the effect of conditional resets at intermediate
readings. The within-interval difference compares those resets with
evolution under the coarse generator. Conditional equilibration inside
one class is not a globally stationary mixture. The nonzero limiting
differences quantify retained boundary response under this declared
clock; physical seconds and physical transition rates are separate
source assignments. [N5-14]

The finite map evaluates 158 complete directed cases, including N5000
and N20000, correction strengths and temperature offsets, packet
controls and four N200 cases. Complete 128-bit Arb integrals retain
explicit infinite tails; 84 direct comparisons expand the N200 exact
spectrum and sum Gibbs weights at 192 bits. The method therefore checks
the new thermal normalization and finite readouts independently of the
Gaussian integration. [N5-14]

Exact occupied windows and measured probability

A later counting calculation retained a selected thermally occupied
N5000 window: 69,904 final records, including 2,056 exact zeros. The
window covers positive-spin numbers 2372 ≤ k ≤ 2628,
aligned-negative-edge indices 0 ≤ t_(a) ≤ 16, and all sixteen boundary
assignments. Here t_(a) = L − d_(neg) counts aligned negative edges,
with L their total number and d_(neg) the disagreeing-edge count. This
t_(a) is defined for the later window calculation and is not the general
shifted correction coordinate used earlier in the chapter. Its original
unit-fill labels satisfy

$$M=2k-N,\qquad
E=4t_{\mathrm{a}}-2L-\frac{M^2-N}{2}$$

The shortcut is mathematical. Reversing the chain’s defect variable
produces a repeated factor with monomial constant coefficient f̂₀ = 32X⁶.
If q_(n) is the coefficient of the reversed defect variable at degree n
in its mth power, logarithmic differentiation gives

$$q_0=(32X^6)^m,\qquad
n\widehat f_0q_n=\sum_{i=1}^{7}\bigl((m+1)i-n\bigr)\widehat f_iq_{n-i}$$

Every polynomial division is exact and checked for zero remainder. Only
sixteen recurrence steps are needed for the N5000 window. The remaining
pair, free spins and boundary root are then contracted exactly. A
separate GPU calculation uses the original defect encoding and modular
transforms; all 17,800 intermediate coefficients agree across the N5000
and N20000 windows before final root contraction. Global reversal agrees
on all 275,104 final records. Exact zeros are preserved as
source-imposed forbidden coordinates. [N5-14]

The N5000 CPU coefficient stage took 0.268194 seconds; the corresponding
GPU route took 0.651927 seconds, with another 4.259603 seconds for
common root contraction, output and mass evaluation. This short
aligned-index regime favors the direct CPU recurrence. It complements
the GPU’s usefulness for the original much larger full-joint coefficient
problem; the measured requests and timing boundaries are explicit.
[N5-14]

For a window W, probability is evaluated against the complete partition
function of the thermal family:

$$\Pr(W)=\frac1Z\sum_{(k,t_{\mathrm a},b)\in W}g(k,t_{\mathrm a},b)
\exp\!\left[\frac{\beta((2k-N)^2-N)}{2N}
-\beta\kappa(4t_{\mathrm a}-2L)\right]$$

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------
        N5000 temperature          Captured Gibbs           Omitted Gibbs
         offset β − β_(c)             probability             probability
  ----------------------- ----------------------- -----------------------
                    −0.03              53.607483%              46.392517%

                        0              37.267444%              62.732556%

                    +0.03              17.264479%              82.735521%
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------

The exact counts capture substantial probability at and below the
transition. Above onset, the ordered magnetization peaks move away from
the window centered at zero, reducing its coverage. Both captured and
omitted probabilities are retained as directed intervals. No uncomputed
count is silently replaced by zero, and the selected window is not
treated as the whole density. The complete N5000 joint construction, the
global first-onset proof and the exact occupied window are three
substantive completed results with separate output definitions and
derivation chains. [N5-14, N5-16]

Chapter V N2000 Ground States and Low Energy Physics

Six specified complete Ising graphs with 2,000 spins were solved exactly
while retaining energy, magnetization, and four ordered boundary spins.
The primary output contains 244,104,392 nonzero integer records. The
retained joint measure accounts for all 2²⁰⁰⁰ labelled spin
configurations of each graph and supports later field and
boundary-response questions without repeating the microscopic
enumeration. The subsequent physical readouts determine exact ground
energies and degeneracies, analytic residual-entropy limits along a
declared size sequence, excitation gaps, field thresholds, and
controlled low-temperature heat capacities. These are substantive
results about how the specified interactions organize the states. [A1,
pp 1-10]

The clearest physical comparison is between negative-fill signed packets
and the same packets joined by alternating bridges. The bridges reduce
the N2000 ground-state multiplicity by a factor of approximately
2.07536 × 10²⁰, lower the ground energy from −10, 840 to −10, 974, and
reduce the first excitation gap from 10 to 4. On the commensurate
sequence N = 60r, their pairing of correction components halves the
limiting ground-state entropy per spin from ln 2/15 to ln 2/30. The
lower ground multiplicity and softer first excitation coexist: they
describe different aspects of the exact spectrum. [A1, pp 5-8]

The computed object and the six sources

An Ising spin is a binary variable taking the values −1 and +1. A spin
configuration specifies the value at every labelled vertex. The
Hamiltonian assigns an energy to each configuration using the pair
couplings J_(ij) and site fields h_(i). In this chapter the sign
convention is

E(s) = −∑_(i < j)J_(ij)s_(i)s_(j) − ∑_(i)h_(i)s_(i),   M(s) = ∑_(i)s_(i)

All six archived N2000 systems have zero fields and pair couplings
J_(ij) ∈ {−1, +1}. Every physical pair is coupled, giving
$\binom{2000}{2}=1,999,000$ nonzero interactions. Energies are
dimensionless, and the complete interaction has not been divided by N.
The sources therefore describe finite, unnormalized complete graphs.
[A1, p 2, Eqs 1-2]

The retained boundary is the ordered vertex list P = (0, 1, 60, 61). A
boundary mask b ∈ {0, …, 15} records those four spin signs, with a set
bit denoting +1. The joint density is the integer count

g(E, M, b) = #{s : E(s) = E, M(s) = M, s|_(P) = b}

Each nonzero record is one supported energy-magnetization-boundary bin
and its multiplicity. It is not one separately visited global
configuration. Each fixed boundary has total count 2¹⁹⁹⁶; the 16
boundary sectors sum to 2²⁰⁰⁰. Keeping the boundary ordered preserves
which retained vertex receives a later perturbation. [A1, p 2, Eqs 3-4]

The construction starts with packets. A full packet contains 15 vertices
arranged in three complete five-vertex layers. Perfect matchings join
successive layers, giving $3\binom{5}{2}+2(5)=40$ parent edges. At N2000
there are 133 full packets and one terminal five-vertex packet, with
5,330 unbridged parent edges in total. The inherited vertex labels are
retained explicitly. In the signed family, one edge in every complete
layer is negative: there are 400 negative and 4,930 positive parent
edges. The chain adds 133 gateway bridges, alternating in sign from +1,
so 67 bridges are positive and 66 negative. [A1, p 2]

For each of these three parents, all absent pairs receive a uniform fill
J₀ = +1 or J₀ = −1, while existing parent edges retain their signs. This
gives six sources. Packet with positive fill is the uniform
ferromagnetic complete graph and supplies an analytic control. The other
five sources contain frustrated cycles. They share the same spin count
and complete physical connectivity, but their correction structures and
spectra differ. [A1, pp 2, 4]

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  Source            Negative        Nonzero     Correction        Largest
              physical pairs    corrections        bound B    implemented
                                                               correction
                                                                component
  ----------- -------------- -------------- -------------- --------------
  Packet                   0              0              0             15
  positive

  Packet           1,993,670          5,330         10,660             15
  negative

  Signed                 400            400            800             15
  packet

  Signed           1,994,070          4,930          9,860             15
  packet                                                   
  negative

  Signed                 466            466            932             30
  packet                                                   
  chain                                                    
  positive

  Signed           1,994,003          4,997          9,994             30
  packet                                                   
  chain                                                    
  negative                                                 
  fill                                                     
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------

The component sizes in the final column describe the implemented packet
grouping. The original source retains all 1,999,000 interactions in
every case. [A1, p 4, Table I]

Why a complete graph admits an exact construction

The dense fill can be carried by a collective observable rather than
represented as a large local factor. Magnetization is sufficient for
that part because binary spins obey

M² = N + 2∑_(i < j)s_(i)s_(j)

Write the source as its uniform fill plus the exact correction
ΔJ_(ij) = J_(ij) − J₀. The complete energy then becomes

$$E=E_c-\frac{J_0}{2}(M^2-N),\qquad E_c=-\sum_{i<j}\Delta J_{ij}s_i s_j$$

This is an identity of the full source. Retaining M preserves the
association needed to restore the dense contribution after contracting
the corrections. A correction-energy marginal alone would lose that
association. [A1, p 2, Eqs 5-7]

The correction graph has bounded packet and gateway interfaces. Local
integer generating functions count packet assignments according to
positive-spin count, correction energy, and the signs still needed by a
boundary or a gateway. A full packet has at most 2¹⁵ = 32, 768 local
assignments. The original preparation produced 22 local tables on two
A100 devices and compared them with independently arranged CPU Gray-code
enumeration. The public preparation route uses the CPU enumeration. [A1,
pp 2-3]

For a bridge correction δ joining gateway spins a and c, define its
nonnegative shifted contribution

$$\tau_\delta(a,c)=\frac{|\delta|-\delta ac}{2}$$

If L_(r + 1, c)(x, y) is the next packet table with gateway sign c, a
forward contraction has the form

F_(r + 1)(b, c) = ∑_(a = ±1)F_(r)(b, a)L_(r + 1, c)(x, y)y^(τ_(δ_(r))(a, c))

The gateway sign is summed only after its interactions have been
included. The four externally retained spins remain fixed throughout.
When a bridge sign equals the fill, its correction vanishes; in the
alternating chain this separates the correction graph into components of
at most two full packets. These components are joined from gateway
tables, without enumerating all 2³⁰ assignments of a paired component.
Repeated scalar components become polynomial powers, and a
length-balanced product tree combines them with the retained component.
[A1, p 3, Eqs 10-12]

For storage and polynomial composition, let B = ∑_(i < j)|ΔJ_(ij)|, let
k = (M + N)/2, and shift correction energy to the integer coordinate
t = (E_(c) + B)/2. A common lattice step d permits u = t/d and T = B/d.
The two packed exponents are

ϕ_(K)(k, u) = k(T + 1) + u,   ϕ_(T)(k, u) = u(N + 1) + k

The global coordinate bounds make both maps injective. Quotient and
remainder recover the coordinates, with out-of-range values rejected.
FLINT polynomial arithmetic, accessed through python-flint, preserves
arbitrary-precision integer coefficients throughout. [A1, pp 2-3, Eqs
8-14]

The counting argument follows the construction. A local table counts
every permitted local assignment once. Multiplication combines disjoint
assignments and adds their observables. Summing gateway signs includes
every compatible bridge state once. Restoring the collective term gives
the complete physical energy. The resulting coefficient is therefore the
multiplicity of one unique (E, M, b). The two exponent orders provide a
strong implementation cross-check; they share local source tables and an
arithmetic library, so their agreement is not described as agreement
between two unrelated mathematical solvers. [A1, p 3]

The completed joint output and its validation

The six primary outputs have sharply different support sizes even though
they describe the same number of spins and physical pairs. [A1, p 5,
Table II]

  Source                                Primary nonzero records    Raw GB   Losslessly compressed GB
  ----------------------------------- ------------------------- --------- --------------------------
  Packet positive fill                                   31,952    0.0083                     0.0042
  Packet negative fill                               73,522,212   19.0423                    11.1916
  Signed packet positive fill                        10,227,200    2.6489                     1.7201
  Signed packet negative fill                        73,556,274   19.0511                    11.1050
  Signed packet chain positive fill                  12,265,280    3.1767                     2.1078
  Signed packet chain negative fill                  74,501,474   19.2959                    11.2691
  Total                                             244,104,392   63.2231                    37.3979

Here GB means 10⁹ bytes. The exact primary byte totals are
63,223,097,516 raw and 37,397,887,721 compressed. Six cases, 16
boundaries, and two encodings give 192 emitted boundary rows
representing 96 distinct case-boundary measures. A boundary row is a
whole conditional measure; a record is one supported bin within it. Only
one encoding is needed to distribute the measure, while both encodings’
scientific receipts are retained. [A1, pp 4-5]

The production took 816.624947 seconds, including row emission and
verified durable output. This timing excludes earlier preparation and
pilot work and later compression and transfer. Fourteen workers used two
FLINT threads each. The recorded CPU time was 16,159.967 seconds,
equivalent to 19.79 average CPU cores during the measured interval,
under a quota of 27.2. The configured memory limit was 465.66 GiB, and
the largest recorded individual worker RSS was 18.69 GiB. Neither is a
measured simultaneous fleet peak. RAM-staged rows were copied, fsynced,
checksum-read back, and renamed before staging buffers were removed.
[A1, pp 4-5]

Correctness was checked at several levels. For a boundary containing
r(b) positive spins, counting free-spin assignments gives the exact
conditional magnetization identity

$$\sum_E g(E,2k-N,b)=\binom{N-4}{k-r(b)}$$

This was checked at every allowed magnetization in every emitted
boundary row. The first two energy moments were obtained separately from
the couplings and fixed boundary signs. Summing all boundaries yields
total count 2²⁰⁰⁰, first moment zero, and second moment
1, 999, 000 2²⁰⁰⁰. Complete canonical row digests compare the full
measures under the two encodings. N12 qualification uses independent
joint variable elimination, N30 compares an uncompressed component
route, CPU and GPU local tables agree, and a deliberately truncated
binary is rejected. Source hashes, binary hashes, moment identities, and
coordinate comparisons answer different questions and are retained
together. [A1, pp 3-4, Eqs 16-19]

The positive-fill packet control is also available analytically. Its
only supported energy at a given M is −(M² − N)/2, and its conditional
multiplicity is the corresponding binomial coefficient. Each boundary
has 1,997 records, giving 31,952 across all boundaries. A fresh
public-runtime computation reproduced all 32 binary hashes for this
control, including both encodings, in approximately 3.07 seconds after
preparation. This demonstrates recovery of the control’s algebra and
serialization; the five nonuniform outputs have much larger support.
[A1, p 4, Eqs 20-21]

Exact ground states from the same source definitions

The paper’s physical expansion reconstructed the published source motifs
and computed their minimum energy and degeneracy at every magnetization.
It used a smaller output than the original full joint measure and summed
the retained boundaries. The two outputs have separate computational
purposes and separate provenance. [A1, pp 5, 10]

A minimum/count state contains an energy e and an exact multiplicity c.
Combining alternatives keeps the lower energy and adds multiplicities
only when the energies tie. Combining independent assignments adds
energies and multiplies counts. Magnetization convolution combines
packet states while retaining gateway signs until the bridges are
processed. At fixed M, the collective fill energy is constant, so
minimizing the correction energy and restoring the fill is exact. A
final minimum over M returns the global ground energy and sums all tied
ground counts. [A1, p 5, Eqs 22-23]

Forward and reversed composition agreed at all 2,001 magnetization
sectors for all six N2000 cases. Separate direct complete-pair sums
verified every sector minimum and count at N5, N12, and N18. The N2000
ground levels also agree with the separately implemented
truncated-spectrum calculation below. These readouts were computed from
the source definitions; the revision did not claim that the 63 GB
historical joint shards were reread for them. [A1, pp 5, 7-8]

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Source       Ground energy       Ground M                 Exact ground multiplicity Ω₀        ln Ω₀/N
