Chapter IX Directional History and Event Reels

Directional computation acts on all 262,144 Theta18 addresses through 18
maps and retains the reciprocal history needed to reconstruct both
directions. An event with incidence B_(e) = I − U_(e) contributes the
positive Gram B_(e)^(*)B_(e) to an append-only reel. The shared-anchor
construction preserves the common stationary state and the complete
nonconstant event information.

The historical constructions in this chapter retain the fixed 8
September 2026 SAMA source stage. Later results are identified by their
own source records.

Overview

Directional computation keeps two things at once: the state reached by a
write and the ordered reciprocal history that produced it. The generic
core acts on all 262,144 Theta18 addresses through 18 directional maps,
completes the W8/X1/W9 lifecycle and retains 72 exact receipts before
any RH, Starbreaker or Mersenne coefficients are applied.

Two reciprocal 81-coordinate histories have an ambient 162-dimensional
description. Their symmetric and antisymmetric decomposition contains 80
nonconstant symmetric coordinates, 81 antisymmetric coordinates and one
common constant direction. The nonconstant quotient therefore has rank

161 = 80 + 81

while the full ambient accounting is

162 = 80 + 81 + 1

The retained common anchor reconstructs both sides exactly. Endpoint
return does not erase ordered history.

The event-reel construction lifts this local receipt into an append-only
global object. For an interaction translation U_(e), the event incidence
is B_(e) = I − U_(e) and the unique event Gram is
Θ_(e) = B_(e)^(*)B_(e). Reciprocal source/target orientations are two
references to one intersection event, not two Theta objects. Appending
an event adds its positive weighted Gram. A shared-reference N144 AFC
cell realizes the W9 pair and the one-Theta lane with equal energy, and
an event family with |ℰ| events has 1 + 143|ℰ| nodes, rank 143|ℰ| and
one common scalar kernel.

The installed derivation is cumulative because several stronger
shortcuts failed. Generic positive Stieltjes pencils do not force reel
contraction; a scalar RII discriminator is insufficient; the local N144
frame alone does not dominate the completed debit; an isolated
recurrence lane changes sign; a whole-matrix AFC order fails; individual
parity factors fail even when their product closes; and a spherical
diagonal Gram order has negative directions. Each failure narrows the
selected statement to the exact event Gram, shared-anchor intertwiner
and endpoint scalar actually supported by the source.

Opening question and conceptual picture

The governing question is:

  How can every reciprocal write append one exact history event,
  preserve the common stationary state and reconstruct both directions
  without either duplicating the event or hard-coding one application’s
  coefficients into the generic history core?

The complete picture is

- local signed Z4^9 write

- W8 source / X1 transition / W9 target

- reciprocal W9 pair W9P

- AFC intersection

- one massless Theta event Gram

- append event to shared-anchor reel

- application adapter reads declared receipt

The source state may return to W8 at completion. The reel nevertheless
grows because the receipt records the event, not merely the terminal
address.

Generic directional core

Exact inventory

The generic core contains

- Theta18 addresses. Exact inventory: 262,144.

- packed factorization. Exact inventory: 512 × 512 = 4⁹.

- directional maps. Exact inventory: 18.

- W8/X1/W9 receipts. Exact inventory: 72.

- nonconstant history rank. Exact inventory: 161.

- nonconstant symmetric coordinates. Exact inventory: 80.

- antisymmetric coordinates. Exact inventory: 81.

- common constant kernel. Exact inventory: 1.

- implementation checks. Exact inventory: 42/42.

The 18 maps are the nine axes with two signed orientations. Four
endpoint reversal classes applied to those 18 directions give the 72
receipt census:

9 ⋅ 2 ⋅ 4 = 72

The core performs exact write, inverse return, direction recovery and
history reconstruction before an adapter supplies application-specific
weights.

State and path are separate outputs

For reciprocal histories L, R ∈ ℚ⁸¹, define

S = L + R,   A = L − R

One common constant coordinate is redundant in the nonconstant quotient.
With anchor c, store

S̃_(i) = S_(i) − c,  i = 1, …, 80

and all 81 entries of A. Reconstruction is

S = (S̃₁ + c, …, S̃₈₀ + c, c)

$$L=\frac{S+A}{2},
\qquad
R=\frac{S-A}{2}$$

The quotient retains 161 nonconstant coordinates; the anchor carries the
one constant direction. A final address alone cannot reconstruct this
ordered pair, which is why endpoint-only controls are deliberately
insufficient.

Append only lifecycle

Exact Write completion returns both sites to W8 and appends the same
reciprocal receipt to both immutable history tuples. The two endpoint
references point to one event object. Appending later events never edits
earlier receipts:

- reel_0 = ()

- reel_1 = (theta_1)

- reel_2 = (theta_1, theta_2)

- …

- reel_n = (theta_1, …, theta_n)

An extremal or compressed finite reader may summarize this reel. It must
not be renamed as the full append-only object.

Event incidence and unique Theta Gram

One translation two ends one event

Let U_(e) be the exact unitary or permutation translation for event e.
Define the incidence operator

B_(e) = I − U_(e)

The unique event Gram is

Θ_(e) = B_(e)^(*)B_(e) ≽ 0

For any state h,

⟨h, Θ_(e)h⟩ = ⟨B_(e)h, B_(e)h⟩ = ∥h − U_(e)h∥² ≥ 0

Replacing e by its reciprocal orientation sends U_(e) to U_(e)⁻¹. The
directed incidence changes orientation, but the source/target pair still
defines one undirected event energy. On cyclic controls, rowwise
incidences sum exactly:

(I − U_(e))^(*)(I − U_(e)) = ∑_(position)b_(e, position)^(*)b_(e, position)

Thus two W9 directions are the pre-intersection readback of one Theta
Gram, not two counted carriers.

Append rule and stationary kernel

With positive event weight w_(e), the reel Laplacian evolves by

L_(n + 1) = L_(n) + w_(e)Θ_(e)

If the underlying event graph is connected on m sites, its incidence
Laplacian has rank (m-1), and the constant vector is the kernel:

L_(n)1 = 0

The stationary substrate baseline therefore survives every event.
Appending history grows the nonconstant range while preserving one
common mode.

N144 AFC frame

F81 contact incidence

The matched F81 contact graph factors as

G_(F81) = P₃▫G_(V27)

so its Laplacian is

L_(F81) = L_(P3) ⊗ I₂₇ + I₃ ⊗ L_(V27)

The two address-preserving handoffs contribute 27 edges each. Their
once- oriented AFC incidence has shape 54 × 81, rank 54 and

(−B_(AFC))^(T)(−B_(AFC)) = B_(AFC)^(T)B_(AFC)

Orientation reversal therefore retains one Gram. Internal incidence has
rank 78; completed incidence has rank 80; the only final kernel is
span {1₈₁}. Exact LDL reconstruction gives

λ₂(L_(F81)) = 1

The gap is carried by the completed incidence while AFC remains a
zero-mass carrier.

Frozen N144 to L162 wiring

The shared state is

z = (U_(left)⁶³, U_(right)⁶³, Θ_(shared)¹⁸) ∈ ℝ¹⁴⁴

The same-view embedding duplicates the shared Theta coordinates into the
two occurrence lanes:

E_(same)z = (U_(left)⁶³, Θ¹⁸; U_(right)⁶³, Θ¹⁸) ∈ ℝ¹⁶²

This map has rank 144. Compose it with the Exact Write quotient Q₈₁:

A = Q₈₁E_(same)

With the retained anchor a = Θ₁₇, exact composition gives

$$\begin{aligned}
\|Az\|^2
=2\sum_{126\ U\ leaves}(z_i-a)^2\\
\quad+4\sum_{17\ nonanchor\ Theta\ leaves}(z_j-a)^2\end{aligned}$$

Thus A^(T)A is a weighted-star Laplacian with one kernel, 126 weight-two
leaves and 17 weight-four leaves. Its spectrum is

$$0^1, 2^{125},
(163-\sqrt{25417})^1,
4^{16},
(163+\sqrt{25417})^1$$

Since $163-\sqrt{25417}>2$, the sharp unbalanced lower-frame constant is
2; the reciprocal-balanced constant is 1.

W9P to Theta isometry and global reel

Equal energy local write

Let G₁₄₄ = A^(*)A. For a source event define two Hilbert-valued
embeddings:

$$J_e^Wh
=\mathbf1_{144}\otimes h
+(e_{U_l0}-e_{U_r0})\otimes\frac{B_eh}{2}$$

$$J_e^\Theta h
=\mathbf1_{144}\otimes h
+e_{\Theta0}\otimes\frac{B_eh}{2}$$

The two W9 deviations each occupy a weight-two leaf, so their energy is

$$2\left\|\frac{B_eh}{2}\right\|^2
+2\left\|\frac{B_eh}{2}\right\|^2
=\|B_eh\|^2$$

The single Theta deviation occupies a weight-four leaf:

$$4\left\|\frac{B_eh}{2}\right\|^2
=\|B_eh\|^2$$

Hence

(J_(e)^(W))^(*)(G₁₄₄ ⊗ I)J_(e)^(W) = (J_(e)^(Θ))^(*)(G₁₄₄ ⊗ I)J_(e)^(Θ) = B_(e)^(*)B_(e)

The AFC intersection preserves event energy while translating a
reciprocal two-lane informational write into one massless history
receipt.

Shared anchor event family

Use one N144 cell per event and identify every cell’s quotient-reference
coordinate. For event set ℰ, each new cell contributes 143 new nonanchor
ranks. Therefore

nodes = 1 + 143|ℰ|

rank = 143|ℰ|

with one common scalar kernel. Stacking events gives

(J_(ℰ)^(W))^(*)G_(reel)J_(ℰ)^(W) = (J_(ℰ)^(Θ))^(*)G_(reel)J_(ℰ)^(Θ) = ∑_(e ∈ ℰ)w_(e)B_(e)^(*)B_(e)

For the completed RH source, continuous and prime-power events use

$$B_x=I-U_x,
\qquad
w_\infty(x)=\frac{e^{-x/2}}{1-e^{-2x}}\,dx$$

and

B_(p, k) = I − U_(klog p),   w_(p, k) = log (p)p^(−k/2)

The completed event operator is the pullback

L_(D) = (J_(D)^(Θ))^(*)G_(reel)J_(D)^(Θ)

This is the exact generic-to-RH seam. RH weights enter through the
adapter; they are not hard-coded in the generic reel.

Preserved deviation chain

Generic positive pencil contraction fails

The positive Stieltjes control

$$f(z)=1+\frac1{1-z}+\frac1{2-z}+\frac1{3-z}$$

has positive nested sections and decreasing extremal constants, yet

$$\Lambda_2(10)=\frac{5{,}655{,}335}{5{,}212{,}334}>1$$

Thus generic positivity does not force adjacent reel contraction. On the
actual source, all 2,880 nonleading coefficients across 96 adjacent
residuals are positive and yield 0 < Λ_(N)(u) < 1 for all u > 0 on every
executed pair. The source-specific signature survives; the generic
promotion does not.

Scalar RII control is insufficient

Positive recurrence data r_(N), a_(N), δ_(N) > 0 occurs in exact
controls whose adjacent residual polynomial has a negative coefficient
or nonpositive value. The scalar signature therefore cannot replace the
paired event Gram. The correction retains the full incidence operator
B_(e)^(*)B_(e).

Local frame debit domination fails

The W9P-to-Theta isometry is exact, but a rational-rotation control has
local event energy 4/5 against raw matched debit 2. The local N144 frame
alone does not supply the completed lower-frame inequality. The source
and endpoint conditions remain necessary.

Isolated recurrence lane fails

In the two-step recurrence

T_(N) = (u + N²)²T_(N − 1) + Q_(N)R_(N)

the completed factor

R_(N) = Q_(N + 1) − (u + N²)²Q_(N − 1)

stays positive on the executed source. The isolated lane
D_(N) = Q_(N) − (u + N²)Q_(N − 1) has negative active coefficients at
D = 0.7, 1. Completion, not the isolated carried lane, owns the selected
sign.

Whole matrix AFC order fails

The determinant and terminal-pivot AFC identities close, but the
stronger matrix order

D_(N)^(T)M_(N)D_(N) < N(N + 1)H_(N)

has a negative direction already at D = 0.7, N = 4. AFC is retained at
the exact quotient and terminal scalar level rather than promoted to
every matrix direction.

Individual parity factors fail

The source terminal scalar factors into a coupled parity product. Each
individual factor fails on some exact controls, while 367 controls close
the product without both component inequalities. The compensating
product is the supported object. A first smoke instrument omitted the
odd Fourier lift and was low by N²; its receipt is preserved and the
corrected instrument changes only that lift.

Spherical diagonal order fails

The mode-local strengthening

L_(N)G_(N)^(o)L_(N) − G_(N)^((e, 2)) > 0

has only 9 positive gaps among 66 tested rows; 57 contain a negative
direction. The first witness is D = 0.7, N = 3 with minimum eigenvalue
approximately −0.2266605255. Every corresponding terminal scalar remains
positive. The failure therefore rejects the matrix strengthening without
discarding the coupled endpoint scalar.

Application adapter authority boundary

The generic reel owns addresses, writes, receipts, the history quotient,
unique event Grams and append-only reconstruction. An adapter may supply
a declared source and read the resulting receipt:

- RH moment adapter. Permitted use: completed continuous/prime weights
  and endpoint kernel; Authority not acquired: global RH claim or
  universal sign by interface alone.

- Starbreaker consumer. Permitted use: typed formation-event history;
  Authority not acquired: scheduler or physical calibration without
  promotion.

- Mersenne application. Permitted use: exact factor-search receipts;
  Authority not acquired: primality or global scheduling from a factor
  miss.

A later adapter supplements the reel. It cannot rewrite earlier events
or insert its coefficients into the generic core retroactively. Frozen
SLCQ2-RZ remains the global source-recorded default SLC; the
source-recorded RH-local handle is SLCQ2-RZ-RH-MOMENT-ADAPTER-V1 and
remains an application-specific consumer.

Established result and forward handoff

The generic core closes 42/42 checks across its complete address, map,
lifecycle and history inventory. The append-only event construction
identifies one reciprocal W9 pair with one Theta Gram, embeds that event
isometrically in the shared N144 AFC frame and grows the reel by 143
ranks per event while retaining one common kernel.

The failure chain identifies the exact boundary of that result: source-
specific completed scalars and full event Grams remain load-bearing;
generic positive-pencil, isolated-lane and whole-matrix shortcuts do not
replace them. SAMA-D000043 carries the completed event operator into the
separately typed RH route.

Sources and evidential scope

This chapter is assembled from the following fixed source manuscripts.
Source-reported campaign outcomes retain their original scope; no new
campaign was executed for this edition.

- Directional history and event reel computation.

Chapter X Equal Observable States and Executable Histories

A common operation separates two histories with the same total

The SLC constructs exact examples in which two objects give the same
declared observation and remain distinguishable under a later operation.
The retained information is an operand of that operation. The smallest
example begins with the ordered histories [1,8] and [8,1]. Both have
terminal sum 9. Applying the same connector rule to the first retained
entry returns two different rational values. [EQ, pp2-4]

A declared observation is a map that exports selected information from a
complete object. Write π for that map and C for an admitted response.
The object retains its ordered source history in addition to its
exported value. For the connector construction, the response rule is the
source lift

$$
L(p)=p+\frac{p^2}{144}$$

The first retained entries are 1 and 8. Direct substitution gives

$$
L(1)=\frac{145}{144},\qquad L(8)=\frac{76}{9},\qquad L(8)-L(1)=\frac{119}{16}=7.4375$$

The terminal sum is exactly equal while the common response is exactly
unequal. These are rational identities, with no fitted tolerance. An
order-insensitive whole-contact control gives

$$
L(1+8)-L(1)-L(8)=\frac{1}{9}
$$

for either order. The distinction therefore belongs to a specific
history-sensitive operation: the lift reads the first retained prefix.
The symmetric contact operation reads both operands without their order
and agrees. The source’s history identifiers name the records; their
labels are not numerical operands in the response.

This example states the computational achievement precisely. A system
receiving only terminal sum 9 cannot reproduce both required
first-prefix responses. It must retain the first prefix, retain the
ordered word, or retain another sufficient receipt from which that
information can be recovered. The result is about the specified
observation and action. It does not claim that the objects agree under
an observation vector that already includes the separating response.

The exact criterion for a sufficient observation

A fiber is the set of complete objects with one exported observation. A
response can be computed from that observation alone exactly when it is
constant on every relevant fiber. In symbols, a map f with C = f ∘ π
exists if and only if

π(x) = π(y)  ⇒  C(x) = C(y)

The proof is short. If C = f ∘ π, equal observations give equal
responses by applying f. Conversely, if the response is constant on each
fiber, define f at an observed value by evaluating C on any object in
that fiber. Constancy makes the choice immaterial. [EQ, pp3-4]

The connector pair violates that condition: f(9) would have to equal
both 145/144 and 76/9. This is the exact obstruction to deleting the
prefix before the response is evaluated. For a partial action, admission
is also part of the contract. If two objects share an observation but
only one admits the action, the observation cannot determine whether the
action is legal.

The criterion also governs composition. Equal exported inputs must have
the same admitted actions, immediate responses, and exported targets if
they are to remain interchangeable in a sequence. A receipt sufficient
for one final scalar can fail under a later operation that reads
information that scalar discarded. The relevant compression question is
consequently which information suffices for the declared action
language.

Receiver sums and directed currents reconstruct the parents

The receiver construction makes the missing coordinate explicit. Let L
and R be nonnegative integer history tensors in a common source-labeled
basis. The even carrier and directed current are

S = L + R,   J = R − L

They reconstruct the parent tensors through

$$
L=\frac{S-J}{2},\qquad R=\frac{S+J}{2}$$

For integer tensors the two numerators must be even coefficient by
coefficient. The native constructor checks that condition and recovers
the supplied inputs. The current may be signed while the stored history
multiplicities remain nonnegative; those are different types. [EQ,
pp4-5]

Applying the actual current adds J on the left and subtracts it on the
right:

Φ_(J)(L, R) = (L + J, R − J) = (R, L)

The sum is conserved, and recomputing the oriented current after the
exchange gives −J. The inverse transfer therefore uses −J. The completed
A3D28 roster contains six unequal history-pair types, each in a
two-cycle, and four fixed self-pairs. Deleted-current and wrong-current
controls reach none of the six unequal targets. Thus the directed
coordinate both distinguishes the orientations and executes the exchange
that the sum alone cannot specify.

Ordered paths and the reconstructive address

Adjacent receiver exchanges on a triple obey the permutation relations
of S₃. Write s₁ and s₂ for exchanging the first two and last two
positions. Then s₁² = s₂² = id, s₁s₂s₁ = s₂s₁s₂, and (s₁s₂)³ = id, while
s₁s₂ and s₂s₁ have different endpoint actions. The braid words s₁s₂s₁
and s₂s₁s₂ reach the same reversed triple through different intermediate
triples. Retaining the action word preserves that path distinction after
the endpoint permutation has been reported. The completed A3D29 body
contains four six-state orbits, 24 states and 48 directed generator
edges. [EQ, p5]

The next source construction joins a rooted triple’s exterior residue,
terminal position, and orientation. An exterior residue records oriented
two-direction area: for source vectors a,b,c it is (b − a) ∧ (c − a),
which changes sign when the directions are exchanged. The source records
48 nonzero two-Write residues and 48 zero one-Write controls. Residue
alone recovers roster and orientation but leaves six possible positions.
Joining the position coordinate closes that inverse problem.

The completed address has four rosters, six S₃ positions, and two
orientations, giving 4 × 6 × 2 = 48 outcomes. Every combination occurs
once. Decoding reconstructs the source and terminal triples, the
two-generator word, action-chain receipts, and signed residue;
re-encoding is byte-identical. Removing roster leaves 12 classes of four
outcomes, removing position leaves eight classes of six, and removing
orientation leaves 24 pairs. These erasure controls show exactly which
information each coordinate supplies. [EQ, pp5-6]

For a fixed root, a mixed-radix index n = 12r + 2p + o, with 0 ≤ r < 4,
0 ≤ p < 6 and 0 ≤ o < 2, selects the 48 outcomes. The shared source
tables remain necessary to reconstruct the full semantic object. This is
an exact address into a declared source body, rather than a claim that
six bits independently contain every source definition.

A whole weave can retain many histories above one invariant

The A3D37 weave carries 48 occurrence-frames per plan: eight cube sites,
three rooted slots and multiplicity two. Its roster contains 288
oriented plans, reduced to 144 by the global C₂ quotient. The
type-resolved weave separates those plans into six classes of 24, while
total holonomy has only one class on the released roster. [EQ, pp6-7]

The invariant is exact, and its common value is insufficient to select a
single plan. The completed result is the retained six-by-twenty-four
history structure. Its load-profile census contains 24 plans with
quadratic load 240, 72 with load 192, and 48 with load 144. A later
source-weighted action can consume those retained distinctions. The
failed total-holonomy singleton selector identifies the information that
invariant omits; it does not erase the constructed weave.

A receiver functional makes the source difference usable

For the occupied receiver example, the before and after vectors are

h₀ = (1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1)^(T),   h₁ = (0, 1, 1, 1, 0, 0)^(T)

Let Q be a real symmetric receiver form and ε its scale. The quadratic
response difference is

ΔH = εtr (QD),   D = h₁h₁^(T) − h₀h₀^(T)

The vectors are orthogonal with squared norm three. Hence D has rank two
and nonzero eigenvalues +3 and −3. The transfer therefore specifies one
exact linear functional of the receiver form, supported on two source
directions. The source records twelve nonzero coefficient weights when
the functional is written in independent symmetric-form coordinates.
[EQ, pp7-8,13-14]

The complete source difference is already available for further
calculation. Assigning a physical Q, scale, and energy reference remains
a separate source operation. The mathematical functional must not be
presented as an already measured nuclear binding energy; its substantive
result is the exact form dependence and retained operands.

Chapter XI Ordinal Selection and the Delegated Exact Engine

The frozen Q2 selector improves weighted strict-pair ordering over Q1 by
63200/594823 on HOLDOUT and 83595/773858 on CONTROL. It recovers every
exact best set on both surfaces without a false singleton or abstention.
Its 70 source-visible features order admissible receipts while the
unchanged arithmetic services remain delegated to SLCV21R.

The historical constructions in this chapter retain the fixed 8
September 2026 SAMA source stage. Later results are identified by their
own source records.

The September SAMA selector chapter cites H000531, H000551 and H000552
for Q2. The accessible August public SAM Research Project live page
instead records V21R, and its named Q2 candidate and promotion artifact
paths were unavailable during preparation. This chapter therefore
preserves the later manuscript account with that retrieval limitation;
it does not independently certify a live pointer transition.

Overview

Exact computation can return several admissible receipts for one query.
The arithmetic substrate certifies what every receipt is, while the
selection plane asks a different question: which admissible receipt has
the best exact return quality when the selector is permitted to see only
source geometry? SLCQ2-RZ is the frozen current answer to that ordinal
question.

The target quality is not a learned label invented after execution. For
each candidate receipt it is the frozen reciprocal-quotiented
lexicographic vector

Q = (d_(direction), r_(inverse), r_(W8), r_(D81))

where lower is better and equal vectors remain a complete target tie. Q2
does not read any component used to construct that target. It exposes 70
exact integer features computed from axis, modulus, shell, direction and
two typed domain labels. It excludes inverse inputs, inverse directions,
returned endpoints, D81 checkpoints, target tiers, custody identities,
hashes, execution order and timing.

The data surface contains 5,120 inherited exact receipts, 1,024
canonical queries and 512 reciprocal/replicate-safe groups. Each axis is
frozen into 80 TRAIN, 24 HOLDOUT and 24 CONTROL groups. TRAIN supplies
640 queries; HOLDOUT and CONTROL each expose a complete 192-query
catalog. Three exact-rational architectures at three predeclared ridge
values form nine fits. HOLDOUT fixes the winner before CONTROL is
opened:

Q2-HYBRID_LISTWISE_ADJACENT_ORDINAL_RIDGE-RIDGE-1D1000.

Against frozen Q1 on the same fresh surfaces, Q2 raises weighted
strict-pair accuracy by exactly 63200/594823 on HOLDOUT and 83595/773858
on CONTROL, while recovering every exact best set, creating no false
singleton, making no abstention and producing no strict score tie. The
result is exact best-set recovery on both frozen splits with the stated
improvement in pair ordering.

The architecture is deliberately layered. Q2 owns the current ordinal
selection plane. Frozen SLCV21R remains noncurrent as a revision but
remains the byte-identical delegated arithmetic and application base. Q1
is an immutable fallback. Neither selection nor promotion assigns
physical scale, absorbs ATOM3D, changes Exact Write semantics or
authorizes application work.

Opening question and conceptual picture

The governing question is:

  Can exact source-visible event geometry order the complete admissible
  receipt catalog more faithfully than Q1 without reading any executed
  value that defines the answer?

The typed route is

- frozen SLCV21R exact query and candidate receipts

- reciprocal/replicate-safe grouping

- target-only exact return-quality vector

- 70-field source-visible feature map

- exact listwise and adjacent-ordinal fitting on TRAIN

- winner lock on complete HOLDOUT

- one comparison on sealed complete CONTROL

- frozen Q2 selection service

- SLCQ2-RZ current pointer

Three separations carry the meaning of the result:

1.  the exact substrate determines admissibility and computes receipts;

2.  the target oracle evaluates return quality but is hidden from
    selection;

3.  the selector orders candidates from permitted source geometry alone.

Collapsing these surfaces would make the question circular. Keeping them
separate lets the result say something precise: the visible structural
fields contain enough ordinal information for the selected exact model
to recover the complete best set and nearly all strict pair orderings on
two held-out complete catalogs.

Recorded authority and architectural planes

Sole source recorded default revision

SLCQ2-RZ has status FROZEN_COMPLETE_STOPPED_PROMOTED_CURRENT and is the
sole source-recorded default SLC pointer. Older v1.x, V20R and SLCV21R
revisions retain their artifacts and scoped results; they are not
alternative current pointers. The older Volume III technical spine
therefore supplies lineage, not present-tense pointer authority.

Q1 remains frozen and immutable as the fallback selector. A fallback is
not a second default: it is a preserved predecessor available under the
declared service boundary.

Selection is not arithmetic

For a query q, let 𝒞(q) be the finite set of receipts certified
admissible by the exact base. Q2 supplies a score

s_(q)(c) = w^(T)ϕ(q, c),   c ∈ 𝒞(q)

with exact rational coefficient vector w and exact integer feature
vector ϕ(q, c) ∈ ℤ⁷⁰. It returns all candidates attaining the maximum
permitted score, subject to the ambiguity policy described below.

The delegated base still owns DOS construction, exact write, reciprocal
return, checkpoints and application execution. Installing a new w
changes the ordering of already-admissible candidates; it does not
change what the candidate means or how its exact receipt is computed.

Four distinct planes

- exact substrate. Owner in the installed architecture: frozen SLCV21R
  services; Output: admissible exact receipts and arithmetic.

- quality target. Owner in the installed architecture: frozen
  reciprocal-quotiented oracle; Output: exact lexicographic target
  vectors.

- ordinal selector. Owner in the installed architecture: current
  SLCQ2-RZ Q2 model; Output: score order and complete top-score set.

- application. Owner in the installed architecture: separately declared
  adapter/model; Output: application-specific interpretation or
  execution.

An ATOM3D model, RH adapter or Mersenne scheduler may call the service
only through its own typed contract. None becomes part of Q2 merely by
using its ordering.

Target construction and blindness contract

Exact target order

The target for candidate c is

Q_(q)(c) = (d_(direction)(q, c), r_(inverse)(q, c), r_(W8)(q, c), r_(D81)(q, c))

Vectors compare lexicographically, with smaller values preferred. The
first coordinate at which two candidates differ decides their strict
order. If no coordinate differs, both belong to the same target tier. In
particular, a custody identifier never converts an exact target tie into
a singleton.

The complete best set is

B_(q) = {c ∈ 𝒞(q) : Q_(q)(c) = min_(c^(′) ∈ 𝒞(q))Q_(q)(c^(′))}

Recovery is exact only when the returned set equals B_(q), not when it
merely contains one member of B_(q).

Permitted source fields

Q2 may read only:

- axis

- modulus

- left_shell, right_shell

- left_direction, right_direction

- route_domain

- physical_relation_domain

From these it computes the fixed 70-field map. The two domain labels are
constant on this source; they serve as a typed zero-variation
interaction check, not as an undeclared ranking signal.

Forbidden target and custody fields

The selector may not read:

- inverse input lanes or inverse directions

- returned endpoint lanes

- D81 checkpoint lanes

- quality vector or quality tier

- receipt index or pair identity

- group hash or source hash

- execution sequence

- timing

This prohibition is load-bearing. Inverse, endpoint and checkpoint
values construct the oracle and would leak the answer. Custody fields
could create memorized ordering without structural meaning. Timings
could import hardware and execution-order artifacts into a mathematical
selector.

Fresh grouped custody

Why the unit is a physical group

Individual receipt splitting is a wrong control because reciprocal
directions and replicated views of one physical relation can then appear
on opposite sides of a split. A model could learn one view and be scored
on its mate. Q2 hashes a fresh namespace together with axis, modulus,
unordered shell endpoints and the two source domain labels. All
reciprocal directions and replicates sharing that identity remain in one
group.

The frozen census is

- inherited exact receipts. Exact count: 5,120.

- canonical queries. Exact count: 1,024.

- reciprocal/replicate-safe groups. Exact count: 512.

- reciprocal canonical queries per group. Exact count: 2.

- observed group multiplicities. Exact count: 4, 10, 16.

The nonuniform multiplicities matter. A group contributes according to
its actual candidate roster; assuming one uniform multiplicity changes
the catalog and its weights.

Split derivation

Within each axis, group hashes are sorted and frozen as

80 TRAIN + 24 HOLDOUT + 24 CONTROL = 128

groups. Across the four axes this gives the 512-group total. The query
census is

4 ⋅ 80 ⋅ 2 = 640  TRAIN queries

4 ⋅ 24 ⋅ 2 = 192

queries on each of HOLDOUT and CONTROL.

TRAIN uses the frozen harder tier rule and contains 18,654 group
references and 182,688 receipt exposures. HOLDOUT and CONTROL retain
every split-local candidate group, yielding complete 192-query catalogs.
Selection therefore cannot improve a metric by silently dropping
difficult candidates.

Deriving the 70 feature representation

Direct and reciprocal alignment

Let the centered source shell endpoints of a query and candidate be
exact integers. Q2 forms both alignments:

- direct: query left -> candidate left, query right -> candidate right

- swapped: query left -> candidate right, query right -> candidate left

For each alignment it retains signed residuals before taking absolute or
squared summaries. Schematically, with query endpoints ((a,b)) and
candidate endpoints ((c,d)),

δ_(direct) = (c − a, d − b),   δ_(swap) = (d − a, c − b)

The map includes the signed components, absolute components, squares,
alignment sums and best/other residual summaries. Because both
constructions are ordered by a source-visible canonical key before
exposure, a whole-lane swap exchanges internal work without changing the
final visible vector.

Midpoint span direction and parity

For endpoints ((a,b)), define signed midpoint numerator and span

m = a + b,   ℓ = b − a

Using the numerator avoids a floating-point half. Candidate-minus-query
relations for m and ℓ retain orientation and separation that coarse
shell bins erased. Individual direction matches and endpoint parity
relations are then appended rather than compressed to a single flag.

Two adic profiles and typed interactions

For a nonzero integer n, v₂(n) is the largest e ≥ 0 for which 2^(e) ∣ n.
Q2 includes four direct/swapped alignment valuations and two
span-relation valuations, with the frozen convention for zero. These
features expose dyadic shell structure without reading any returned
value.

Route-domain and physical-relation-domain equality fields and their
declared interactions preserve the type boundary. Fourteen bounded
quadratic cross terms add fixed pair interactions; each is clipped at
exact magnitude 16,777,215. The clip is part of the pre-execution
feature contract and not a post-result adjustment.

Why Q2 succeeds where the 20 field map collided

Q1’s coarser 20-feature map has strict-pair feature-collision rates
2.3255657565% on HOLDOUT and 2.1898074324% on CONTROL. When two
candidates with different target order have the same visible vector, any
deterministic linear score must tie them. Q2’s signed, unbinned and
reciprocal-invariant map has zero strict feature collisions on both
surfaces. This removes that representational obstruction before model
fitting; it does not guarantee a correct order by itself.

Exact ordinal fitting

Three predeclared architectures

The bounded roster contains:

1.  EXPANDED_QUERY_CENTERED_LISTWISE_RIDGE, which fits query-centered
    tier grades over the harder TRAIN catalog;

2.  HYBRID_LISTWISE_ADJACENT_ORDINAL_RIDGE, which appends exact
    differences for adjacent strict tiers with frozen weight four; and

3.  LEXICOGRAPHIC_CONDITIONAL_MULTI_HEAD_RIDGE, which learns the first
    target coordinate globally and later coordinates only inside
    equality classes of the preceding coordinates.

Each architecture is fitted at exact ridge values

$$\lambda\in\left\{\frac1{1000},\frac1{100},\frac1{10}\right\}$$

giving 3 × 3 = 9 fits. There is no floating-point training and no
full-scale hyperparameter sweep.

Listwise and adjacent observations

For each training query, a target tier grade is centered within its
complete candidate list. The listwise design therefore learns relative
order without letting different query offsets dominate.

For adjacent strict tiers, choose c₊ from the better tier and c⁻ from
the next worse tier. The hybrid supplies the exact difference row

Δϕ = ϕ(q, c₊) − ϕ(q, c⁻)

with a positive ordinal target and frozen weight four. A fitted vector
should therefore satisfy

w^(T)Δϕ > 0

for that adjacent relation. Chaining adjacent relations transports the
order through the tier sequence while the listwise rows retain the
complete query geometry.

With exact design matrix X, target y, diagonal frozen observation
weights W and ridge λ, the rational normal system is

(X^(T)WX + λI)w = X^(T)Wy

All inputs and outputs remain Fraction values. Exact fitting makes score
equality a mathematical event rather than a floating tolerance choice.

Winner and tie policy

The three hybrid fits have identical complete-catalog ranking metrics.
Only after mathematical metrics and parameter count tie does the frozen
key order select the lower regularization identifier. The resulting
winner is

Q2-HYBRID_LISTWISE_ADJACENT_ORDINAL_RIDGE-RIDGE-1D1000.

At service time, a score tie spanning distinct target vectors triggers
abstention. A score tie wholly inside one exact target vector returns
the complete tied best set. The promoted wrapper names a top-score
multiplicity explicitly as AMBIGUOUS_TOP_SCORE; it does not choose by
receipt ID.

HOLDOUT selection and sealed CONTROL

Predeclared material comparator

Before execution, success required all three conditions:

- at least one percentage point of weighted strict-pair gain over frozen
  Q1 on HOLDOUT;

- positive gain on CONTROL; and

- perfect exact-best-set recovery on both complete surfaces.

HOLDOUT selected and locked the model. CONTROL was opened once afterward
for the comparison to the frozen Q1 baseline.

Exact results

- HOLDOUT. Frozen Q1: 530826/594823; Q2 winner: 594026/594823; Exact
  delta: 63200/594823; Decimal point gain: +10.625009.

- CONTROL. Frozen Q1: 689509/773858; Q2 winner: 386552/386929; Exact
  delta: 83595/773858; Decimal point gain: +10.802369.

The CONTROL Q2 fraction has denominator half the Q1 display denominator,
so the like-denominator comparison is

$$\frac{386552}{386929}
=\frac{773104}{773858}$$

and

$$\frac{773104-689509}{773858}
=\frac{83595}{773858}$$

Q2 retains 6,376 wrong weighted strict pairs on HOLDOUT and 6,032 on
CONTROL. Those residual errors remain in the record. At the service
boundary, however, best-tier accuracy and exact-best-set recovery are
both 1/1 on both splits, with zero false singletons, zero abstentions
and zero strict score ties.

The frozen comparison improves weighted pair ordering while retaining
every exact best set and all maximizing ties.

Deviation correction and retest chain

Candidate construction failures

Five preserved attempts materially shaped the frozen candidate:

- 001. Failure: a cross-axis unit fixture mixed an axis-specific
  assumption into the test; Correction: construct the fixture inside its
  declared axis/type; Retest consequence: boundary tests reached the
  intended feature question.

- 002. Failure: code assumed uniform group multiplicity; Correction:
  retain the frozen observed roster {4,10,16}; Retest consequence:
  complete catalogs and exposure weights reconstructed exactly.

- 003. Failure: reciprocal orientation changed the visible feature
  vector; Correction: canonicalize complete direct/swapped blocks by
  source-visible keys; Retest consequence: whole-lane swap became
  invariant.

- 004. Failure: a serializer could not carry the exact fraction surface;
  Correction: encode exact numerators/denominators through the declared
  receipt form; Retest consequence: checkpoint and replay identity
  remained exact.

- 005. Failure: the Q1 collision diagnostic read the wrong comparison
  source; Correction: recompute that diagnostic from the frozen Q1
  comparator on Q2 surfaces; Retest consequence: the corrected Q1
  collision rates changed; Q2 model selection and CONTROL result did
  not.

The first four attempts stopped before model selection or CONTROL
access. The fifth corrected a predecessor diagnostic only. Keeping these
attempts makes clear which faults affected construction and which did
not affect the frozen winner.

Promotion wrapper corrections

Promotion also preserved two distinct corrections:

- Attempt 001 expected an assertions_passed field, while the independent
  receipt records assertion_count=165. The wrapper was corrected to read
  the actual schema before installation continued.

- Attempt 002 installed an executable resolution correctly, but the
  pointer inherited explanatory usage language and stale promotion
  fields. Pointer metadata was corrected, producing current pointer
  SHA-256
  751f6e6a6dbe01cfdc335cf303824887148c9eb657ded691264e37c6f61fa318, and
  the promotion surface was revalidated.

Neither correction retrained Q2, reopened Q1 or mined CONTROL.

Wrong controls retained as lessons

The following alternatives would answer a different question and are not
the installed controls:

- receipt-level random splitting, because it leaks reciprocal or
  replicated physical relations;

- incomplete HOLDOUT or CONTROL candidate rosters, because they can hide
  misranking and false singleton behavior;

- target-visible fields, because they make ordinal prediction circular;

- custody-ID tie breaking, because it converts exact ambiguity into an
  arbitrary order;

- floating score tolerances, because exact equality would depend on
  runtime;

- opening CONTROL during model choice, because CONTROL would cease to be
  the postselection surface.

Freeze and promotion process

The candidate freeze passed 8/8 release checks, 14/14 reconstruction
checks, 165/165 independent assertions and, after the diagnostic
correction, 8/8 release checks again. The promotion wrapper then passed:

- pre-install. Result: 22/22.

- post-install. Result: 12/12.

- independent promotion validation. Result: 20/20.

- final release. Result: 6/6.

Promotion changed the live selection pointer through H000551; H000552
records the pointer-metadata correction. It preserved the candidate, Q1
and delegated base artifacts rather than rewriting them.

The promotion performed no retraining, CONTROL mining, physical
calibration, ATOM3D specialization, remote transfer, prime work,
publication or deployment. These are not missing steps in Q2. They are
separate campaigns requiring their own contracts and authority.

Established result and boundary

This document establishes the following typed statement:

  On the frozen Q2 grouped catalogs, the 70-feature exact hybrid
  listwise-plus-adjacent ordinal selector materially improves
  strict-pair ordering over frozen Q1 and recovers every complete exact
  best set. Its promotion makes SLCQ2-RZ the sole source-recorded
  default SLC selection revision, while exact arithmetic remains
  delegated to frozen SLCV21R and Q1 remains the immutable fallback.

It does not establish a physical energy scale, a new DOS, a change to
Exact Write, a global optimizer for every application, native GPU
credit, ATOM3D specialization, an RH conclusion or a Mersenne status.
The next chapter uses Q2 only as a typed application dependency and
keeps its Mersenne scheduler and certificates separate.

Sources and evidential scope

- Slcq2 rz ordinal selection engine.

Chapter XII Starbreaker Formation on Its Own Grammar

Starbreaker retains 2,976 formation ledgers and 482,112 typed atoms with
their stages, event orders, incidence and record origins. A
structure-first replay reconstructs the completed parent byte for byte
and passes all 78 declared checks using its own grammar. The chapter
develops the source construction and the later reception and carrier
calculations that consume it.

The historical constructions in this chapter retain the fixed 8
September 2026 SAMA source stage. Later results are identified by their
own source records.

Overview

Starbreaker is a formation application with its own native grammar. It
retains 2,976 ledgers, 482,112 typed atoms, seed/collapse/final stages,
two event orders, two D18 pair maps, same and reversed occurrence views,
contact incidence, record origin and constrained M126/N144/N144+d/L162
readouts. N100 contributes structure-first routing discipline; its
100-spin graph is not substituted for this application.

The exact state ladder begins with M126 retention. Fee-exempt Theta18
provides an independent 18-coordinate lift, so 2¹²⁶ ⋅ 2¹⁸ = 2¹⁴⁴. L162
is an ambient occurrence view whose valid image still contains 2¹⁴⁴
states, not 2¹⁶² independent matter configurations. Active mismatch
coordinates travel in an N144+d sidecar rather than being promoted to
another object or capacity.

The structure-first replay detects those native quotient, fiber and
readout certificates. It reproduces the completed parent byte for byte,
passes 78/78 checks and never invokes dense H14F fallback. The W9
history refinement adds a rank-161 target-local order-history receipt,
but the mode is emit-only: it may record cross-stage memory and may not
alter scheduler grouping or allocation without a separate tested
promotion.

The Courtroom evidence supplies a complete methodology chain. CR210
reconstructs a scoped external-collapse directional-contact surface.
CR211 freezes a typed 162-slot particle-ledger interface while leaving
address-to-template incidence null. CR005c identifies why stage ordering
cannot be promoted to physical escape time. CR005d builds a
dimensionless continuous flow and carrier-QNM source bridge. CR005e
stops on a numerical trace-residue gate; CR005f freezes one exact
trace-free correction; CR005g runs the unchanged morphology design and
records the boundary that no stable Starbreaker/CCSN morphology match
cleared the gates. CR005h constructs a complete per-carrier ledger but
fails three controls; CR005i corrects only those controls, independently
replays all 96 scenarios and 53,568 carriers, adopts the byte-identical
ledger and leaves CR005h immutable.

Opening question and conceptual picture

The governing question is:

  How does Starbreaker carry a source-native formation history from
  typed ledgers through exact state accounting, directional contact,
  continuous source construction and controlled application readouts
  without replacing its grammar, erasing failed tests or promoting
  dimensionless receipts into physical observables?

The application picture is

- Starbreaker ledgers and typed atoms

- seed / collapse / final formation stages

- lexicographic and reverse event orders

- exact M126 retention

- fee-exempt Theta18 lift to N144

- optional active mismatch sidecar N144+d

- constrained L162 occurrence views

- emit-only W9 rank-161 history

- dimensionless source/application bridges

- physical calibration joins remain explicit

The route is layered. A state count is not a waveform. A source
quadrupole is not strain. A carrier with positive source influence is
not the substance transported by a gravitational wave. Keeping those
types separate is what allows the exact and exploratory work to
accumulate safely.

Native objects counts and type boundaries

Ledger and atom census

Each of 2,976 ledgers carries one 162-slot typed surface, giving

2, 976 ⋅ 162 = 482, 112

structured atoms. The slots are not assumed occupied merely because they
exist. Unary, pair, triad, carrier, support, scalar and rejected classes
remain distinct, and categorical relations remain unresolved until their
own geometry stage.

Starbreaker has three stages and two event orders. Therefore the number
of stage/order snapshots is

2, 976 ⋅ 3 ⋅ 2 = 17, 856

Executing both D18 pair-map controls doubles the runtime rows:

17, 856 ⋅ 2 = 35, 712

These identities are direct state-accounting checks. A dropped ledger,
stage, order or pair map changes the total immediately.

Persistent and transient types

The formation engine preserves the following firewall:

- O. Role: persistent matter identity; Nonidentity: not a substrate site
  phase.

- W8. Role: unresolved/returned substrate-site phase; Nonidentity: not a
  retained history rank.

- W9. Role: direct target phase during active contact; Nonidentity: not
  an independent source row.

- X1. Role: one transient H2 mirror bit; Nonidentity: not matter and
  ledger contribution zero.

- U. Role: coordinated reciprocal record exchange; Nonidentity: not a
  new material object.

- M126. Role: retained matter/output state; Nonidentity: not universal
  occurrence transport.

- Theta18. Role: fee-exempt directional carrier/fiber; Nonidentity: not
  retained mass lift.

- N144+d. Role: active defect-sidecar custody; Nonidentity: not extra
  independent capacity.

- L162. Role: constrained occurrence readout; Nonidentity: not 2¹⁶²
  independent physical states.

Completion returns both contacted sites to W8 and clears X1 while
retaining updated records. “State returns; history does not” means the
endpoint type can repeat while its reciprocal receipt remains nonzero.

Stage and control surfaces

The two event orders are lexicographic and reverse-lexicographic. The
retained pair-map controls are D18_P1_SHIFT_14 and D18_M1_SHIFT_08. Same
and reversed views test occurrence reconstruction from opposite readout
orientations.

These are application fixtures. A pair map or contact-incidence field
does not become a physical matter mirror, q orientation, Theta partition
or elementwise pairing unless a separate source selects it.

Construction interfaces

Particle ledger interface

CR:CR211@15 freezes one prototype 162-slot ledger and 321 distinct QP
templates. Its structural equalities include

18 + 126 + 18 = 162

9 ⋅ 18 = 162

and six copies of the 27-address H27 rank census. The CR210d boundary
retains the typed values 81/144/126/162.

The most important output is negative in a precise sense:
address-to-template incidence is explicitly null. No slot is claimed to
contain a particle merely because the register has that slot, and no
elementwise QP-to-F81 assignment is manufactured. The interface supplies
typed storage and structural authority; physical content, mass, binding
and Starbreaker dynamical incidence remain open.

Continuous flow and carrier QNM bridge

CR:CR005d@21 replaces the earlier affine stage probe with a zero-fit
continuous structural flow. The three stored states are joined by
exponential radial collapse, a bounce impulse at dimensionless phase
t = 1, and constant radial plus shortest-branch angular release. The
maximum reconstruction error at the stored states is 2.191 × 10⁻¹⁴.

For unit carrier positions x_(c)(t), a trace-free quadrupole has the
form

$$Q_{ab}(t)=\sum_c\left[x_{c,a}(t)x_{c,b}(t)
-\frac{\delta_{ab}}{3}|x_c(t)|^2\right]$$

The source surface uses d²Q_(ab)/dt²; a third derivative supplies a
power proxy. The carrier-QNM response convolves the source with the
sealed dimensionless kernel

$$g(u)=\frac{e^{-(4/45)u}\sin((3/8)u)}{3/8}$$

so ω_(R)M = 3/8 and ω_(I)M = 4/45 are fixed rather than fitted.

The run tracks 1,993 dual carriers. Of these, 1,541 satisfy escape
before closure on the continuous flow:

$$\frac{1{,}541}{1{,}993}=0.7732062217762168$$

The median closure-minus-escape margin is 0.04020605301428759. Across
the construction there are 10,515,538 transition roots and zero root
failures. All 14/14 construction gates and 10/10 controls pass; all 96
scenarios and 482,112 atoms are present. Maximum relative trace error is
2.5997906629060057 × 10⁻¹⁵.

The bridge remains dimensionless. Unit carrier weights are not matter
masses, phase time is not seconds, and the QNM response is not
gravitational-wave strain, luminosity, frequency in hertz or a detector
forecast.

Exact formation state derivation

M126 retention

The structured application begins with two exact N72 custody packets.
Each retains

2⁷² = 4, 722, 366, 482, 869, 645, 213, 696

binary states as a 64 × 10 conditional tensor. Their declared matter
join is rank 126 and accounts for

$$\begin{aligned}2^{126}=85070591730234615\cdot10^{21}\\\quad+865843651857942052864\end{aligned}$$

M126 configurations across 4,096 two-sided sectors. The scalar
background has 127 oriented marked sectors and 64 occupied energy bins
from −7875 through 63.

M126 retains record origin without promotion in 468 stage/order
snapshots and requires promotion in 17,388. Those counts sum to the
complete snapshot census:

468 + 17, 388 = 17, 856

The split demonstrates why M126 is a retention/output state, not a
universal occurrence-transport state.

Fee exempt Theta18 lift

Theta18 contributes 18 independent directional coordinates, so

2¹²⁶ ⋅ 2¹⁸ = 2¹⁴⁴

and

$$\begin{aligned}2^{144}=22300745198530623141535\cdot10^{21}\\\quad+718272648361505980416\end{aligned}$$

The lift is fee-exempt: no Theta multiplicity is folded into X1 and no
retained mass is attached to the carrier fiber.

N144 d and L162 readout

For a same-view mismatch mask, define

d_(t) = popcount (m_(t)),   0 ≤ d_(t) ≤ 18

The application route is

- d = 0 -> exact N144 independent union state

- 0 < d < 18 -> N144 plus active d-coordinate sidecar

- d = 18 -> L162 constrained occurrence readout

The L162 ambient ceiling is 2¹⁶², but each valid same or reversed image
contains 2¹⁴⁴ states. Their ratio is

$$\frac{2^{162}}{2^{144}}=2^{18}=262{,}144$$

Thus L162 supplies 18 constrained occurrence coordinates over the N144
image; it does not add 18 independent matter spins.

Byte identical structure first replay

The v1.0 replay inspects Starbreaker’s own certificates and chooses the
M126 quotient, N144 fiber lift, N144+d sidecar and L162 constrained
readout. Dense H14F fallback is unnecessary. The exact route census is

- N144. Rows: 1,262.

- N144+d. Rows: 17,922.

- total non-extreme. Rows: 19,184.

The replay retains all 2,976 ledgers, 20,415,176 onset events per order
and 35,712 runtime rows. It passes 78/78 checks in 5.251708 seconds, and
fresh runtime output is byte-identical to the completed parent. The
method changes; the Starbreaker grammar and output do not.

Emit only W9 history

The target-local W9 history operator is present across
discovery/validation, both pair maps and both endpoint gauges. Every
branch has cumulative and cross-stage rank 161. Seed equals its
stage-local control; collapse and final retain nonzero cross-stage
memory.

W9HistoryMode is emit-only. It may return the typed history sidecar but
has no authority to change phase-pool grouping, N144 allocation, N144+d
fallback or L162 routing. A scheduling use would require a separate test
and promotion.

For normalized pixel distributions P, Q, the primary statistic is

$$\mathcal A(P,Q)=\sum_{pixel}\sqrt{P_{pixel}Q_{pixel}}$$

Trace free projection correction

CR:CR005f@SAM-RESEARCH freezes one permitted correction. Trace freedom
requires

S_(xx) + S_(yy) + S_(zz) = 0

After the two locked finite differences, the successor sets

S_(zz) := −(S_(xx) + S_(yy))

before observer projection. This changes no source, door, sample count,
grid, view, statistic, null, evidence ladder or physical boundary. It is
an exact algebraic projection with zero fitted parameters, not a relaxed
threshold. CR005f is the correction precommit; the executed retest is
CR005g.

Per carrier ledger control failure

CR:CR005h@21 constructs a complete 53,568-row per-carrier attribution
ledger. Every occurrence receives a unit-occurrence trace-free
quadrupole, individual power, exact leave-one-out aggregate-power
change, bind/escape class and frozen identity. Positive

ΔP = P_(aggregate) − P_(without carrier)

marks reinforcement; negative ΔP marks coherent cancellation.

The candidate content was complete, but exactly three construction
controls failed, so the campaign verdict remains

- FAIL_PER_CARRIER_GW_LEDGER_CONSTRUCTION

The ledger is preserved rather than deleted or tuned to pass.

1.  G02 compares the sealed CR005d verdict against its exact string
    instead of the wrong shortened prefix.

2.  G05 evaluates third-difference linearity inside a frozen roundoff
    envelope and retains an independent integrated-power guard, rather
    than demanding literal floating equality after derivative
    amplification.

3.  G08 first checks static constancy and isotropic cancellation at the
    quadrupole level, then bounds derivative roundoff, rather than
    demanding a literal post-third-difference zero.

The envelope is fixed before the appeal runner:

512 ϵ_(mach) ∥D³∥_(∞)max_(t)∑_(i)|Q_(i)(t)|

Candidate scores, thresholds, histories, flow and every ledger row
remain unchanged. The correction step and the independent replay in §5.3
are separate links. CR005h remains the immutable failed parent after

The two cumulative chains are

- CR005e failure -> CR005f correction precommit -> CR005g executed
  retest

- CR005h control failure -> CR005i bounded correction -> CR005i

Observability and physical boundary

Escape and closure observability boundary

CR:CR005c@21 begins with the frozen three-stage history grammar:

- 101 -> contact, open, returned contact

- 010 -> open, transient contact, open

- 100 -> contact, open, open

- 110 -> contact, contact, open

There are 8,340 history-101 relations and 59,191,245 ending-zero
relations. For every 101 relation, a piecewise-affine Cartesian probe
finds one outward crossing on seed-to-collapse and one inward crossing
on collapse-to-final. Median coordinates are

λ_(release) = 0.566282338

λ_(return) = 1.132859211

with open interval 0.617486748.

These values establish local near-far-near geometry. They do not
establish physical timing because λ is a stage-normalized affine
coordinate and the order λ_(release) < λ_(return) is already encoded by
the 101 bits. The frozen record has no physical solver clock,
independent Home/horizon seal or independently defined release surface.
A pair’s own inward recrossing cannot also be the external closure used
to test its escape.

The open physical joins are therefore explicit:

- phase-to-seconds calibration;

- numerical carrier mass/energy weights;

- lossless T3 open-q to T4 closed-energy mapping;

- independently selected physical mirror and release surface;

- complete-event feedback acceptance;

- scheduler use of W9 history; and

- full RH-aware event semantics.

T6B carries exact dimensionless thermodynamic response through all 2,976
ledgers while preserving the carrier inventory and complete Theta18
address multiset. It does not assign physical energy, temperature,
duration, rate, fusion threshold, numerical A or a physical mirror.
Global SLC authority remains frozen SLCQ2-RZ; bounded Starbreaker
services retain their own typed application lineage beneath that
source-recorded default selection surface.

- 1,920 positive rank-two waves

- 128 exact cancellations

- 0 rank-one cases.

Each cross family is nonzero in 1,024 cases. The interference census is
896 constructive, 1,024 destructive and 128 completely cancelling. The

Live ATOM3D reception and re emission

J1 executes the live Starbreaker source, E/M1 emissions and ATOM3D

E/M1, SO1, J1 and J2 are isolated/non-promoted classical

Starbreaker’s exact formation object retains its native ledgers, atoms,

- Starbreaker formation engine.

Closed receiver cycles retain an exact accumulated excursion

The Starbreaker accumulation experiment computed 768 closed receiver
histories spanning 3,072 shell steps. Every cycle returned to its
initial response action while retaining positive logarithmic variation.
For the EDGE002 forward source at support parameter ρ = 1, the action
alternated between

A⁻ = 1, 834, 312, 320,   A₊ = 4, 227, 734, 656

with exact ratio r = 33, 029, 177/14, 330, 565. The action is the
declared norm of the signed pre-Gram receiver amplitude,
A(k) = x(k)² + y(k)². The numerical history retains packet addresses,
relation, direction, support and ancestry. [SB, p4]

For a positive action sequence, logarithmic rise U sums positive
adjacent logarithmic changes, fall D sums the magnitudes of negative
changes, variation is V = U + D, net change is L = U − D, and maximum M
is measured against the original initial action. The four-shell cycle
A⁻ → A₊ → A⁻ → A₊ → A⁻ has

U = D = 2log r,   V = 4log r,   L = 0,   M = log r

A stationary history at A⁻ shares the endpoint and net change but has
zero variation. Recovery preserves that distinction. A second appended
cycle doubles rise, fall and variation while preserving the original
reference and all maximum occurrences. Independent account and tie-set
reconstruction agreed across the completed source histories; recovery,
append after restart, and source/domain rejection checks passed. The
recorded managed source/test execution took 77.96 seconds, with 819
initial receipts and five further installation receipts. The time
belongs to that declared workload. [SB, pp4,11]

This is a completed computational result: returning state does not force
an empty history account. The retained account supports a later
excursion query and composes with the next cycle.

Two source channels recover all declared three Write histories

The common-receiver construction transmits both signed phase h and axis
occupancy |h| through the same response map T. Occupancy is formed at
the source, so the pair is (Th, T|h|). In general T|h| differs from
|Th|. Taking an absolute value after reception cannot recreate a source
channel that was discarded beforehand. [SB, pp4-6]

The complete fixed-initial-condition census contains 98,304 three-Write
histories in 16,384 families. Ordered observations of both source
channels recover every source order and historical barrier, with zero
order ambiguity. The primary decoder advances through all legal next
Writes using the observed channels and calibrated templates. It does not
receive the true order, barrier or source identifier. A separate decoder
reconstructs with Gaussian-integer phase arithmetic and all
law-generated words.

The comparison identifies the actual gain. The extra source channel

The returned body comprises 393,216 frames and 12,582,912 exact scalar

Signed circulation selects an exact sixteen history Li6 family

The Li-6 candidate grammar has six source addresses and nine directed
relations. Site currents report the signed current entering and leaving
each site. Cycle currents report circulation around the graph’s closed
loops. For edge-current vector z, the two readouts are j = Bz and
c = Cz. The incidence matrix B has rank five, C has rank four, and their
stacked matrix has rank nine. Site and cycle data therefore recover
every edge current exactly, while site currents alone leave a
four-dimensional kernel. [SB, pp5-7]

The source-action comparison uses five motif covers, each assigning
every relation once. Cover 00 has nine pair motifs. The other covers
replace one core triangle’s pair motifs by one three-owner motif.
Circulation readouts favor covers 03 and 04. Without the hidden
depth-loop term, each has action 91/2 and 64 minimizing histories.
Adding the specified loop response selects the same sixteen histories in
both, with action 737/16 = 46.0625 in the catalog observed channel. All
six center placements and 128 support settings preserve that selected
family. The native coefficient channel selects the same covers and
histories at value 969/16. [SB, pp6-7]

The family has a short exact derivation. On the previous 256 contact
minima, the site current is (0, 0, 0, 0, −α, α), where α is a unit
quarter-phase. Writing τ for another unit phase, the depth-square
circulation has squared magnitude |z₀ − 3τ|² in {4, 10, 16}. The
hidden-loop minimum requires z₀ = τ. The remaining unit-phase equations
give, in the declared nine-edge order,

z = (τ, τ, −τ, −τ, −τ, τ, τ, τ, α)

Four choices for τ and four for α give exactly sixteen histories. Their
remaining pair exposures sum to two, the three-owner response is 1/3,
and the hidden contribution is 9/16. Thus the observed-channel action is

$$
\frac{43}{4}\cdot2+72\cdot\frac13+\frac9{16}=\frac{737}{16}$$

The census establishes global minimality within the finite candidate
family; the algebra explains why these states survive. The result
retains both tied covers rather than selecting one displayed history.
[SB, pp6-7]

The primitive computation evaluated 5,563,227 new response values in
1.645563 seconds. H14F direct formulas, independent Ryzen expansions,
and Radeon integer-feature contractions agreed on every value, with
separate stored readback. Factored feature batches and inverse maps
avoided a full Cartesian array while preserving 2,097,152
state/reference rows. The later minimum analysis took 8.437689 seconds
and retained 245,760 cases and 119 distinct minimizing sets. Those
stages and counts have different units and should remain separate. [SB,
pp7,11]

Native learning continues from exact source objects

The GEN3-RXT-R7.1 update completed 36,864 fresh construction families,
1,216 new Starbreaker histories and 288 horizon cases. Of those horizon
cases, 252 formed under the declared horizon model. The family count
excludes 1,024 reused calibration families; the history count includes
128 new calibration histories. Production contributes 32,768 fresh
families and 1,024 histories, and continuation contributes 4,096
families and 64 histories. [SB, pp8-9]

Two exact identities define the new observables. For response vectors
x,y with positive supports a,b, put E_(L) = ‖x‖²/a, E_(R) = ‖y‖²/b and
E_(P) = ‖x − y‖²/(a + b). Their repayment is

$$
E_L+E_R-E_P=\frac{\lVert bx+ay\rVert^2}{ab(a+b)}\ge0$$

For two finite source energies E₃ and E₁₁, the minimum of their sum
equals the sum of their separate minima exactly when their minimizing
sets intersect. The equality states are the entire intersection. GEN3
retains that intersection as a source-bound bitmap, including all ties,
rather than returning only one witness. These identities let a learned
policy select a next source question without replacing the exact
calculation that answers it. [SB, pp7-9]

The imported shared-four-state-orbit policy was correct on all 81,406
families in the reused holdout; the channel-minimum-set policy was
correct on 80,037. These are target-specific results for the pinned
models, not a score assigned to all 45 imported policies.
Distinct-observation selection also retained every recovered history
while saving eight scalar reports on each comparison set: 516→508 on 224
histories and 633→625 on the fresh 256-history confirmation. The latter
required more query nodes, 1,899→2,163. The measured benefit is fewer
reports and exact replay of the saved policy; the source also records
its arithmetic cost. [SB, pp8-10]

The source-action scores and horizon cases retain their computational
meanings. A physical energy or propagation assignment requires the
corresponding measurement map. This leaves the completed inverse, exact
minima, native production and reusable policies intact as defined
results.

Chapter XIII Source Histories and Inverse Reconstruction

From an event address to an executable history

The directional core developed earlier supplies exact phase addresses
and reversible Writes. Its applications must additionally state what
leaves the source, which channels arrive together, what the receiver
records, and which unknowns its inverse is asked to recover. The
completed source-history work turns these questions into explicit
computational experiments. A returning address, a recovered program and
a recovered source preparation are distinct outputs. The examples below
calculate each from its declared observations rather than reading the
answer from source identifiers.

The applied chain begins with Starbreaker’s actual generation-two
packets and the A3D41 contact receiver. It then varies supplied initial
information, simultaneous sources and spatial observations. Finally, it
follows complete accepted-reading histories through declared dynamics.
These applications use the exact inverse and retained arithmetic
interfaces developed elsewhere in this volume, while keeping their own
source maps and executable questions central. Sean Brady supplied the
research direction; the technical maps, derivations and independent
reconstructions are attributed to Codex in the cited completed records.

Full two-channel reception reconstructs the declared source histories
exactly. The mixed and partially observed comparisons quantify which
histories remain indistinguishable under each supplied observation. The
current dynamical constructions use explicit source kinetics and
attempt-time units; their energy and physical propagation maps remain
attached to their own recipients.

Starbreaker s packets retain motion above a returning phase

The completed J4 packet roster supplies 18, 318 incoming histories. Its
three selected relations are the original edges 002, 006 and 084, each
with forward and reverse rays. For incoming address q_(in), the declared
propagation law is
q_(out)(e, d, k) = q_(in) + h_(e) + kv_(e, d) (mod  4)
These are nine-coordinate phase records. The source has 3053 incoming
addresses per relation/direction family, and four shells per incoming
history. The applied R4 calculation expands the frozen translations into
signed unit Writes, executes the retained translation and propagation,
and then performs an explicit inverse-translation reconstruction.
Formation and production of the original J4 response remain the
supplying stages. [V3PJ4]

All 73, 272 shell phase states match the frozen source. Every incoming
address is recovered, and every one of the 18, 318 paths returns to its
starting address after reconstruction while retaining nonzero
accumulated winding. The calculation executes 366, 360 signed native
unit Writes. It reproduces 52, 116 unique raw output addresses and
21, 156 raw address collisions. Relation, ray direction and incoming
ancestry distinguish the colliding histories.

This is an immediately useful example of state and history being
separate computational objects. The returning phase is the current
state; the executed signed lift and winding are retained path
information. Raw endpoint equality therefore leaves meaningful
distinctions in the saved source computation. The unit-Write
decomposition specifies the adapter’s intermediate microsteps. Physical
spin and orbital motion require their own source assignment rather than
being inferred from that decomposition.

Every packet has an authenticated native calculation receipt, with zero
failed or incomplete calls. The recorded 277.202 seconds includes
preparation, current-runtime execution, phase attachment and receipt
writing. There is no matched earlier-engine timing comparison. The
achieved result is exact packet propagation and inverse recovery on the
actual roster, with its full phase and signed-history output available
to later receivers. The complete phase and signed-history outputs can
therefore be received by later operations without reconstructing the
original packet campaign.

One event emits two complementary observations

The common-reception construction starts from two existing native
readouts of one A3D41 event: signed phase h and axis occupancy |h|. It
forms their typed pair at the source, then sends both through the same
J4 response T:
(h, |h|) ↦ diag (T, T)(h, |h|)
The phase advances once. A unit Write rotates its signed component
through the four-phase cycle, while occupancy follows the two-phase axis
quotient. The occupancy channel is computed before reception; taking the
absolute value of the received signed signal gives another observation.
[V3PCOMMON]

For the three relations, write A = X₀₀₂ and B = X₀₀₆, with the exact
template identity X₀₈₄ = −B. The two received components have the form
S = z₀A + (z₁ − z₂)B,   U = a₀A + (a₁ − a₂)B
where z_(j) carries signed phase and a_(j) its axis occupancy. A nonzero
complex minor certifies independence of A, B at each of the 128 declared
response settings. A Write on relation zero is therefore distinguishable
from a Write on either other relation. For relations one and two, equal
signed increments require opposite phases, hence equal parity. Equal
occupancy increments require opposite parities. Both conditions cannot
hold simultaneously. [V3PCOMMONDERIV]

This local calculation explains the completed inverse. Beginning with
the declared initial phase and directions, the decoder compares each
observed change with every remaining legal next Write, keeps all matches
and advances the phase. Its input excludes the actual order, source
address, barrier and source hashes. A separate implementation enumerates
law-generated words using Gaussian-integer phases and reconstructs the
contact profile through the full incidence Gram.

Both channels and their ordered observations recover all 98, 304
declared three-Write histories, their orders, phase paths, contact
profiles and native barriers. Signed reception alone recovers 73, 728
orders and 77, 824 barriers; occupancy alone recovers 49, 152 orders and
65, 536 barriers. Both endpoints without intermediate records recover no
unique orders. Their 6144 barrier recoveries occur where all six orders
already share a barrier. These are controlled comparisons on the same
source families.

The motivating pair makes the missing information tangible. At phase
(0, 2, 0), positive Writes and response setting one, orders 012 and 021
have the same entire signed history. Their contact profiles are
respectively (2, 4, 6, 2) and (2, 4, 4, 2), with barriers four and two.
Their occupancy records differ and the two-channel inverse recovers
them. The construction is an exact discrete receiver; moving emission,
stationary-site encounters and measured propagation/readout are the
identified physical extension maps.

Recovering a program can require less than recovering its source

The information-bridge series enlarges the application from three
distinct Writes to all six signed letters, with repeats, reversals and
changing directions. It includes all sixty-four phase triples, every
word of length one through three and four response settings. The source
executes 16, 512 histories and 46, 464 Writes, producing 66, 048
received histories across those settings. Word length, legal alphabet
and calibration remain supplied. [V3PINFO]

Per response setting, full observations with initial phase and
directions recover all 16, 512 programs, source histories and barriers.
With directions alone, the complete program is still recovered in every
case, but only 16, 288 initial source histories and barriers are unique.
With initial phase alone, 10, 944 programs are recovered. With neither
initial phase nor directions, 8192 programs are unique.
Direction-supplied observations recover every program; removing
direction or initial-phase information gives the smaller exact recovery
counts stated above.

A single negative Write on edge 002 illustrates the separation. Starting
from (0, t, t) for any of four t values gives the same observed event.
Its program is unique while the barrier can be zero or two. The
executable answer should consequently contain a unique Write and a
retained source/barrier candidate set. Returning only one aggregate
success flag would erase this difference.

The complete length-one records contain every transition of the
sixty-four-state graph. At each response setting, 384 transitions reduce
to 360 distinct keys of direction, before-observation and
after-observation. Every key has one signed Write label. Concatenating
valid transitions therefore gives a local word decoder for any finite
walk under those same supplied-direction and full-sampling conditions.
The executed word census remains lengths one through three; the local
composition statement follows from the complete transition table. Missed
events, physical superposition and another alphabet require their own
observation contract.

Superposition and spatial sampling expose different inverse fibers

Two sources writing into the same receiver supply another applied
question. Both initial states and directions are known. The series
compares all nine source-choice pairs in each family, giving 589, 824
mixed received cases. Native vector-current computation supplies the
two-lane sum; its separate recovered-parent and odd-current fields are
excluded from the mixed observation. [V3PINFO]

The mixed signed-plus-occupancy signal uniquely recovers 137, 088 of
147, 456 source-choice pairs per response setting, or 92.96875%, with
maximum answer-set size three. Signed mixing alone recovers 87, 616.
Duplicating the mixed two-lane signal produces exactly the same inverse
partition. Separately observed contributions recover every pair. The
mixed observation recovers most source-choice pairs, while separately
observed contributions recover the complete roster. The additional
information comes from a genuinely distinct source contribution, rather
than another copy of the same scalar output.

The same computational principle appears in a spatial inverse that
directly uses the declared radial accumulation law a(r) = M( < r)/r. The
source roster contains 141 distributions of six units among six shells,
with each shell amount zero, one or two. Six receivers between
successive shells recover all distributions. Multiplying each reading by
its radius gives enclosed amounts; differences return the shell
populations. Reading only after shells one, three and six leaves fifteen
classes and only two unique distributions. The outer reading is always
12/13, so one outer reading or six copies of it retains one class of all
141 sources. The six distinct radial readings reconstruct the full
source; the fixed-total outer reading cannot distinguish any of the 141
distributions.

For the six directions ±x, ±y, ±z, total M, signed dipole D and
trace-free diagonal quadrupole Q also recover every source.
Opposite-pair totals obey P_(k) = (Q_(k) + M)/3, and the directed
populations are (P_(k) ± D_(k))/2. Omitting signed quadrupole or dipole
leaves larger inverse fibers. The powers (M², ∑D², ∑Q²) give only nine
classes and one unique distribution. They even merge two geometrically
distinct opposite-pair multisets: (0, 0), (1, 2), (1, 2) and
(0, 1), (0, 1), (2, 2) share powers (36, 2, 54). Full signed moments
preserve the distinction. This is a completed angular-information
application of the exact receiver, with no temperature-spectrum or
cosmological parameter assignment added.

From source factors to declared temporal dynamics

A static source density does not by itself select an attempted-event
clock. The shared dynamics applications make that kinetic completion
explicit. For positive source weights w_(i), bit-attempt rate ν_(b) and
departure factor c_(i), a neighbouring flip i → j has rate
$$Q_{ji}=\nu_b c_i\frac{w_j}{w_i+w_j},
 \qquad \pi_i\propto w_i/c_i$$
The diagonal is minus the outgoing column sum. Ordered readout masks act
on the unnormalized accepted substate; normalization occurs only when
its full accepted-history probability is known. The endpoint without
intervening masks is retained separately. [V3PDYNAMICS]
[V3PDYNAMICSGUIDE]

Three shared operations supply the generator, ordered readout and
projected memory. SB uses its exact sixteen-boundary-state source
density at inverse temperature 1/100, unit boundary attempts, unit
departure clocks and all-minus preparation. Three equally spaced
observations require coordinate zero to remain negative. Full history
acceptance is 0.6382671065; the two-state memoryless approximation gives
0.6322980041. Their interval difference excludes zero, and the tested
partition is certified nonlumpable.

For a declared coarse map C, lift R with CR = I and hidden remainder r₀,
projection has the exact form
$$\begin{aligned}
 A_c=CQR,\quad D=(I-RC)Q|_{\ker C},\\
 B=(I-RC)QR,\\
 M(t)=CQe^{tD}B,\quad I(t)=CQe^{tD}r_0,\\
 \dot v=A_cv+I(t)+\int_0^tM(t-u)v(u)\,du\end{aligned}$$
The full generator is Q, with D acting on the retained hidden
complement. The installed operation returns both sampled memory kernel
and initial-memory forcing. The applied conclusion is specific: the
visible bit alone omits information needed by the next accepted
observation. This sixteen-state boundary model is an explicit kinetic
source, rather than a newly solved trajectory of every microscopic N100
spin.

The completed Starbreaker extension through five thousand sites
