The larger SB study uses frozen exact polynomial factors to calculate
the boundary weights. For boundary b,
P_(b)(x, y) = R_(b)(x, y)∏_(j)F_(j)(x, y)^(a_(j))
The variable x counts up-spins, while y carries the losslessly
compressed correction index. At inverse temperature 1/N, substituting
the exact exponential correction and multiplying Arb polynomials gives
coefficients A_(b)(k). The partition is a complete finite sum over
k = 0, …, N, with the original quadratic magnetization factor. Both
original encodings retain their source and plan identities. Common
exponent shifts are restored in exported absolute logarithms and cancel
only in the specified boundary ratios. [V3PSBEXT]

This source-factor route avoids requiring absent historical bulk shards.
Eighteen qualification cases compare the factor contraction with
expanded exact polynomial summation at N = 12 and retained certified
partitions at N = 100, 500. The new anchors N = 2000, 3000, 4000, 5000
produce twenty-four additional cases; the combined study contains 409
sizes and 2454 cases. No magnetization sector is cut. Every new
full-minus-memoryless accepted-history interval is strictly positive,
and every tested two-state partition remains certified nonlumpable.

At N = 5000, positive-fill full acceptance is about 64.1593-64.1757%,
while negative fill gives 63.2314-63.2392%. The memory contributions, in
percentage points, are
$$\begin{array}{l|rr}
 \text{Source family}&+1\ \text{fill}&-1\ \text{fill}\\\hline
 \text{packet}&0.933746&0.017661\\
 \text{signed packet}&0.919123&0.009812\\
 \text{signed chain}&0.918002&0.009810
\end{array}$$
All six acceptance curves and memory differences decline from the prior
N = 1800 anchor. Empirical slopes over N = 2000-5000 range approximately
−0.464 to −0.474 for positive fill and −1.0003 to −1.0007 for negative
fill. These are measured finite-range slopes; an asymptotic law remains
the source-defined next derivation. The completed roster therefore
retains measurable source-dependent boundary memory through N = 5000
under the declared finite-source dynamics.

The application sequence now gives Volume III several finished objects:
exact returning packets with retained winding, a two-channel local
inverse, source and program inverse fibers, spatial distribution
inverses, complete ordered accepted-state evolution, and source-factor
dynamics that retain quantified memory. Each makes an executable
question concrete. Their useful reuse depends on carrying the particular
observations, source preparation and clock with the result, so another
consumer can apply the completed computation without changing what it
answers.

Chapter XIV Exact Values Certified Functions and Retained Composition

The early exact-state and language chapters establish a grammar whose
accepted operations preserve their mathematical meanings. The later
native runtime turns that foundation into a shared research system. An
exact source can become a certified logarithm, a complete spectral
factorization can enter a new matter calculation, and a returned matrix
can become an operand of another registered operation. The computation
retains the source, basis, error account and derivation needed to
interpret each result. This chapter develops that working mathematical
architecture through the completed logarithm, capacity and
retained-input constructions. [V3R01] [V3R02]

The historical language candidates and Q2 selector retain their original
dates and roles. The source snapshot used here selects SLC-GEN5-R1 with
CE SLC-GEN5-CEV1-R1 and generation G2-SPIN-CLOCK. Older GEN2 and GEN3
operation prefixes remain supported calls, rather than separate current
global engines. The current revision records shared precision from 64
through 4096 bits, general complex matrices through dimension 2048 and
supported Hermitian logarithms through 512. These bounds are admission
capacities. Individual thermal, polynomial and augmented-response
algorithms retain their own smaller envelopes. [V3R01] [V3R03]

Three mathematical forms of a returned value

An exact rational, an exact finite logarithmic expression and a
certified interval answer different questions. Rational arithmetic can
return 7/3 − 4/3 = 1 without approximation. A logarithmic calculation
can retain log 2 as an exact expression even though a finite decimal
representation is unavailable. A numerical consumer can then enclose
that expression between exact rational endpoints at a declared working
precision. The interval is the returned numerical object; its width
records the remaining certified uncertainty.

The native scalar contract accepts integers and exact rational or
decimal strings. Interval endpoints are exact lower and upper values.
Binary floating-point operands are not silently promoted into scientific
exactness. Administrative host timing has a separately typed finite
representation and does not supply mathematical source data. This
separation lets the same engine manage deadlines while preserving the
exact scalar semantics of its calculations. [V3R02]

A GEN5 logarithmic expression is a rational-weighted sum
ℒ = c + ∑_(j)q_(j)log r_(j),   r_(j) > 0,  q_(j) ∈ ℚ
Arguments can remain factored powers. An exponent such as 10¹⁸ need not
be expanded into the corresponding enormous integer before its logarithm
is represented. Canonical factor cancellation gives
log 12 − log 3 − 2log 2 = 0
as an exact normalized result. Dimensioned inputs provide a compatible
positive reference, so logarithms act on dimensionless ratios with their
source meaning attached. [V3R02]

Signed combinations preserve cancellation before numerical
exponentiation. Equal positive and negative contributions can disappear
exactly; small remaining differences use stable source operations such
as expm1. Increasing precision then improves the arithmetic enclosure of
the surviving expression. It does not remove a declared source radius,
alter a physical unit or infer a missing reference.

This distinction appears directly in the completed precision controls.
The normalized expression log 4 − 2log 2 cancels exactly at 64 bits. The
positive quantity log (1 + 2⁻⁴⁰⁰) is also certified at 64 bits through
the stable logarithm-one-plus path. The apparent number of decimal zeros
in an input is consequently an incomplete guide to the arithmetic work.
Algebraic structure, cancellation and the requested observable determine
the useful route. [V3R04]

A source scalar and a spectral profile are different logarithms

The certified GEN3 source-log construction makes the target explicit.
Its registered mean/cut source admits an ordered rational vector
a₀, …, a_(s − 1). Put M = ∑_(i)a_(i). The source quantity is
$$Q=\frac{M^2}{s}
 +\sum_{r=1}^{s-1}
 \frac{\left(\sum_{i<r}a_i-rM/s\right)^2}{r(s-r)}$$
The native record’s mean field retains the signed sum M. Its name is not
an instruction to replace M by M/s. [V3R05]

The same registered kernel has exact eigenvalue rule
$$\lambda_0=1,\qquad
 \lambda_k=\frac1{k(k+1)}\quad(k\geq1)$$
Projection onto its polynomial modes gives weights
w_(k) = A_(k)²/∥p_(k)∥². The scalar target and spectral target are
log (1 + Q),   ∑_(k)w_(k)log (1 + τλ_(k))
They are separately typed readouts, and neither is a log determinant.
For the source (1, −1) and τ = 1, the complete spectral expression is
2log (3/2), while the scalar expression is log 2. The difference is
mathematical content, not numerical disagreement. [V3R05]

After modes through m have been retained, the exact residual mass is
$$R_m=\sum_i a_i^2-\sum_{k=0}^m w_k$$
It bounds remaining source energy by R_(m)λ_(m + 1). Full degree has
zero tail; a negative residual is rejected. Adaptive refinement adds
missing modes instead of recomputing the retained projections. New
values of τ reuse the same source modes. A direct source-cut route
separately evaluates complete Q without requesting spectral projections.

This eigenvalue rule belongs to the admitted mean/cut kernel. It is not
imposed on an arbitrary exchange matrix because both happen to possess
spectra. The source contract retains basis, dimensionless units,
reference and ordered history. The logarithm consequently acts on a
specified source quantity, while other operators use their own
registered spectral definitions. [V3R05]

Numerical log evaluation uses rational range reduction and an
explicit-tail rational atanh series. A tolerance-met return, a valid
budget-limited enclosure and an exact symbolic expression remain
distinct result states. Exhausting a term or mode budget retains the
last valid certificate. An overlapping interval does not become an exact
equality or ordering. These behaviors give a later consumer enough
information to choose whether it needs more arithmetic, more source
modes or merely the existing expression. [V3R05]

Operator functions retain support and order

A matrix logarithm requires its operator domain. The installed supported
Hermitian path handles positive support with an exact zero kernel,
retaining the projector and zero-extending the supported logarithm. An
uncertain kernel remains unresolved. A negative-support input is not
reinterpreted as a positive operator. The older modular call returns
−log , while ordinary GEN5 operator logarithms return +log ; the
convention is part of the operation identity. [V3R06]

The completed logarithm expansion found a useful algorithmic change. The
previous sixteen-state method returned unresolved spectra for a fully
coupled positive repeated-mode source and repeated noncoordinate kernel
blocks, although analytical projectors gave their exact spectra. The
successor certifies a complete positive spectral window without
separating every repeated eigenvalue.

A positive Gershgorin lower bound can supply that window directly.
Otherwise directed LDL pivots establish positivity, and a validated
inverse row-norm bound U gives
λ_(min) ≥ U⁻¹
The existing series then uses the complete spectral window and its tail.
Certifying strict positivity before symbolic rank elimination also
avoids unnecessary kernel work. Repeated modes retain their complete
multiplicity without requiring a separately resolved eigenbasis. [V3R07]

The source-defined gain was visible before the larger capacity change.
An initial 64-state attempt spent more than 159 seconds in symbolic rank
work and was interrupted. The completed positive-window route took 1.306
seconds for its dense 64-state call. The matched 128-state candidate
took 5.939 seconds. These timings belong to the declared nine-family,
256-bit, one-thread comparison, including its native return boundary.
They identify the practical value of a better certificate rather than a
general dimension-independent speed claim. [V3R07]

The qualification retains noncoordinate rank-one support, repeated
kernel blocks, small positive modes, finite term exhaustion and
irreducible source widths. Its meaningful failed and unresolved cases
remain beside the successful certificates. Independent rational
scalar-log/projector references check every emitted matrix entry. The
resulting certificates enclose every emitted matrix entry against the
separately arranged rational scalar-log and projector references.
[V3R07]

Ordered operator response uses the same distinction between a full
operator and a scalar summary. For
A(t) = A₀ + tA₁ + t²A₂ + ⋯
an ordered log jet retains all admitted matrix words. A Fréchet
direction need not commute with A₀. The calculation differentiates the
actual operator function rather than substituting a scalar derivative of
a selected eigenvalue. Formal derivative order remains separate from a
finite-displacement Taylor remainder, which needs its declared source
control. [V3R02]

Capacity is qualified at a specified workload

The capacity extension increased the shared range from 64-1024 to
64-4096 bits, general matrices from 512 to 2048, and supported
logarithms from 128 to 512. One native limits definition governs
admission, discovery and ordinary refinement. Thermal compatibility
retains its matrix-128/log-16 contract; GEN5 thermal jets must fit their
complete augmented operator within dimension 512. A large base matrix
and a high derivative order cannot each consume the full bound
independently. [V3R03]

The completed dense matrix fixture uses A = I + J in dimension 2048 and
a source vector with zero sum, so Ab = b. Every matrix entry is nonzero,
and all 4096 returned real/imaginary components were checked. The
supported logarithm fixture uses dimension n = 512 and
$$A=2I+\frac{J}{8n},\qquad P=\frac Jn,\quad P^2=P$$
Its exact operator identity is
log A = (log 2)I + log (17/16)P
Every matrix entry and the full trace enclosed the independent
reference. The large dimensions were qualified at 128 bits; 4096-bit
scalar and small-matrix work was qualified separately. Their combination
is an admission capacity, not a measurement of every possible large,
high-precision workload. [V3R03]

The same extension reused a completed N = 10¹⁶ Gibbs source for
order-two boundary response at 4096 bits with zero repeated original
Gibbs integrations. Its original pressure uncertainty remained in the
output. More arithmetic headroom improved the downstream numerical work
without recasting the retained source as newly integrated at that
precision. This gives capacity a useful scientific interpretation: it
expands which subsequent calculations a completed source can support.

The measured dense 2048 product took 26.303637 seconds, and the dense
512 supported-log case took 46.675142 seconds at their stated settings.
Complete dense output preserves every entry, so storage and
exact-certificate work grow with matrix dimension squared and precision.
The runtime’s compact spectral routes address that output problem when a
complete factor representation answers the research question more
efficiently. [V3R03]

A completed result becomes a native operand

Retained mathematics makes reuse part of the execution semantics. An
object carries a kind, complete source binding, mathematical value,
provenance and dependencies. Discovery can list a read-only collection
without loading all coefficients. Selecting a reference requires an
exact source match. Adopting that reference brings its dependency
closure into the ordinary session store; the new calculation can then
consume it recursively inside a matrix, polynomial or symbolic payload.
[V3R08]

The eleven retained-result operations separate discovery, selection,
retrieval, publication, execution/reuse, moments, factor comparison,
symbolic composition, dependencies, export and import. Publishing a
caller-supplied exact operand identifies its source; it is distinct from
obtaining a newly executed mathematical answer. Export carries the
chosen roots and their derivation closure. Import authenticates the
supplied closure without repeating arithmetic, while preserving its
original evidence.

Execution reuse is keyed by the complete scientific payload and exact
implementation identity, apart from its immutable output name. A
different source, operand, parameter or implementation is a new
mathematical request. Conversely, a repeated consumer can receive the
same completed calculation under a separately retained consumer lineage.
This prevents a file name or friendly label from becoming the
mathematical equivalence rule. [V3R08]

The completed collection contains 1441 typed objects. It includes
exchange levels L = 2, 3, 4, 8, 10, 12, 13, 14, 846 distinct
larger-level integer polynomials, all 165 retained Li7 T/K pairs and 32
symbolic quartic classes. Missing levels 5, 6, 7, 9, 11 remain absent
from that seed collection. New research can publish further admitted
objects through the same interface without treating an index gap as a
previously solved spectrum. [V3R09]

The associated L14 composition demonstrates the mathematical
consequence. Each local quartic family closes 268, 435, 456 states over
47 partitions, represented by 90,772 factor/weight terms. The batch
composed all 32 families using the retained exchange and local
operators. It performed zero new exchange solves and zero new quartic
solves. A complete local-pair/collective composition took 1.235260
seconds; the 32-family composition calls took 36.009 seconds. [V3R09]

Those are symbolic spectra retaining J, μ, g and the optional collective
ν. Equal all-to-all unordered-pair coupling is explicit; a physical
energy scale is a separate source assignment. Each algebraic root’s
multiplicity is its factor multiplicity times Schur-weight multiplicity,
with coincident specialized energies added. Compact references avoid
expanded polynomial-degree limits and repeated coefficient
materialization.

Exact moments and factor comparison become inexpensive receiving
questions on that retained object. The L14 normalized moment sequence
begins 1, 7/2, 203/8, 1393/8; the L12/L14 comparison finds 23 shared
factors while retaining each level’s multiplicities. Fresh-process
recovery returned the L14 composition with composition and moment
arithmetic disabled. The acquired mathematical object, rather than a
report about it, was available to the new consumer. [V3R09]

From a spectrum to a complete receiving calculation

The six cooperating GEN5 kernel families share exact expressions,
operator functions, thermal response, histories/protocols,
source/receiver composition and retained refinement. They use one matrix
wire and ordinary source-bound references. A finite partition
calculation therefore receives all declared degeneracies,
Z = ∑_(r)g_(r)exp  (−βE_(r) + ∑_(a)f_(a)Q_(ra))
while a noncommuting block source receives its full Hermitian exponent
and direction. A retained complete Gibbs source supplies its absolute
log Z = N pressure and boundary response without replacing normalization
by boundary ratios. [V3R02]

Complete spectral factors can likewise supply a partition sum after
real-root isolation and multiplicity reconstruction. Exact preparation
can survive numerical refinement while only arithmetic precision
changes. These paths connect the dense exact-state foundations to modern
finite thermal and operator calculations. The source remains the same
mathematical object through each change of representation.

The architecture also distinguishes a numerical bound from a physical
interpretation. A supported log, entropy, source action or complete
thermal derivative answers its registered question. It receives a
physical energy, clock or matter meaning only through the corresponding
source and reference map. This is how Volume III carries the
constructive mathematical results of the first two volumes forward: it
preserves their actual operands and makes subsequent operations
composable, rather than substituting one recurring number or matrix
dimension for a complete source definition. [V3R02] [V3R08]

Chapter XV Executable Methods and Learned Questions

The next computational development concerns how a problem selects its
calculation. A retained spectrum can support several equivalent methods;
a numerical goal can admit several working precisions; and a
hidden-source question can be answered through different permitted
probes. The completed SLC work makes these choices explicit mathematical
objects. A method is an executable checked graph. A precision policy
proposes work and then faces the actual certificate. An investigative
query is selected from its measured observation partition and retained
for later use. These constructions extend the earlier formal language
and ordinal-selection chapters through actual native execution. [V3R10]
[V3R04] [V3R11]

A method library contains executable definitions

The installed GEN5 mathematical library initially supplied 34
parameterized recipes. Its six operations discover, admit, compile,
execute, fit and select methods. A recipe declares exact parameters,
applicability assumptions, source namespace, operation dependencies,
checks and a returned observable. Compilation binds it to a source
instance; execution follows the admitted graph and retains each returned
dependency. Merely admitting a definition does not certify every
possible instance. [V3R10] [V3R12]

The recipe language permits up to 32 parameters, 64 steps and 128
checks. An input can be an exact rational, interval, matrix, polynomial,
logarithmic expression or retained object. Step outputs can feed later
steps through explicit field references. Forward references, cycles and
undeclared placeholders are rejected. There is no dynamic source-code
evaluation inside a recipe. The mathematical content consists of
installed native operations with their existing source, basis and
precision contracts. [V3R12]

Applicability is executable. A matrix can be required to be square, two
parameters to have the same shape, or a scalar to be positive. A failed
assumption returns an inapplicable instance before mathematical steps
execute. A failed later check retains the completed dependencies and the
explicit failure. This gives the caller a concrete account of which part
of a proposed route was actually applicable.

Checks also preserve their mathematical distinctions. Complete exact
equality compares canonical exact values. Interval containment checks
that a target lies in an enclosure. Successful native admission records
the operation’s contract; it is different evidence from an independently
checked algebraic identity. Every returned step has explicit coverage. A
successful instance therefore returns a selected mathematical output
together with the checks that give that output its stated role. [V3R12]

The catalogue covers exact log cancellation and ratios, operator
determinants and responses, classical and quantum information, complex
Grams and four-point Fourier action, finite spin partition functions and
cumulants, complete factor spectra and failure/reset protocols. A custom
symbolic recipe also executed the independent coefficient identity
(x − 1)(x − 2 − g) = x² − (3 + g)x + (2 + g)
Its legacy mathematical stage used the same retained-result path
developed in the preceding chapter. [V3R10]

Source indexing and implemented mathematics have different scopes

The library’s pinned source index covers 372 OpenAI result families and
719 manuscripts. That index preserves family/manuscript relationships,
author attribution, source availability, formalization metadata and
licenses. It is broader than the executable recipe set. Five donor
families received focused source review; four supplied concrete finite
transfers. [V3R10]

Those transfers are specific. Family 107 supplies a tensor Gram identity
and native matrix compositions. Family 130 supplies an exact four-point
Fourier transform over ℚ(i) with its full orthogonality identity. Family
221 supplies finite spin partition, normalized-weight and
directional-cumulant calculations. Family 234 supplies the normalized
finite pressure
N⁻¹(log Z − Nlog 2)
under the exact state-count condition dim  = 2^(N). Their original
assumptions remain attached to their source definitions. The finite
implementations do not inherit every asymptotic or thermodynamic
statement from their donor papers.

Family 109 remains reviewed source material for integer multiplication
and precision refinement; its all-length multiplication algorithm is not
an installed recipe in this result. The index also does not mean all 719
manuscripts were downloaded or every formalization was executed. The
report explicitly records local Lean execution as not run. These
distinctions locate the completed computational transfer precisely while
keeping the larger source collection available for future method
development. [V3R10]

Namespace transfer is equally explicit. A SAM_land method can enter a
STANDARD problem through a declared method-only mapping naming the donor
and recipient interpretation. The compiler inspects the complete adopted
dependency closure, including retained parameters and recipe defaults.
The mapping transfers the specified method; it does not silently rename
the donor’s physical law or source assumptions. A learned selector
crossing the same namespaces needs its corresponding explicit model
mapping. [V3R12]

Learning selects equivalent executed routes

The native method learner receives pairs of successful execution traces
for one exact problem. Their selected outputs must be canonically equal.
Problem identity includes task type, normalized inputs, source binding
and precision. Training, development and reserved splits retain whole
problems, so the same source instance cannot contribute examples to both
fitting and reserved evaluation. [V3R12]

The default target is native operation count. A measured elapsed-time
target is also available, with its execution conditions retained. Cost
ties do not manufacture winner labels. Features describe mathematical
structure: dimensions, nonzero and negative counts, rational bit
lengths, operation types, assumptions and retained-input counts. They do
not contain the mathematical answer. Exact class-balanced CART fits the
method comparison and uses development data to select its bounded tree
depth.

Selection compiles feasible candidates, ranks their ordered pairs and
preserves rational score ties. It returns compiled methods; their
execution remains a subsequent native operation on the new source.
Imported models are advisory. A model package preserves its native
record, feature schema, training dependencies and objective, while a
newly selected result still undergoes the current recipe checks. [V3R12]

The actual 54-preparation frustrated-spin example supplies a concrete
lesson. Its normalized source rows form a 54 × 18 batch, and its common
receiver is an 18 × 34 map. All 1836 returned receiver intervals contain
the independently retained exact endpoints, and the complete output
matches the earlier native CPU result at 1024 bits. The original Gibbs
integrations were not repeated. [V3R10]

Two recipes calculate that same observable. The direct route uses one
product; the alternate reconstructs the receiver through two adjoints
and then multiplies, using three operations. The lesson separates six
training, twelve development and thirty-six reserved preparations. The
learned selector chooses the direct route for the complete batch. That
full batch is a new batch shape assembled from the original
preparations; the thirty-six-preparation subbatch is the reserved source
split. The result is a learned execution choice with a measured cost
difference, rather than retrieval of a previously labeled answer.
[V3R10]

The later spin/clock installation adds five executable compositions:
source projection, hidden residual map, residual payment, generalized
hidden-direction projectors and fifth-coordinate lower response. It
brings the recorded method count to 39 and native operation count to 254
in the G2-SPIN-CLOCK generation. The earlier 34-method/248-operation
report remains its historical installation snapshot. Both describe
completed stages of the same retained architecture. [V3S11]

Adaptive precision proposes work the certificate accepts it

Precision learning has a related but distinct target. A case names an
installed numerical operation or compiled method and an executable width
or sign goal. The controller proposes a starting precision, executes
native trials and checks the returned complete enclosure. Failure can
trigger higher precision. The learned choice never replaces the
mathematical acceptance test. [V3R04] [V3R14]

For selected intervals I_(j) = [l_(j), u_(j)], an additional declared
output radius e and target width w, acceptance is
max_(j)(u_(j) − l_(j) + 2e) ≤ w
A matrix path checks every real and imaginary component. Source radii
already present in the native return stay present; the separate
additional radius is paid before acceptance. Positive, negative, nonzero
and exact-zero goals operate on the selected real interval under their
own native criteria. [V3R04]

The default ladder starts at the installed minimum and doubles to the
declared maximum. Explicit starts override advice. An exact successful
case can remember its cheapest observed successful precision or elapsed
cost. The word observed matters: an untested lower precision is not
reported as a measured optimum. Models accelerate unfamiliar cases,
while checked case memory can reuse what a particular calculation
already established. [V3R14]

The completed lesson trained on ten one-log cases and four development
cases. Their executions supplied forty witnessed rows, and development
selected a depth-one native tree. Six reserved cases used two-log signed
expressions with coefficient scales and accuracy goals absent from
training. The baseline required eighteen numerical trials; the learned
policy required six, reaching the baseline’s first successful ladder
point in every case. Fresh stores kept the comparison free of
preexisting target results. The same imported model repeated
eighteen-to-six on the qualified pod. [V3R04]

The primary matched-work result is a 67 percent reduction in numerical
trials. Recorded local execution-loop totals were 338.723 milliseconds
and 130.001 milliseconds, excluding planning, transfer and cold return.
The report retains one exceptional development witness; certification
still determines every future acceptance. The saved proposal policy
reduces numerical trials under the declared matched-work protocol, while
the native certificate decides each acceptance. [V3R04]

Several controls show what precision learning must distinguish. A
declared source radius of 10⁻⁶ against a 2⁻⁸⁰ width goal returns
source-refinement-required with zero arithmetic trials. A retained
interval [1, 2] cannot be narrowed to a point by increasing arithmetic
precision; its observed width plateau requests source or truncation
review. A goal beyond the policy ceiling returns its unresolved ceiling
status. An initially unresolved nearly singular exact solve, by
contrast, succeeds after escalation to 256 bits.

Imported execution traces do not become recipient-local training
witnesses. The recipient must execute the case locally to obtain its
runtime-owned marker. This prevents a supplied or rehashed provenance
string from fabricating measured labels. Mathematical reuse and evidence
transfer remain useful, but they retain the distinction between
possessing a completed result and witnessing a new local training
outcome. [V3R14]

Exact inverse reconstruction preserves the requested target

The earlier native inverse release provides another form of
computational intelligence: choose an observation that resolves the
property actually requested. Its source relation retains every
compatible ordered history. A receiver frame or difference may leave
several states compatible, and the planner evaluates permitted probes
using exact weighted information. Applied observations refine the
original relation instead of replacing its reconstruction target.
[V3R15]

In the declared unknown-phase fixture, the original observation leaves
sixteen histories. For initial-state reconstruction, the selected probes
reduce the relation
16 → 4 → 1
For the original action ratio, history counts follow the same sequence
while ratio alternatives reduce 8 → 2 → 1. The original barrier is known
after the first probe, even though four histories remain. Its next plan
returns no gain. Knowing the requested property and knowing the whole
source history are different stopping conditions. [V3R15]

Exact information uses the declared probability measure. Binary, uneven
and three-way tests retain rational weights and logarithmic terms such
as log 3. These information quantities remain distinct from
source-action ratios. Source restrictions and unavailable probes also
remain explicit; an unavailable observation does not remove a compatible
source from the relation.

The completed qualification passes 55,917 assertions, covering 384
original source members, 2304 probe extensions, equal-observation
fibers, variant receivers, short ordered histories and guards. Appending
a probe preserves the original target even when the extended history’s
barrier changes. A resumed session retains its full sealed decision
plan, alternative predictions and all maximizing ties. The retained
decision plan preserves the original target and all maximizing query
ties across fresh execution and recovery. [V3R15]

This inverse machinery is a completed finite method under its declared
source and observation contract. It supplies a useful basis for learned
investigative queries: the learner can compare the partitions generated
by permitted actions, count their costs and retain a method whose
information matches the original question.

A learned query changes how information is gathered

The unfamiliar-query lesson starts from sixteen hidden phase contexts
and two primitive Writes, A and B. Each query reports one selected
value: a signed receiver difference, its sign, or one of the native
logarithmic history channels. Full source frames remain in receipts and
are not policy observations. The objective orders correct resolution
first, readout count second and Write count third. [V3R11]

The first attempt is the optimal adaptive tree over fourteen primitive
queries. The learner then enumerates words of lengths two through four,
with the same seven readouts and primitive continuations where needed.
Its 196 generated methods plus the incumbent give 197 candidates. The
selected AB:DELTA query performs two Writes before returning one
combined receiver difference. BA:DELTA is the other exact best tie.

The selected partition resolves all sixteen training contexts. Readouts
fall from 28 to 16 while Writes rise from 28 to 32. After freezing, the
same method transfers to three withheld direction bindings: all 48 fresh
cases are correct, with 48 rather than 84 readouts and 96 rather than 84
Writes. New method searches are zero. The improvement changes how
information is collected; it does not make every cost decrease. [V3R11]

The broader clock-history lesson acquires four additional variation
queries and grows its library from six to ten methods. Training resolves
128 cases with 168 rather than 220 readouts; development uses 68 rather
than 80. In final longer-history transfer, however, a primitive
variation query already resolves every case in one report. The learner
selects that simpler option and records no advantage over the primitive
policy. The successful method remains useful without assigning a gain to
a source where the gain was absent. [V3R16]

Fresh known-source recovery restores the acquired decision machine and
reconstructs 32 histories with 43 readouts, with zero new model rows,
policy planning or method searches. A separate declared-question bundle
answers 64 questions with 32 probes instead of 40 sequential probes. The
joint history target supplies the narrower state, ratio and barrier
answers under the same source contract. Both results retain exact
question/transcript bindings and their corrected completion records.
[V3R16] [V3R17]

The division of contributions is explicit. Codex supplied the task
mapping, legal query grammar and generic finite search; native source
executions supplied every candidate’s observation partition and exact
cost. These experiments learn and reuse a method. They do not measure an
autonomous change of the search algorithm or assign physical seconds to
computational resets and Writes. [V3R11]

Persistent histories and language retain their own boundaries

The modern persistent-history kernel retains occurrence identities and
every directed event, rather than only a final total. Range queries and
edits preserve U, D, V, net change, maxima and all ties. Point edits
provide explicit replacement incident edges, and the earlier tree root
remains usable. Gaps remain gaps; the consumer can request complete
positive segments rather than silently joining unrelated runs. [V3R02]

Its logarithmic algorithmic work has a specific meaning. Range and edit
paths use O(log N) tree work, with exact integer arithmetic, output and
export counted separately. Repeated finite protocols use binary powering
with O(log n) ordered matrix compositions at fixed state dimension and
derivative order. Those protocols retain success, failure, reset, record
and recipient channels, whose complete transition closes stochastically.
A survival result keeps its accepted mass and declared source clock. The
logarithmic work count is not a claim that every matrix or source
integral has logarithmic arithmetic complexity. [V3R02]

Native language is another typed interface to this computational system.
The later compiled operations parse sourced concepts, sentence frames,
argument fragments and declared arithmetic. For basic arithmetic, the
language kernel builds the operand graph and the existing exact
operation evaluates it; a conversational wrapper does not become a
replacement calculator. Source meanings retain their document identity
and page, while runtime status comes from current authority. [V3R18]

Its admitted capacities and historical lessons have their own scope. The
installed code contains indexed-library and streamed-training
operations, typed frames and conversation state. Those capacities are
not counts of current learned data. The live conversation authority
records that chat, its sustained training programme, saved models and
designated data were owner-withdrawn; the services are stopped and
guarded against restart. Shared native operation code remains installed.
This edition presents the mathematical and language contracts as
retained computational work and preserves that current removal status.
[V3R19]

Across methods, precision and questions, the completed pattern is
consistent. Source identity and exact mathematical outputs make
alternate routes comparable. Witnessed costs make a particular route
choice learnable. A retained graph, model or decision plan makes that
choice reusable. Actual execution and its source-defined certificate
still determine the returned result. Volume III thus adds a constructive
account of method acquisition to the exact-state foundation while
retaining every important distinction among a formal language, a learned
selector, a numerical certificate and a physical source interpretation.

Chapter XVI Exact Exchange and Symbolic Matter

A local source becomes an interacting exact object

The exchange program constructed a continuous route from retained Li7
source operators to finite interacting chains and then returned their
spectra to symbolic matter calculations. Its completed outputs include
nineteen arrangement classes, a fully matched finite polynomial Bethe
benchmark, a complete length-thirteen exchange spectrum accounting for
67,108,864 states, and composition with thirty-two local-matter families
in 2.011662 seconds of recorded assembly. The assembly reused completed
exchange factors and local quartics. [EX, pp2-10]

The local exchange space V has four states. Chain length L counts copies
of this four-state factor; it is distinct from isotope mass number,
Ising spin count and Starbreaker layer count. T is the retained local
return operator, K the signed source-role interaction, and Q(g) = T + gK
their symbolic local family. On the chain, P exchanges two factors, and
the periodic exchange Hamiltonian is
$H_{\mathrm{ex}}=\sum_{j=1}^L P_{j,j+1}$. Independent parameters J, μ, g
and later ν remain symbolic.

The signed interaction uses inherited source incidence vectors d and
their diagonal component matrices D^(a):

$$
K=\sum_{a=1}^{3}D^aTD^a$$

All thirty-five assignments of three proton roles and four neutron roles
produce nineteen exact K spectra and nineteen exact T + K spectra. The
classes are the sixteen reversal pairs and three palindromic singletons.
Equal species counts or equal counts of unlike neighbors do not
determine the result. The source-role operator reads additional
arrangement information. Species erasure sends K to zero, and sign
erasure changes its spectrum. [EX, p2]

The full coupling calculation constructs 35 × 48 = 1, 680 four-state
component polynomials det (qI − T − gK). Five exact coupling evaluations
determine each degree-at-most-four coupling dependence, and every
coefficient array agrees with an independent determinant expansion over
all twenty-four permutations. Comparing complete 192-state
characteristic polynomials gives equality only at g = 0 between distinct
arrangement classes. The nineteen whole-spectrum classes therefore
remain distinct for every nonzero coupling. This concerns equality of
entire spectra; it does not exclude individual level crossings. [EX, p3]

The canonical quartic retains the actual source matrix

In the canonical component, T has diagonal entries 84 and off-diagonal
entries 8 on the path 2-1-3-4. K has diagonal (672, 2016, 672, 672),
with path entries 96,32,32. After ordering along that path and
subtracting (84 + 672g)I, define

a = 8 + 96g,   b = 8 + 32g,   h = 1344g,   x = q − (84 + 672g)

The centered matrix is tridiagonal with diagonal (h, 0, 0, 0) and
off-diagonal (a, b, b). Its characteristic polynomial is

p(x; g) = x⁴ − hx³ − (a² + 2b²)x² + 2hb²x + a²b²

This follows from successive tridiagonal minors: p₀ = 1, p₁ = x − h,
p₂ = xp₁ − a², p₃ = xp₂ − b²p₁, p₄ = xp₃ − b²p₂. At g = 0, the
uncentered eigenvalues are $80\pm4\sqrt5$ and $88\pm4\sqrt5$. In the
original 192-state generated Li7 instance, each has total multiplicity
forty-eight. The quartic is derived from the retained matrix rather than
fitted to sampled eigenvalues. [EX, pp2-3,12]

The 1,680 component occurrences yield 165 distinct ordered operator
pairs, thirty-two symbolic quartics and 162 uniform-field compatibility
classes. These count different properties. An ordered pair keeps the
actual matrices in an aligned basis; a quartic keeps their eigenvalue
multiset; a compatibility class determines which placed operators
preserve the fixed transfer family. Equal local spectra do not by
themselves establish that differently placed operators are compatible.
[EX, pp5-6]

The source extension supplies the exchange law

Sean Brady’s explicit extension lifts the completed receiver-current
swap to two separately owned four-state factors:

P|i, j⟩ = |j, i⟩

The local return operator is not silently replaced by a permutation. T
and K retain their source roles; P is the newly specified joint action.
It preserves the sum of one-hot contents, reverses their directed
difference, and squares to the identity. The rational exchange family is

R(u, v) = (u − v)I + ηP,   η ≠ 0

The Yang-Baxter consistency condition reduces to a scalar coefficient
identity. For this family the residual coefficient is
η²[(u − w) − (u − v) − (v − w)], which vanishes identically. Native
exact word coefficients on the sixty-four-state three-factor space and
an independent permutation replay check the construction. The R-matrix
belongs to established integrable-system mathematics; the project result
is the specified source extension, its exact execution, and the
resulting reusable source-to-matter chain. [EX, pp3-4]

A complete finite polynomial Bethe benchmark

A transfer matrix is obtained by letting an auxiliary four-state factor
interact successively with the chain and tracing out that auxiliary
factor. With auxiliary label a, the local operators and ordered product
are

L_(aj)(u) = uI + ηP_(aj),   M_(a)(u) = L_(aL)(u)⋯L_(a1)(u)

τ_(L)(u) = tr_(a)M_(a)(u),   [τ_(L)(u), τ_(L)(v)] = 0

At the regular point its logarithmic derivative gives

$$
H_{\mathrm{ex}}=\eta\tau_L(0)^{-1}\tau_L^{\prime}(0)=\sum_{j=1}^{L}P_{j,j+1},\qquad L+1\equiv1$$

with no identity offset in the retained gauge. At L = 2 the two periodic
bonds give H₂ = 2P₁₂. This convention fixes the spectral numbers used in
every subsequent matter calculation. [EX, p4]

The finite benchmark constructs sixteen exact nested polynomial tuples:
two at L = 2, four at L = 3 and ten at L = 4. Each is checked against
the simultaneous eigenspaces of every transfer coefficient, including
the full GL4 multiplet multiplicities. All 336 states are accounted for,
and five hundred exact transfer-coefficient commutators vanish.

  Chain length   Exchange eigenvalue and multiplicity     Total states
  -------------- -------------------------------------- --------------
  2              −2:6; 2:10                                         16
  3              −3:4; 0:40; 3:20                                   64
  4              −4:1; −2:50; 0:60; 2:110; 4:35                    256

Polynomial divisibility also handles singular Bethe-root presentations.
At L = 4, the tuple Q₁ = z(z + 1), Q₂ = Q₃ = 1 gives the regular
collected eigenvalue Λ(z) = 4z⁴ + 4z³ − 2z − 1 and energy
Λ^(′)(0)/Λ(0) = 2. Dividing individual terms at the singular roots would
obscure that exact result. The benchmark instead collects the numerator,
checks exact divisibility, and compares the resulting polynomial with
the actual joint eigenspaces. The complete finite result is L = 2, 3, 4;
the large-chain spectrum below uses a separately established
representation route. [EX, pp4,11]

Exact representation structure closes length thirteen

The full tensor space has dimension 4^(L), but the exchange operator
lies in the permutation algebra. Schur-Weyl decomposition separates a
local-matter representation U_(λ) from a permutation representation
S_(λ) for each partition λ of L with at most four rows. Exchange acts on
S_(λ), and multiplying its eigenvalue multiplicity by dim U_(λ) recovers
the full tensor account. The exact dimension closure is

$$
\sum_{\substack{\lambda\vdash L\\\ell(\lambda)\le4}}\dim U_\lambda\,\dim S_\lambda=4^L$$

Momentum splits the permutation blocks further. Exact modular
characteristic polynomials and bounded Chinese remainder reconstruction
avoid uncontrolled rational coefficient growth. At L = 10, momentum
reduces the largest block from 768 to 77. A separately recorded
fifty-four-sector comparison takes 117.611 seconds for the rational
characteristic-polynomial stage and 0.962 seconds for its modular
counterpart. These are times for that stage and comparison. [EX, pp4-5]

The raw-coordinate route retains an invertible basis matrix B and the
retained action H_(B) without first normalizing the basis. The pencil
H_(B) − xB has determinant (−1)^(n)det (B)det (xI − B⁻¹H_(B)), so its
exact normalization and coordinate maps recover the same spectrum. At
L = 13, opposite momenta are paired over the real cyclotomic field
ℚ(ζ₁₃ + ζ₁₃⁻¹), of degree six. A 7,920-dimensional rational
primitive-parity problem becomes a largest 1,320-dimensional matrix over
that field. Supported GPU work constructs exact projector/action
residues and columns; CPU arithmetic performs certified lifting and
spectral computation. [EX, pp5,13]

The completed L13 record closes all thirty-nine partitions and accounts
for 4¹³ = 67, 108, 864 states. Its 713.1002004-second clock is the
resumed hybrid completion, importing thirty-two finished partitions and
completing the remainder. It is not a fresh whole-L13 timing. The
completed factors and multiplicities are the result used by the
subsequent matter calculation. The attached article’s L14 run was only
launched at its cutoff and is not included in that completed count. [EX,
pp5,10; H001563]

One retained exchange solution supports thirty two matter families

A uniform sum of local operators commutes with every site permutation.
For a local Q, the identity P(Q ⊗ I) = (I ⊗ Q)P gives
[P, Q ⊗ I + I ⊗ Q] = 0. Consequently the homogeneous Hamiltonian

$$
H=JH_{\mathrm{ex}}+\mu\sum_{j=1}^{L}Q(g)^{(j)}
$$

uses the same exchange decomposition. If q_(i)(g) are the four local
roots and n = (n₀, n₁, n₂, n₃) is a Schur occupation with
∑_(i)n_(i) = L, then its combined energy is

$$
E_{\lambda,e,n}=Je+\mu\sum_{i=0}^{3}n_iq_i(g)$$

The multiplicity is the exchange multiplicity m_(λ)(e) times the exact
Schur-weight multiplicity K_(λ, n). Coincident energies add their
multiplicities. This formula explains the reuse: a new local family
needs the retained exchange factors, local roots, occupations, source
maps and multiplicities; it does not require the exchange operator to be
solved again. [EX, p6]

  ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Retained     Partitions        Local   States per   Factor and     Assembly
  exchange                    families       family weight pairs      seconds
  input                                                          
  ---------- ------------ ------------ ------------ ------------ ------------
  L12                  34           32   16,777,216       54,633     0.842111

  L13                  39           32   67,108,864       35,184     2.011662
  ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

Both rows perform zero new exchange solves and zero new local-quartic
solves. The L13 shared template retains 133 distinct exchange factors.
Its assembly clock measures the composition stage, separately from
obtaining those factors. This is the main computational significance: a
completed many-body spectrum remains an exact operand for an entire
family of further matter calculations. [EX, pp6-7; H001565]

The all-length one-site criterion also identifies when nonidentical
local sources may be mixed. For L ≥ 2 in the homogeneous untwisted
family, a sum ∑_(j)A_(j)^((j)) commutes with every transfer matrix
exactly when A_(j) − tr (A_(j))I/4 is independent of site. Uniform
traceless content is sufficient; scalar offsets may differ and remain in
the final energy. The retained example K₀₃₆ − K₀₁₂ = 336I has that
property. The necessity follows from commutation with the cyclic shift
at τ_(L)(0), then partial tracing its shift defect. This is an
all-length algebraic statement with its declared transfer-family
assumptions. [EX, p6]

Fusion and a conserved current connect composite channels

The pair projectors E_(±) = (I ± P₁₂)/2 select ten symmetric and six
antisymmetric local states. The exact fused/fundamental R-polynomials
act on forty- and twenty-four-dimensional spaces. All fifteen bivariate
coefficient matrices in the mixed Yang-Baxter comparison vanish in each
channel, checked in the faithful regular representation of S₄. Uniform
matter actions lift into the same fused bases and have eigenvalues
q_(i) + q_(j), with i ≤ j in the symmetric channel and i < j in the
antisymmetric channel. [EX, pp6-7]

On three factors the oriented exchange current is
C = ∑_(j)[P_(j, j + 1), P_(j + 1, j + 2)]. It commutes with H_(ex) and
obeys

C² = 3H_(ex)² − 27I,   det (xI − C) = x²⁴(x² + 27)²⁰

The current’s diagonal pair-parity blocks vanish; both cross maps have
rank twenty. It therefore transports the two twenty-dimensional
mixed-symmetry sectors between the symmetric-pair and antisymmetric-pair
carriers while preserving their exchange energy. The fully symmetric
twenty-dimensional sector and fully antisymmetric four-dimensional
sector form its kernel. These are exact operator identities and ranks,
with no physical time assigned to C. [EX, pp7-8,13; H001565]

The collective interaction preserves the exact family

The completed compatibility analysis also supplies a genuine two-object
interaction. Equal coupling of every unordered pair gives

$$
Y_Q=\sum_{i<j}Q^{(i)}Q^{(j)}=\frac12\left[Q_{\mathrm{tot}}^2-(Q^2)_{\mathrm{tot}}\right]$$

Expanding the square proves the identity, and both uniform sums commute
with every site permutation. Thus JH_(ex) + μQ_(tot) + νY_(Q) preserves
the entire transfer family at every length. On a Schur occupation,
define s = ∑_(i)n_(i)q_(i) and r = ∑_(i)n_(i)q_(i)². Its energy is

$$
E_{\lambda,e,n}=Je+\mu s+\frac\nu2(s^2-r)$$

with the inherited multiplicities. Existing exchange and weight records
therefore already support this collective two-object term. [EX, p8]

The nearest-neighbor density family spanned by T ⊗ T, T ⊗ K + K ⊗ T and
K ⊗ K has a different completed answer. For all 165 retained pairs, its
strict three-site commutator outputs have rank three. For L ≥ 5 no
nonzero combination in that specified span commutes with the retained
H_(ex). This delimits that proposed geometry; the collective
construction is an explicit compatible successor. Spatial coefficients,
nuclear calibration and all-length polynomial Bethe completeness remain
separate questions. None is required to state the completed finite
spectra or the all-length compatibility identities already derived.

Chapter XVII Exact Application Reuse and Learned Reconstruction

A method becomes useful through its source contract

The same exact computational interface can serve different scientific
objects while keeping their questions distinct. A Li-6 construction asks
which source arrangements minimize a declared action and how a receiver
distinguishes retained ties. An ATOM3D observer asks which learned
expressions return genuinely different observations on a task roster. A
tau reconstruction asks whether native source distinctions help identify
useful measured objects and training methods. The completed applications
below supply actual operands, source maps and outputs for each question.

Their common feature is source-bound reuse. A retained expression,
inverse table, source factor or method memory can be applied again
without rebuilding the calculation that produced it. The meaning of the
reused object travels with its source inventory, observation roster and
task. Equal array sizes or matching labels do not establish a
cross-domain identification. The applications are therefore connected
here through their executed mathematical interfaces and source-defined
results, with no new scientific computation performed for this
manuscript.

Li 6 grammar retains the information in circulation

The isotope-specific Li-6 construction contains three proton and three
neutron objects on six named sites. Its nine oriented relations give
site-incidence rank five and cycle rank four. Together those coordinates
recover all nine edge currents. A source action based only on endpoint
currents would omit the circulation distinction used by the completed
grammar. The source retains six one-body addresses, one hidden-nine
support, road, color owner and weak vector, while distinguishing
partition, depth, route, source role and conjugation. [V3PLI6]

There are five motif covers. A cover assigns every relation once, either
through nine pair motifs or one three-owner motif and six remaining pair
motifs. The full 321-row catalog supplies a typed source; candidate
constituent projection selects the declared pair and triad rows. Unused
scalar or rejected-control records remain in the catalog without
becoming constituents of this isotope. The reference masks retain the
separated source obtained by removing its two depth joins.

The completed calculation selects two covers. Without hidden-loop
response, each favored three-owner circulation cover has sixty-four
minimizing histories and action 91/2. The hidden-nine depth-loop
response selects the same sixteen histories in both covers at observed
action
$$S_{\min}=\frac{737}{16}=46.0625$$
The pair-only cover instead has minimum 1385/16 with hidden response.
Two other triad covers have 363/4. The selected native-channel minimum
is 969/16 with the same two covers and sixteen histories. All ties are
retained. Across six center placements and 128 response settings, the
selected family is unchanged.

This source shows two distinct reductions: circulation selects the motif
covers, and the hidden-loop response refines their histories. The
endpoint-only triad comparison favors the pair-only cover. Replacing it
by the retained circulation changes the assembly preference on the same
constituent inventory. An explicit
same-site-current/different-circulation witness records the information
responsible for the change.

The factorized computation retains 245, 760 minimum cases and 119
distinct minimizing sets across its masks, channels, direction maps,
receiver variants, covers, placements and response settings. The
hardware calculation evaluates 5, 563, 227 primitive response values,
agreeing exactly across H14F direct forms, Ryzen independent expansions
and actual Radeon 780M integer contractions. Two source-feature batches
avoid materializing a large Cartesian product while their inverse maps
still cover 2, 097, 152 state/reference rows. The retained minimizing
sets and the three agreeing primitive calculations establish exact
source-action selection for this construction grammar. The action
46.0625 is in candidate source units; its measured binding-energy
assignment has a separate physical recipient.

Active probes resolve a family whose source action is tied

The original sixteen histories, two covers and six placements give 192
retained Li-6 configurations with the same observed action. The
signed-readout application asks how an observer can distinguish them
using new native quarter Writes. It evaluates the response to an
intervention instead of selecting among tied constructions through a
hidden identifier. [V3PLI6DECODER]

At known history 14336, a single Write on edge 002 or 006 already
distinguishes the twelve cover/placement alternatives. With initial
history unknown, all eighteen scalar action probes resolve ninety-six
pairs. Their remaining ambiguity is simultaneous global-current
reversal. The exact search over 153 probe pairs finds eight tied pairs
with the same ninety-six-class distinction; no single probe has more
than twenty-four outputs on the roster.

The selected decoder uses two independent response experiments. Each
starts from the same restored initial configuration: one positive Write
on the source edge of index two, and one negative Write on the source
edge of index five. The measured changes are accompanied by the signed
N01 site-current polynomial Re α + Im α, whose value is +1 or −1. The
two action changes distinguish the reversal pairs; the signed bit
supplies orientation. The native finite inverse recovers state, cover
and placement in all 192 cases. The experiments are independent
restored-state probes, rather than two consecutive changes to an already
perturbed source.

The source computation executes 288 transitions, 3264 construction
evaluations and 3456 signed response differences with their retained
arithmetic graphs. Independent rational checks match every difference.
All ninety-six inverse pairs have the predicted reversal association,
and fresh graph recovery applies the usable decoder without replacing
its source. The signed response differences distinguish the retained
constructions and preserve their reversal associations under independent
rational reconstruction.

One difference is only 43 inside response values of order 10¹². Retained
exact signed arithmetic computes it while reusing 560 prior graph nodes.
This is a concrete application in which preserving exact operands
matters to the scientific receiver: a small cover distinction remains
available above a large source baseline. It does not itself assign the
experimental resolution, magnetic coefficient or MeV conversion. The
decoder identifies the admitted finite source family at response setting
one, with every cover and placement still available.

The observer chooses distinct reports without retraining memory

The next ATOM3D application keeps learned memory fixed and changes only
method selection. Its predecessor contains thirty-six atomic methods and
ten clock methods. Several compatible expressions can produce the same
vector of reports over a declared task’s source histories. Reporting all
of them spends observations without distinguishing another candidate.
The native distinct selector computes that equivalence in the installed
exact arithmetic. [V3PSELECTOR]

For compatible methods e, f, the task-specific decision is
D(e, f) = ∑_(s)(q_(e)(s) − q_(f)(s))²
Every difference, square and sum is native exact signed-log computation.
Zero identifies equal report vectors on the declared roster. A signed
sum of differences would be insufficient because its terms can cancel.
In calibration, twelve compatible methods have eight distinct report
vectors. Seven of sixty-six pairs are equal and fifty-nine differ;
twenty-two unequal pairs have signed differences summing to zero. The
squared test preserves all twenty-two distinctions.

The quotient retains method identities and fiber multiplicities. Its
exact residual method-identity information is (5/6)log 2. The selector
follows the original compatible-method priority, takes the first
representative of each exact report class and fills up to four distinct
slots. All expressions and ties remain in memory. Their equivalence is
bound to the task, source roster and frozen method memory, rather than
declared as a global identity between expressions.

The previous 224-history reuse comparison keeps all histories correctly
recovered while reducing scalar reports from 516 to 508. Fresh
confirmation declares all 256 legal length-eight words on one A3D29
orbit, disjoint from 416 predecessor paths. Both selectors recover
256/256 histories; the distinct selector uses 625 reports instead of
633. Saved policies replay all confirmation cases exactly on the
opposite host, with no new source-model generation, ranking or search.

The cost comparison retains its ordering: unresolved histories first,
scalar reports second, executed query nodes third. Fewer reports can
require additional arithmetic. Query nodes increase from 1899 to 2163 on
fresh confirmation, while scalar bytes decrease from 2749 to 2715.
Selector preparation is separately measured; recovery reuses its saved
policy. The saved selector improves its declared observation objective
using existing learned methods. The counts above show separately what
happens to scalar storage and executed arithmetic.

The completed tests remain available while this research line is
owner-paused. Its eight services are stopped and the verified local
checkpoint is retained. The pending final T500 checkpoint-copy status is
carried by its live authority. That operational boundary does not alter
the completed native equivalence and paired-selector outcomes.

An exact symmetry accelerates full memory in a matter source

Application reuse can also improve execution without reducing the
represented state. The two retained A3D41 six-spin phase graphs have
sixty-four states, zero fields, inverse temperature 1/4, unit attempts
and unit departure clocks. Global spin reversal sends state s to 63 − s
and leaves every pair energy and flip gap unchanged. The actual
generator therefore commutes with that reversal. [V3PPARITY]
[V3PPARITYDERIV]

For paired states set x_(i) = p_(i) + p_(63 − i) and
y_(i) = p_(i) − p_(63 − i). The transformed generator has two
independent thirty-two-coordinate propagation blocks. This is an
invertible representation of all sixty-four coordinates. The symmetric
block is a probability generator; the antisymmetric block carries signed
response. A visible-bit mask couples the blocks:
x⁺ = (x + Sy)/2,   y⁺ = (Sx + y)/2
where diagonal S records the accepted bit of each representative.
Repeating propagation and these masks reconstructs the original
unnormalized accepted substate for arbitrary initial preparation and
every declared finite ordered reading sequence.

This exact representation retains the preparation dependence exposed by
the full source. In one graph, two initial states with the same visible
bit have three-read acceptance approximately 0.47596 and 0.95360; in the
other, corresponding values are 0.40943 and 0.99147. Formal refinement
of the two visible classes reaches all sixty-four classes in both
sources. The parity method accelerates those full histories while
retaining their hidden-state information.

The pilot’s propagation medians improve by factors 2.004 and 2.176 on
one i9 core. Including the separately measured common generator cost
gives factors 1.343 and 1.265. Both endpoint and accepted-substate
operators are checked on all sixty-four basis preparations. The source
states, timing scopes and complete retained response remain explicit.
The full response is preserved while propagation work is reduced in the
measured pilot. The adapter is a qualified research candidate rather
than an unrecorded global operation installation.

Tau learns correspondence and training utility separately

The tau pilot asks a different applied question: can native SAM
distinctions organize measured constituent observations and help choose
useful reconstruction methods? The source vocabulary includes measured
constituents, pair/three-owner roles, amount versus directional
contribution, signed receiver values, native Write barriers and relative
grammar alternatives. These are declared source mappings into measured
data. The pilot acquires finite empirical correspondences over that
vocabulary, while actual matched training supplies a separate

Correspondence acquisition uses 8192 training observations. For each
task, exact native conditional information selects among eight role
recipes, then evaluates all fifteen pairs of six native readout families
jointly. Each candidate report has four training-median bits, and
constituent count supplies one-, two- and three-or-more-object contexts.
Exact ties and conditional target probabilities remain in the acquired
memory.

For transverse-residual positivity, the selected representative is
energy near-jet, paired with signed assembly and relative grammar.
Identification selects momentum near-jet with endpoint and signed
assembly. Six-class decay selects momentum near-jet with signed assembly
and relative grammar. Their conditional bundle information is
approximately 0.329848087, 0.181193140 and 0.347745237 nats, with
respectively 875, 818 and 881 saved cells: 2574 source-bound

The positivity question concerns signs of transverse residual
components, a different target from the final trained angular-error
metric.

The saved probability tables can predict new known cells after restart
without another neural fit or information-planning calculation. On
eligible new observations, positivity accuracy is 30.10% versus the
same-row count-only 27.53%; identification is 80.36% versus 72.18%;
decay is 54.51% versus 53.58%. The known/eligible counts are 2256/2485,
4792/4985 and 2260/2485. The remaining 229, 193 and 225 eligible
observations are explicitly unknown cells. A source-bound memory can
therefore supply useful predictions and an exact account of where its

Matched fitting selects methods the information ranking did not

Thirteen methods, three seeds and twenty epochs produce thirty-nine fits
and 780 epochs on 20, 000 training rows and 5000 validation rows.
Checkpoints and choices freeze before 5000 new confirmation rows are
opened. Every fit uses the corrected residual process, training-only
component scales, an anchored momentum head, fixed hidden widths,
zero-start added inputs and matched seed/epoch orders. Shuffled controls
break only the added native-feature-to-row association. [V3PTAU]

Validation selects an established momentum-Q3 direction method with
transverse assembly/response, an established charge-mixed identification
method and the corrected energy-Q3 decay base. Their new confirmation
means are 0.010031877 radians, 0.982346871 AUROC and 0.692733319
macro-F1. The selected direction readout reduces its measured-jet
starting error from 0.010917399 radians by 8.11% on this new sample.

The best learned-bundle alternatives are also fixed by validation. Their
new direction error is 0.010072426 radians, 0.40% above the selected
reference; their identification AUROC is 0.982390874, a gain of
0.000044002; their decay macro-F1 is 0.692669915, a change of
−0.000063404. The matched test does not improve direction or mean decay
accuracy; identification AUROC increases by the stated small amount. The
pilot separately completes acquisition and reuse of the learned bundle.

These outcomes locate the distinction between information and training
utility. A native report can carry conditional information about a
declared task without being the validation-selected input for its
trained reconstruction loss. The completed method memory consequently
records actual pre-confirmation fitting results alongside correspondence
memory. It returns the selected three training plans after restart with

not enter those method rankings.

Chapter XVIII Certified Source Integration and Retained Response

An exact finite table becomes more useful when another calculation can
receive it with its source, clock and dependencies intact. The completed
spin work supplies two detailed examples. One transfers certified
integration to original signed-Write source paths and then executes
complete carrier trajectories. The other compiles retained Gibbs-source
matrices into response methods and improves the receiver by calculating
a new source observable. Both examples develop computation as a chain of
mathematical objects, with the receiving operation kept explicit.
[V3S08,V3S11,V3S14]

The source path precedes the numerical integral

For the original signed W1/W4/W7 source, execution first returns the
actual wrapped phase state (n_(a), n_(b), n_(p)), orientation and
declared path role. The path is then
$$\begin{align}
 a(\theta)=(n_a-n_p)\pi/2+k_a\sigma\theta,\\
 b(\theta)=(n_b-n_p)\pi/2+k_b\sigma\theta\end{align}$$
with 0 ≤ θ ≤ π/2. The role coefficients (k_(a), k_(b)) are (1, 0),
(0, 1) and (−1, −1). Its original density is E = 6 − 2cos a + 2cos b,
with role derivative
g = 2sin a,   − 2sin b,   − 2sin a + 2sin b
respectively. The elapsed-source integral is
$$\begin{equation}
 I=\int_0^{\pi/2}\frac{12\sqrt{E^2+g^2}}{E^2+36}\,\mathrm{d}\theta\end{equation}$$
The initial state, signed role and orientation determine the integrand.
An aggregate endpoint or total action is not substituted for that source
history. [V3S09]

The successful transfer evaluates this same integral in two coordinates.
Angular quadrature is paired with the half-angle chart t = tan (θ/2):
$$\begin{equation}
 \cos\theta=\frac{1-t^2}{1+t^2},\quad
 \sin\theta=\frac{2t}{1+t^2},\quad
 \mathrm{d}\theta=\frac{2\mathrm{d} t}{1+t^2}\end{equation}$$
Both charts run at 384 bits. Initial quarter-turn sines and cosines are
exact integers. Certified analytic branch handling remains active for
the square roots. The two resulting enclosures must be finite, positive,
overlap and have radius below 10⁻⁶⁰. The full half-angle enclosure is
passed onward; no midpoint or intersected point replaces it. A
constant-density branch is also checked against π/2. [V3S09,V3S12]

The clock receives this operand through native products and solves,
preserving the original calibration and wait:
$$\begin{equation}
 \Delta t=\mathrm{ratio}\left[
 \frac{RI}{c(1-A_\mathrm{los})}+\mathrm{waiting\ gap}\right]\end{equation}$$
Internal carrier time, elapsed source time, propagation length and the
later clock readout remain separate quantities. This distinction
connects directly to Volume I: a source-derived duration enters a
specified receiver and does not acquire every other clock meaning merely
by being positive. [V3S09,V3S11]

Source matrices become executable methods

Let b₄ = (d, e, u_(d), u_(e)), with Gram G₄, Dirichlet matrix D₄ and

H = G₄⁻¹D₄,   T₄ = D₄G₄⁻¹D₄ + EW₂E^(*)
Here E is the source’s matching hidden-coordinate embedding. The

Z = L⁻¹EW₂,  B = Z^(*)G₄Z,  Q = B + W₂
The retained and complement terms are computed with their full mixed
