Balancing retains a small advantage over uniform strengthening at
matched coupling-only budget. Uniform strengthening performs better when
hidden fields are counted in the strength budget. Four-reading
confirmation is competitive at the original source strengths and
unchanged exposure, but changes sampling and measurement costs. Five
readings suppress false emissions further while increasing missed-prime
opportunities; more confirmation does not monotonically improve the
combined fidelity objective.

These comparisons place the observed intervention among explicit design
alternatives. The original intervention achieved a large measured
improvement with pair strength and cadence fixed. It does not establish
balancing as a universal optimum at every resource convention. The
controls identify which resources and error opportunities a subsequent
optimization must price.

Validation and what the retained records support

The balanced source passed 1,080 source checks including all 280
profile/phase/input transition matrices against a separately implemented
direct Padé exponential. After column normalization, the maximum entry
discrepancy was 1.5821×10^-14; the initial unnormalized comparison and
its column-mass drift are retained. Short-run qualification contributed
64,574 checks, including scalar CDF sampling, independent modulo-based
return reconstruction and final-state/count/checksum agreement.
[PE2-PE3]

The matched campaign checked all 25,600 chunk hashes and chains and
scored all 127,999,999,872 candidate decisions against the independent
sieve. It checked 1,572,864 retained microscopic prefix samples against
their output decisions. The fresh continuation checked 6,400 chunks,
scored 31,999,999,968 decisions and checked 393,216 prefix samples.
Together these phases contain 159,999,999,840 scored decisions across
paired profiles, a work count rather than a count of distinct integers
or independent statistical replications. Whole-sequence scoring is
extensive; complete independent replay of every microscopic transition
is not claimed.

Native source construction, qualification, matched execution and fresh
continuation retain their result, export and checkpoint receipts. The
archived source and matched data comprise 26,220 files and
25,821,384,418 bytes; the fresh archive contains 6,535 files and
6,350,494,109 bytes. Final hash receipts record successful T500 custody.
A standalone clean replay of baseline seed 1000063 through one billion
matches its final microscopic state, event count and checksum while
emitting exactly the reference prime sequence. [A2, Section VI; PE2]

The achieved result is a source-linked reliability method: construct a
finite microscopic gate, retain its dynamics and event history, localize
a failure, choose a coupling change from the algebra, and measure the
complete successor ensemble. The finite successes, local derivations and
resource comparisons remain distinct and jointly usable. Their scope is
the specified stochastic source, digital recurrence, cadence and
observed or numerically analyzed ensembles. Physical gate calibration,
an average causal measurement of post-error feedback, and new matched
ensembles for the alternative resource designs remain separate tasks.

Chapter XXV Prime Event Continuation and Retained Clocks

The prime-event construction was continued far beyond its matched
billion-index reliability study. The retained local CPU trajectory
completed 1,039,515,000,000 decisions with zero false events and zero
missed primes before its intentional stop. A separate three-GPU
continuation of the same seed reached eight trillion decisions with
three false emissions and no missed primes. These are completed
trajectories with their own source, checkpoint and scoring records.
Their lengths and error counts describe those realized paths; the
matched and fresh-seed ensemble comparisons remain the separate
reliability evidence developed in the preceding chapter.
[V3PUB01,V3PUB02,V3PUB03,V3PUB04]

This chapter preserves the later developments of the expanded
manuscript: measured transfer from exact graph responses, lower-leakage
gates, a finite first-error bound, long raw execution, and exact
clock-sensitive response. The event index, microscopic preparation and
internal activity clock each retain information that later operations
can use. Complete numerical and source scopes are stated with the result
they concern.

Source support and driven history before the feedback successor

The selective compiler preceding the seven-spin gate enumerated all 67
four-boundary sign subgroups. A single subgroup projection retains an
interaction family closed under XOR: two retained disjoint pairs also
permit their four-spin product, while overlapping retained pairs permit
the remaining pair. Unequal nonnegative copies of gauge-transformed
stars can remove unwanted companions. The completed search examined
6,560 nonempty copy vectors with multiplicities zero, one or two; its
derivation extended the relevant minima to arbitrary nonnegative integer
multiplicities in that dictionary. Independent local modules then permit
the two pair strengths and the triple orientation to be selected
separately. Character cancellation fixes the interaction support at
every temperature. Matching its amplitude is a separate calculation at
the selected temperature. [V3PE01]

For inverse temperature β, summing one hidden pair module gives
2cosh [βu(e + r)]. The four-hidden-spin triple module has partition
factors 16cosh (4βt) and 16cosh (2βt)⁴ in its two visible-parity
sectors. Thus the effective coefficients before setting β = 1 are
$$a(\beta,u)=\frac{\log\cosh(2\beta u)}{2\beta},\qquad
 b(\beta,t)=\frac{4\log\cosh(2\beta t)-\log\cosh(4\beta t)}{2\beta}$$
The original scale-two source has b = 2.9609499856382886, one-bank gap
2b = 5.9218999712765772 and equilibrium false-output probability about
0.00267293946 per reading. The finite three-reading experiment retains
the full state rather than treating those readings as independent
equilibrium samples. [V3PE02]

The earlier driven experiment used externally specified return periods.
For periods 5/7, pair strength two and Γ = 8, balanced correctness was
71.737824% with uniform accumulation, 71.236764% with an
exposure-matched rough profile, and 71.525318% when those same rough
values were reversed. At fast relaxation the ordering difference fell
below numerical comparison resolution. These calculations establish that
equal total exposure does not determine a source’s response to an
ordered input sequence. The clamping and accumulation profiles were
supplied drives; the experiment did not assign their work or
backreaction. [V3PE04]

A retained feedback reconstruction shows the distinct role of event
creation order. In one rough-profile path, an extra event at 312596611
changed event-count parity before prime 483920363, sending that prime’s
channel to a different bank. At 967840726 = 2 × 483920363, period-two
and prime-period returns occupied one bank in the uniform path and
separate banks in the rough path. One path emitted falsely while the
other suppressed. The earlier extra event did not divide the later
candidate; its effect passed through event order and bank assignment.
The mechanism study replayed 230 selected five-million chunks and 264
detailed windows. The example establishes that feedback path, alongside
the ensemble and first-error evidence in the preceding chapter. [V3PE06]

A measured bridge from exact graph responses

The spin/prime dataset made the common interface explicit. Its completed
first build joined 6,000 spin cases at sizes 1-1000, 91,620 boundary
ground-response records, 183,240 certified thermal readouts and 183,272
interaction-coefficient records, together with the two historical gate
variants and their observed outcomes. It preserved graph identity, port
ordering, temperature, source pointers and aliases. The 1,056
seed/horizon records include repeated measurements; the database does
not turn them into 1,056 independent runs. Full joint density tables
were not expanded simply to make the join. [V3PE08]

Every indexed spin graph had zero local fields. All 91,620 odd-degree
coefficient intervals contained zero, as required by global inversion. A
direct odd triple could therefore not be extracted from an unchanged
zero-field four-port source. The completed connection instead pins one
port. For the original even four-port expansion, setting a boundary spin
to p yields
βF(e, r₁, r₂ ∣ p) = input terms + e(d + ur₁ + vr₂ + wr₁r₂)
The four-body term becomes the visible triple w; the pair joining output
to the pinned port becomes the output bias d. A declared local output
field can cancel that bias at the specified temperature. This is a
concrete boundary condition and source modification. It explains how odd
gate terms arise while the original zero-field inversion theorem remains
intact. [V3PE03] [V3PE08]

For the N = 1000 positive-fill packet at β = 4/1000, the selected pinned
role and sign convention give dimensionless coefficients
$$\begin{align*}
 d=u=v=-1.907980462097066,\\
 w=+1.730671594798363\end{align*}$$
After the specified bias cancellation, the triple-to-pair ratio is
b/a = 0.9070698726632545. Transferring that ratio into the existing
six-hidden-spin compiler at gate temperature β = 1, and fixing the mean
pair coefficient to 3.6534264659876117, constructs a new 128-state
model. Its finite-rate transition calculation does not inherit the
dynamics or relaxation time of the original 1000-spin graph. [V3PE03]

The measured comparison covered both profiles, 35 phases and all 128
incoming states. Worst uniform one-bank false-emission probabilities
were 1.91437 × 10⁻⁸ for the original gate, 5.10148 × 10⁻⁹ for the new
N = 62 positive packet ratio, 2.60902 × 10⁻⁹ for N = 300, and
2.31833 × 10⁻⁹ for N = 1000. The last gives an 8.26-fold improvement
over the historical gate. The already balanced design remained better at
3.04423 × 10⁻¹⁰. The negative-fill N = 1000 packet supplied a
contrasting ratio of approximately 8.66 × 10⁻⁷ and one-bank leakage
0.124998814. Boundary response is therefore a useful design feature, but
its sign and scale must actually be retained. [V3PE03]

Keeping the pinned output bias provides an instructive control. The
unadjusted N = 1000 design suppresses one-bank false emissions to about
7.0 × 10⁻¹⁹, but its worst no-bank missed-emission probability is
2.67 × 10⁻⁶. Cancelling the bias brings the latter to 1.79 × 10⁻⁹ while
giving one-bank error 2.32 × 10⁻⁹. Three-confirmation readout treats
emission and suppression asymmetrically, so even a favorable smallest
equilibrium margin is not a complete dynamic objective.

Entropy is part of this transfer. The retained thermal response
reconstructs the pinned field through
$$\frac{\beta}{2}\Delta F
 =\frac{\beta}{2}\Delta\langle E\rangle-\frac12\Delta S$$
For the selected no-return packet field the actual decomposition is
3.6386520569 = 4.0074318192 − 0.3687797623
Ground energies alone would miss the entropy contribution that changes
the gate bias. The exact joint and boundary machinery of Chapter VI
supplies the source identity, normalization and thermal information
needed to make this connection reproducible. [V3PE03]

The source search examined 11,268 source/temperature pairs with 192
role/gauge choices, both with and without bias cancellation: 4,326,912
candidate evaluations. Twenty-four selected candidates received 96 Arb
truth-table checks, and six compiled gate designs received finite-rate
evaluation. No new closed-loop stream was generated by this connection
experiment. Positive packet and frustrated signed-chain ratios at
N = 1000 differ by only about 5.95 × 10⁻⁷; their conditional errors are
correspondingly almost equal. The completed result identifies
boundary-response structure as the effective design input. It does not
identify frustration itself as the cause of the measured improvement.
[V3PE03]

Uniform leakage improvement and the first error theorem

A separate continuation increased the balanced source strength while
retaining the same readout. At u = 5, the analytic balance gives
t = 2.8465622403941495 and a = b = 4.653426410750604. Temperature
remains one, attempt exposure remains Γ = 512, and there are still three
readings. Under balance, the no-bank field is 3a and every blocked input
field is −a. Independent equilibrium readings suggest an error scale
near e^(−6a); this explains the design direction, while the actual
result uses the complete finite-rate sampler. [V3PE09] [V3PE10]

All 35 × 4 × 128 = 17, 920 phase/bank/state inputs strictly improved.
The worst blocked-input emission probability fell from
3.04422969 × 10⁻¹⁰ to 8.0581104 × 10⁻¹³; worst no-bank suppression fell
from 9.06033914 × 10⁻¹⁰ to 2.250089 × 10⁻¹². The minimum improvement
across individual inputs exceeded 377.78. These are probabilities of the
encoded CDF sampler. Its binary64 transition tables were independently
compared with matrix exponentials, but exact finite-table counting has
its own precise meaning. [V3PE09]

Let L = 2⁵³ and let C_(s)(j) be a stored row CDF. The number of
uniform-grid draws selecting state j is
W_(sj) = ⌈LC_(s)(j)⌉ − ⌈LC_(s)(j − 1)⌉
with endpoints clipped to the draw range. Each row sums to L. Set
V₀(s) = 1 and
V_(k + 1)(s) = ∑_(j: e(j) = −1)W_(sj)V_(k)(j)
Then the exact three-reading emission probability is V₃(s)/L³, with
denominator 2¹⁵⁹. This counts retained-state paths through all three
readings. It establishes exactness for the encoded sampler under
independent uniform-grid draws, rather than asserting that rounded
tables equal exact real exponentials. [V3PE10]

The conditional table also supports a useful finite first-error theorem.
Suppose accepted history is exactly the primes before the next
candidate, and fresh draw triples are independent uniform-grid draws
conditional on the entire past. Let c bound blocked-input emission over
all phases and incoming states, and d bound no-bank suppression. The
induction above assigns a nonzero mask to every composite and mask zero
to every prime, regardless of the microscopic preparation. Disjointly
decomposing by the position of the first error gives
$$\begin{align*}
 \Pr(\text{an error through }N)
 \le [N-1-\pi(N)]c+\pi(N)d\end{align*}$$
with the bound capped at one. Dependence of incoming states across
candidates is allowed. The hypothesis concerns new conditional draws; a
fixed PCG64 seed is a realized deterministic algorithmic path. After an
error, accepted periods can be wrong, so this proof is a first-error
bound and does not bound every subsequent error in a corrupted feedback
history. [V3PE10]

Raw execution exact continuation and resource evidence

The lower-leakage source was actually run without correction, learner,
factoring veto or replacement of event history. The initial paired CPU
pilot used seed 9368001 and an extra draw seed 10368001. Both original
and u5 sources completed 100 million decisions with zero false emissions
and zero missed primes, producing 5,761,455 events. Median complete
rates were 8.540 and 8.487 million decisions per second respectively,
including truth observation, compression and durable checkpoints. Six
timing trials, three per source, executed 600 million decisions in
total, reusing the same 100-million prefix for each source; they measure
timing and reproducibility, not six independent accuracy exposures.
[V3PE11]

An indexed-history integration then reached 200 million unique decisions
for the u5 seed, with 11,078,937 accepted events and zero errors.
Resident-reference, continuous-indexed and
stopped-at-85-million/restored paths agreed at every checked chunk and
endpoint. Six hundred million executed decisions across those paths
represent the same 200-million unique prefix. The bounded 8 MiB decoded
cache reduced resident memory and authenticated old history on demand.
Payload was durably written before checkpoint publication. An
uncommitted journal was deliberately quarantined and regenerated; this
establishes the tested orphan-recovery case, rather than an unstated
arbitrary-power-failure theorem. The complete checkpoint preserves
actual source state, RNG and history index. [V3PE12]

A later RTX3090 implementation qualified the raw 128-state source,
rather than applying the earlier eight-state GPU controller’s
checked-repair rule. Reusable RAM buffers and exact disjoint PCG64
subsequences gave 14.43-14.70 million decisions per second for the whole
100-million prefix, including CPU startup, initial GPU parity checking,
observation, compression and durable writes. Later 90-million windows
reached 17.60-17.89 million per second. The matched CPU reference, all
checked chunk states and draw-stream endpoints agreed; stopping at 35
million and restoring reproduced uninterrupted execution. Repeated GPU
qualification paths remain one unique 100-million source prefix. This
was a qualified backend and bounded performance result, not a silently
started continuous production run. [V3PE13]

The installed local resource successor preserved seed 9370001 at
migration, both PCG64 states and all 23,336 prior history records. Its
scientific configuration remained byte-equivalent while the execution
cache and checkpoint index changed. Adjacent production windows showed a
10.3% complete-rate gain, from 9.772 to 10.776 million decisions per
second; this observation includes overlap and contention rather than
isolating a kernel change. The later 908.480-billion rollout observation
was subsequently superseded by the completed local endpoint. [V3PE14]
[V3PE15]

The local CPU trajectory was intentionally stopped on 3 October 2026 at
13:15:20 UTC after 1,039,515,000,000 decisions, through candidate
1,039,515,000,001. It retained 39,036,981,351 events in 207,903
five-million chunks, with zero false events and zero missed primes. Its
final 500-million chunk-loop window ran at 7.152970 million decisions
per second; current throughput is zero because this is a stopped result.
Final checks authenticated the frozen source, all 207 index blocks and
positions/ranks of all indexed records, plus the final journal and
complete terminal state/RNG agreement. That check did not replay the
trillion-decision path or rehash every earlier journal. STOP remained
present and continuation disabled. [V3PUB01] [V3PUB02] [V3PUB03]

The same seed’s separately continued three-GPU execution stopped at
exactly 8,000,000,000,000 decisions on 3 October at 16:49:18 UTC, with
279,010,070,814 actual events, three false emissions and no missed
primes. The false positions were 2, 274, 828, 165, 298,
2, 379, 059, 006, 438 and 2, 687, 315, 963, 345. Each error reproduced
in a retained one-billion CPU replay with preceding history, microscopic
state and both RNG streams. Every false emission fed back unchanged. The
independent segmented sieve only scored decisions. The final
hundred-billion interval averaged 155.410939 million decisions per
second; this dated production result uses a later Blackwell
configuration and does not replace the earlier RTX3090 pilot
measurement. Its 1,600,000 committed records, final state and
archive-chain custody were retained. Three GPUs separated speculative
proposals, bank-mask construction from authenticated actual history, and
ordered event extraction/scoring; CPUs retained ordered state
consumption, exact random streams and the sole durable writer. Local and
pod executions overlap on the same seed, so their prefixes are not
summed as independent accuracy exposure. Both endpoints are intentional
stops, not current running counters. [V3PUB04] [V3PUB05] [V3PUB06]

Native clocks retain information beyond accepted indices

The clock continuation returns to the balanced seven-spin source with
its retained kinetic policy, β = 1, Γ = 512, η = 3/8, and actual
source-time observation schedule. It does not treat the u5 production
horizon as a new clock experiment. For the actual state S_(t) and bank
b, let λ_(b) be the sum of all seven flip rates and κ_(b) the
output-spin flip rate. Two native activity coordinates are
τ_(all)(t) = ∫₀^(t)λ_(b)(S_(s)) ds,   τ_(out)(t) = ∫₀^(t)κ_(b)(S_(s)) ds
The compensated flip counts are martingales under the source process.
Their usual expectation identity applies to the declared unconditioned
or appropriately stopped horizon; it does not automatically hold after
selection on a future accepted history. [V3C04] [V3PE17]

For any positive activity a, time change gives the column generator
Q^(a) = Qdiag (1/a) and speed measure proportional to aπ, where π is
stationary for Q. Dividing by the output hazard makes the output flip
rate exactly one from every microscopic state. Therefore, if C_(e)
groups only the visible output,
$$C_eQ^\mathrm{out}=H C_e,
 \qquad
 H=\begin{pmatrix}-1&1\\1&-1\end{pmatrix}$$
This is an exact two-state output quotient in output-activity time, for
every bank and hidden preparation. Its apparent simplicity does not
remove the source’s memory. The actual readings occur at random stopping
coordinates τ_(out)(t_(j)) inherited from source-time waits.
Substituting equal clock waits changes the protocol. The all-spin
activity clock does not even have this output-only quotient, because κ/λ
varies within each output sector. [V3PE17]

The full accepted clock law uses the tilted kernel
F_(b)(z, t) = exp [(Q_(b) − zdiag κ_(b))t]
The masked product 1^(⊤)DF₃DF₂DF₁ρ is the clock-weighted acceptance
mass. Divide by its value at z = 0 to obtain the conditional Laplace
transform. Normalization and all three masks are part of the observable.

At bank zero, initial states 24 and 102 both show output −1 and have
acceptance mass 0.999999999093968. Their conditional transforms at z = 1
are nevertheless 0.9999998550562574 and 0.2252263286074421. Directed
mean-clock bounds put the first mean below 0.000141473371361 and the
second above 1.49064947744, a separation of at least 1.490508004067729
activity units. Bank-one Laplace values also differ, but its displayed
mean intervals overlap and do not order those means. These are declared
hidden preparations, not equal-frequency production populations. [V3C04]

A finite raw-feedback experiment ran 32 fresh seeds through candidate
2048: 65,504 decisions, 23,256,750 actual flips and 54,138 output flips.
Each seed accepted the same 309 events without errors, yet all 31
comparison seeds had different internal clock coordinates from the
first. Doubling every rate while halving every laboratory wait
reproduced states, readings, bank history and both clocks bitwise. The
achieved clock is therefore a source-path quantity invariant under the
common time-unit change. Its units are activity; a physical lapse or
calibration in seconds was not supplied by this construction. [V3C04]

An accurate event quotient can omit clock memory

The next completed comparison retained output and hidden bits 5 and 6,
giving eight coarse coordinates. Let C group these blocks and R lift
each block using its declared conditional Gibbs weights. Then CR = I₈,
P = RC is a projection, and the omitted residual has dimension 120. The
stored rate model is bound as exact binary rationals with diagonals
rebuilt exactly; conditional weights are normalized within blocks. This
does not silently assert that the stored Gibbs weights are stationary
for the rounded generator. [V3PE18] [V3PE19]

For a masked, possibly tilted interval operator T, decompose the full
state as x = Ru + v. The exact update is
$$\begin{align*}
 u'=CTR u+CTv,\\
 v'=(I-P')T(Ru+v)\end{align*}$$
The signed injection CTv is the source information lost by replacing the
residual with zero. This is the same structural issue treated for spin
preparation and selected readout in Chapter XVIII, now evaluated on the
actual prime gate and clock.

For three readings, set T_(i) = DF_(b)(z, d_(i)) using the actual
source-time waits d_(i), and write
$$\begin{align*}
 G_\mathrm{full}=CT_3T_2T_1,\\
 G_8=CT_3PT_2PT_1P\end{align*}$$
The exact difference is G_(full) − G₈ = Δ₀ + Δ₁ + Δ₂, with
$$\begin{align*}
 \Delta_0=CT_3PT_2PT_1(I-P),\\
 \Delta_1=CT_3PT_2(I-P)T_1,\\
 \Delta_2=CT_3(I-P)T_2T_1\end{align*}$$
These are respectively the preparation replacement, first-reading reset
and second-reading reset. Keeping the full first interval removes only
Δ₀. Adding all three signed operators restores the evaluated full
response for every preparation; it is not an autonomous eight-state
production algorithm. [V3PE19]

For the retained bank-zero protocol, the ordinary eight-coordinate
endpoint discrepancy was at most 6.175 × 10⁻²⁰ in L¹, while the
clock-weighted discrepancy was about 0.774772. States 102 and 120 share
all retained coordinates, yet their conditional Laplace responses differ
by 0.7747725033. Any initializer using only those coordinates must
therefore miss at least one response by 0.3873862517. Keeping the exact
first microscopic interval reduced the complete clock-weighted endpoint
discrepancy to at most 2.636 × 10⁻¹¹ uniformly over all probability
preparations. Event-only agreement had hidden a substantial preparation
response. [V3PE18]

For bank one, the post-first-interval unnormalized discrepancy was at
most 1.575 × 10⁻¹⁴, but acceptance was only about 3.014 × 10⁻¹⁰. Its
conditioned bound is consequently 5.222 × 10⁻⁵. Retaining this
denominator prevents a rare accepted branch from appearing accurate
merely because its total mass is small. Joint clock closure would
require both a generator intertwining and a block-constant clock hazard;
the latter fails in all eight blocks for all four banks. The exact
two-state activity-clock output quotient remains valid because its
random stopping map still carries that hidden information. [V3PE18]
[V3PE19]

What has been established and the next source bound join

The completed connection is substantial: an exact thermal response
supplies interaction features; a pairwise microscopic gate realizes
them; its finite-rate probabilities guide a source change; raw feedback
creates recurrence history; and complete masked operators expose timing
information that accepted indices alone cannot recover. The large-source
transfer has not yet received a matched closed-loop prime-stream
comparison, and the full three-term clock correction has not yet become
a smaller autonomous production representation. [V3PE03] [V3PE09]
[V3PE18]

Two focused assignments follow directly. One is to select new
boundary-derived designs against the actual three-reading objective
across every bank sector, then compare the selected gates in matched raw
feedback. The other is to preserve the joint clock/readout injection at
actual bank changes, retaining clock-selected preparation and both later
reset terms. The latest frustrated-spin source work supports the method
of carrying actual residual directions, signed mixed terms and
physical/background payments through a selected consumer. Its 28-spin or
large-N source, N/ξ site-attempt clock and resolvent parameter remain
distinct from this seven-spin gate and clock Laplace parameter. Reusing
that method is an identifiable mathematical connection; assigning its
numerical constants to Prime Event would change the source. [V3S14]

Hidden memory is a calculable part of the gate

A separate projection analysis applies the retained-history method to
the actual balanced 128-state gate. It projects onto the output
probability and lifts through conditional hidden-spin Gibbs states.
Eliminating the remaining hidden-state component gives a memory
convolution plus an initial-memory term. All four input sectors have
nonzero sampled kernels, and output-flip rates vary substantially among
microscopic states sharing the same output. The output bit alone
consequently does not form a closed Markov state. [PE6]

For a switch from both-return equilibrium to no-return input, duration
one and uniform accumulation 1/4, the full three-reading acceptance at
Gamma=8 is 0.306867084; a two-state Markov projection gives 0.581372959.
At Gamma=64 the corresponding values are 0.984884914 and 0.999052329. At
the production rate Gamma=512, the largest tested switching discrepancy
is 1.82×10^-12. These calculations identify when hidden preparation and
memory materially affect reduced predictions, while explaining why a
reduced picture can be accurate at the production cadence. They are
separate domain probes, not changes installed retrospectively in the
archived billion-index ensembles.

Chapter XXVI Jacobian Maps Return and Retained History

The JC1 polynomial map has exact Jacobian determinant −2 and a fiber
construction whose single and double windings produce different sheet
histories. Complete Write programs can return to the same endpoint
across all 262,144 addresses while retaining different event counts,
orientations and semantic hashes. This chapter derives those
distinctions and follows the finite and projective sheet calculations
that refine them.

The historical constructions in this chapter retain the fixed 8
September 2026 SAMA source stage. Later results are identified by their
own source records.

Overview

The Jacobian program asks how an exact polynomial map can return its
target state while retaining a nontrivial history of sheets, braids,
escape and projective completion. That is a mathematical and
computational question, so JC1 and HF1-HF6TH belong in Volume III. Their
earlier matter-volume-labelled files remain immutable provenance, but
relocation changes placement and current interpretation rather than any
executed result.

Bounded status of the archived algebra. The following formulas and fiber
claims are source-reported archived algebra, not a globally verified
theorem certified by this edition. Their printed coefficients lie in the
rational numbers. If intended as a global polynomial map over the
characteristic-zero complex field, an everywhere constant nonzero
Jacobian together with distinct preimages of one target would amount to
a counterexample to the Jacobian conjecture. This edition does not
certify that global claim. The intended domain, source interpretation
and independent mathematical adjudication remain unresolved here. The
finite Exact Write endpoint/history comparison below is logically
separate and does not require accepting a global counterexample.

The JC1 record reports the polynomial formulas

$$\begin{aligned}
P(x,y,z)=(1+xy)^3z+y^2(1+xy)(4+3xy),\\
Q(x,y,z)=y+3x(1+xy)^2z+3xy^2(4+3xy),\\
R(x,y,z)=2x-3x^2y-x^3z\end{aligned}$$

and reports that their Jacobian determinant simplifies to

det D(P, Q, R) = −2

An exact elimination chart produces a cubic fiber polynomial, its
resultant and discriminant. On the frozen slice V = −16/27, R = 1, one
winding around U = 0 transposes two escaping sheets, while double
winding returns the sheet permutation to the identity. The same winding
programs compile through Complete Exact Write on one Theta18 axis. Four
positive writes, four negative writes and eight positive writes all have
zero compiled translation across all 262,144 addresses, yet their
ordered W9 receipts retain four, four and eight events with different
orientations and semantic hashes. JC1 passes 18/18; independent
validation passes 9/9. The exact comparison distinguishes the event
histories even when all compiled endpoints coincide.

The portable result is narrower and stronger than an endpoint slogan:
endpoint equality does not imply program-history equality. It does not
by itself install JC1 monodromy, a quotient, a Newton field or a
tensor/path-jet field as a matter payload.

HF1-HF6TH then form a valuable but historically scaffolded discovery
chain: local reducers and a chosen Z4^2 cell grammar; nonproper
meridians and exact polynomial cells; finite versus projective sheet
custody; path-dependent escape corrections; blinded path-jet
discriminators; fresh-map transfer; Newton-polygon signatures; and a
complete mixed-jet compiler. Every executed finding and correction
remains in custody. The current clean restart does not assume that the
HF2 torus, its generators, H_SAM, W9 or any carrier/matter meaning
arises naturally from the polynomial map.

The restart therefore reverses the order of dependence: reconstruct the
source algebra independently, derive invariants from actual fibers and
the regular-value set, freeze algebraically generated loops, examine
monodromy, properness and infinity, and only then reveal legacy HF
observations. An optional SAM comparison requires an explicit compiler.
A later matter application requires carrier compilation, reciprocal
preservation, an identified matter consumer and a load-bearing
controlled output change. None has executed. This future bridge is not
standalone SAMA Volume IV, which is the RH program.

Exact JC1 source algebra

Polynomial map and constant Jacobian

Let

F : 𝔸³ → 𝔸³,   F(x, y, z) = (P, Q, R)

with P, Q, R as stated above. The derivative matrix is formed by
differentiating each component with respect to ((x,y,z)). The archived
record reports that polynomial expansion and factorization of this
determinant cancel the variable-dependent terms and leave

det DF = −2

Within the archived statement, this determinant is nonzero at every
affine source point and is read as absence of a local Jacobian
singularity. The record then investigates escaping branches and
properness. These global interpretations remain subject to the
bounded-status qualification above; they are not newly certified by the
finite history experiment.

Elimination chart

JC1 uses

$$x=\frac1m,
\qquad
y=m(s-1),
\qquad
z=(5-3s-m)m^2$$

Substitution into P, Q, R, followed by elimination of m, gives the exact
resultant

Res_(m)(U − Q, V − P) = s³Φ(U, V; s)

where

Φ(U, V; s) = K(U, V)s³ + (U² − 12V)s − 4V

and

K(U, V) = U³ − U² − 18UV + 27V² + 16V

The cubic discriminant factors as

Disc_(s)Φ = −4(U³ − 18UV + 54V²)²K(U, V)

Thus the two load-bearing target components are

D(U, V) = U³ − 18UV + 54V²

and K(U, V). Their repeated and simple factors distinguish finite-root
braid behavior from coefficient loss and projective escape.

Exact calibration and loss fibers

In the archived record, the target (P, Q, R) = (−1, 1, −1) is assigned
the eliminated x-fiber

(x − 1)(3x + 1)(3x + 2)

with three exact sources

(1, −2, 9),  (−1/3, 4, 27),  (−2/3, −1/2, −9/8)

The record reports that each maps to the same target and has Jacobian
−2. This is precisely the source-reported claim whose global scope is
not certified here.

At the coefficient-loss target

(U, V, R) = (0, −16/27, 1)

the unique finite source is

(x, y, z) = (1/2, −8/3, 16)

The eliminated x-fiber drops to (2x − 1)/2. Two other cubic branches
have moved to projective infinity rather than collided at an affine
Jacobian zero.

Monodromy from the fiber

Frozen slice

On V = −16/27,

$$K(U,-16/27)=\frac{U(3U^2-3U+32)}3$$

At U = 0, the cubic coefficient vanishes while

$$\Phi(0,-16/27;s)
=\frac{64}{9}s+\frac{64}{27}$$

One branch therefore tends to s = −1/3. Balancing the cubic and linear
terms for the other two branches gives

$$s^2U\longrightarrow-\frac23$$

Those branches escape with magnitude proportional to |U|^(−1/2).

Loop continuation

Take a small circle

U(θ) = ρe^(iθ),   V = −16/27,   R = 1

At each step, numerically continue the three roots of Φ by minimum
matching to the preceding roots. A positive winding and a negative
winding both return the finite branch and exchange the two escaping
branches:

[0, 1, 2] ↦ [0, 2, 1]

The opposite loop orientation changes path direction but not the
transposition. A double winding applies the transposition twice:

(1 2)² = e

so the sheet permutation returns to [0,1,2]. The offset zero-winding
control also returns identity. Maximum recorded fiber residuals stay
below 5 × 10⁻¹³ on the winding paths.

This chain separates target return, sheet permutation and path winding.
Double winding can have identity permutation while still containing a
nonempty ordered program.

Exact Write compilation and retained history

Theta18 event map

Theta18 addresses form ℤ₄⁹ in packed two-bit coordinates. On axis j, one
positive or negative event applies the exact signed ℤ₄ step. For axis
zero and initial address zero, the positive program visits

- 0 -> 1 -> 512 -> 513 -> 0

while the negative program visits

- 0 -> 513 -> 512 -> 1 -> 0.

Four equal signed steps are zero modulo four. Hence the programs

w₊ = (0, +1)⁴,   w⁻ = (0, −1)⁴,   w₊₊ = (0, +1)⁸

all compile to translation zero. Because the compiled action is a group
translation, zero translation returns every one of the 262,144 input
addresses, not only address zero. Sequential and compiled native
execution agree on the full address set.

Ordered receipt construction

Before endpoint collapse, retain for event r

(j_(r), ϵ_(r), a_(r − 1), a_(r))

The JC1-to-W9 adapter assigns event r to reciprocal channel r, with

L_(r) = ϵ_(r),   R_(r) = −ϵ_(r)

The W9 common and directional components are

C_(r) = L_(r) + R_(r) = 0,   A_(r) = L_(r) − R_(r) = 2ϵ_(r)

Therefore the positive four-event receipt begins ((2,2,2,2)), the
negative receipt begins ((-2,-2,-2,-2)), and the double receipt has
eight positive entries. Their L¹ receipt norms are 8, 8 and 16, and
their semantic hashes are distinct. Reciprocal reconstruction is

$$L_r=\frac{C_r+A_r}{2},
\qquad
R_r=\frac{C_r-A_r}{2}$$

The exact endpoint is the same; event count, sign, order and receipt
remain different.

JC1 result and boundary

The principal campaign passes 18/18 and independent validation passes
9/9. The complete comparison preserves the endpoint equality and the
distinct ordered receipts.

The established portable statement is:

- endpoint compilation can erase which closed program occurred;

- an ordered event receipt can retain that distinction.

This directly joins the append-only reel of SAMA-D000042. It does not
imply that a particular carrier, intersection or matter ledger consumes
the full JC1 polynomial object.

ω(w) = z̃(w)/4 ∈ ℤ⁹

H_(local) ≅ ℤ²

$$\mathcal T_{rj}

=\sum_{p\ge r}c_{pj}\binom{p}{r} u_0^{p-r},

Future matter bridge boundary

A future Jacobian object reaches a matter application only if one

    and

The first two establish transport; the third establishes consumption;
the fourth establishes consequence. A history hash that survives
delivery without a consumer is not the completed bridge. A consumer that
changes for an undeclared correlated field is not the controlled payload
result.

No current JC1/HF field has completed this chain. Depth training, Q2

Chapter XXVII Relational Composition

The retained foundation closes with two source-originated proposals
about relational composition and interaction. They connect the finite
objects developed in the preceding chapters and state the mathematical
assignments that a broader composition law would need. Their status as
proposals is kept explicit alongside the completed constructions that
motivate them.

The historical constructions in this chapter retain the fixed 8
September 2026 SAMA source stage. Later results are identified by their
own source records.

Orientation

SAM already contains three ingredients that are usually discussed at
different scales: a nonzero accumulation field sourced by matter, an
exact W8/X1/W9 write lifecycle with Theta carrier structure, and the
accumulated effect of many stellar sources in galaxy halos. The Nonzero
Amplification Principle identifies these as one recursive mechanism.

The central statement is:

  Matter writes. The substrate carries. Nonzero writes accumulate.

Matter does not have to occupy the route between two sources for
information to cross that route. A source changes the substrate
accumulation; the lifted substrate carries the source-conditioned write
through matter-free space. The same composition that turns
particle-scale writes into a body-scale presence can turn stellar writes
into a galactic accumulation halo.

This document records an owner-originated conceptual bridge for
continued mathematical development. It does not assign a test result
classification.

Established inputs and new principle

Established inputs

The new bridge begins from established, separately typed SAM structures:

- the universal accumulation floor A_0;

- the spherical source accumulation lift A(r);

- the closed-matter return to accumulation through source support and
  carrier compression;

- the W8/X1/W9 substrate-atom relation;

- Theta18 as a tensor carrier; and

- the Complete Exact Write computation surface.

These inputs retain their established definitions. The new concept does
not replace A_0, redefine W9 as a separate object, turn Theta18 into
matter, or replace the Exact Write with a continuum metaphor.

Owner originated Nonzero Amplification Principle

  Every nonzero constituent substrate write survives coherent
  composition, allowing particle-scale information to accumulate
  recursively into macroscopic and galactic accumulation structure.

The principle adds a cross-scale interpretation to the established
inputs:

1.  a particle contributes a nonzero source-conditioned write;

2.  many particle writes compose into the coherent write of a body;

3.  the body-scale write is carried by matter-free substrate;

4.  overlapping body writes establish local informational contact;

5.  body writes compose again at larger scales; and

6.  billions of stellar writes form the extended galactic accumulation
    halo.

The information bearing substrate

Introduce provisional notation for the source-conditioned lift:

ΔA_(S)(x) = A_(total, S)(x) − A₀

This notation distinguishes three states that must not be collapsed:

- Matter present. Meaning: A local material source participates in
  creating or changing the write..

- Matter absent and Delta A_S(x)>0. Meaning: The substrate remains
  source-conditioned and carries information without an intervening
  material messenger..

- Delta A_S(x)=0, equivalently the source route has returned to A=A_0.
  Meaning: The active source-conditioned connection is absent at that
  point..

The substrate is therefore not a passive geometric backdrop. Changes in
A change the local write environment. In the owner-originated mechanism,
those changes continually affect the W8/X1/W9 lifecycle and Theta
carrier activity throughout the connected matter-free region.

Define the source-conditioned contact domain provisionally by

Ω_(S) = {x : ΔA_(S)(x) > 0}

A route between two systems is in active source contact when it remains
in the appropriate connected component of this lifted domain. A point at
which the source-conditioned lift returns to zero supplies the proposed
disconnection condition.

Exact composition rather than point particle messaging

For a composite source S, write

ΔA_(S)(x) = 𝒞_(S) ({δA_(i)(x) : i ∈ S})

where each constituent contribution is nonzero on its active route and
𝒞_(S) denotes the exact composition law.

The symbol 𝒞_(S) is deliberately not replaced by ordinary linear
addition. SAM has an Exact Write, retained histories, typed carriers and
directional computation. The correct composition law should be recovered
or derived from that algebra so that cross terms, history and carrier
structure remain intact.

The conceptual content is nevertheless exact enough to distinguish the
mechanisms:

- an individual Earth particle does not send an isolated command to an
  individual Moon particle;

- all particle writes in Earth compose into an Earth-scale accumulated
  write;

- all particle writes in the Moon compose into a Moon-scale accumulated
  write; and

- their source-conditioned domains overlap in the matter-free substrate,
  where continuing changes in A, W8/X1/W9 and Theta provide local
  contact.

The Moon responds inside the Earth’s accumulated substrate write while
also contributing its own write to the same substrate.

One recursive bridge across three scales

Quantum to macroscopic

The quantum bridge is the passage from exact local substrate
transactions to a coherent composite source:

$$\{\text{particle Exact Writes}\}
\xrightarrow{\mathcal C}
\text{body-scale accumulated write}$$

Object-level informational presence need not be introduced as an
unrelated primitive if it is the coherent closure of the constituent
writes.

Earth to Moon

Earth and Moon participate in one shared lifted substrate. Their
continuing internal particle activity changes their composite writes;
those changes are carried through matter-free space by the substrate
itself. The orbital response is therefore downstream of an
information-bearing write process, not merely a description of the
resulting path.

Stars to galaxy

The galactic bridge applies the same composition recursively:

$$\{\text{stellar body writes}\}
\xrightarrow{\mathcal C}
\text{galaxy-scale accumulated halo}$$

Each star contributes a nonzero effect at large distance. Billions of
such contributions produce the extended galactic accumulation structure.
The particle-to-body and star-to-galaxy steps are thus two applications
of the same nonzero-preserving composition law.

Technical continuation

The next investigation begins with the exact algebra already present in
SAM:

1.  Composition operator. Identify 𝒞 inside Complete Exact Write and SLC
    execution, including retained-history and directional terms.

2.  Nonzero preservation. Determine the exact condition under which a
    constituent nonzero write survives composite closure.

3.  A-dependent lifecycle. State the operator by which a local change in
    A changes W8/X1/W9 execution and Theta carrier activity.

4.  Contact domain. Derive the connected A>A_0 route and the exact A=A_0
    disconnection condition.

5.  Recursive closure. Determine when a composite write is admitted as a
    source input to the same operation at the next scale.

6.  Existing SLC orbit. Inspect whether the current particle-orbit
    emulator already contains part of this composition and transport
    law.

7.  Cross-scale comparison. Express particle/body, Earth-Moon and
    star/galaxy examples through one shared receipt format.

This route is intended to discover the exact operator already implied by
the combined SAM structures, rather than introduce separate quantum,
orbital and galactic mechanisms.

Relational interaction and completed composition

Orientation

The first Nonzero Amplification document proposes that constituent
substrate writes can compose recursively from particles to bodies and
from stellar bodies to galaxies. The relational proposal adds the
following intended correction: composition depends on how the
constituents interact.

Two planets light-years apart and the same two planets closely orbiting
have the same constituent inventory. They do not have the same
reciprocal history, overlap, directional relation or completed substrate
write. The nearby orbiting pair forms a coherent composite source; the
distant pair approaches two separately carried source writes.

The central statement is:

  Nonzero amplification is relational. Interacting writes create
  completed information that is absent from an inventory-only sum.

Inventory and interaction are differently typed

Let S be a composite system. Its source data includes at least:

- constituent writes {w_i};

- a typed active relation network R_S;

- retained reciprocal or directional history H_S;

- the local and route-dependent accumulation state; and

- the completed W8/X1/W9 and Theta carrier state.

The proposed completed composition is therefore written schematically as

ΔA_(S) = 𝒞_(S) ({w_(i)}, R_(S), H_(S))

An inventory-only control would instead evaluate

ΔA_(diag) = ∑_(i)δA_(i)

The difference

ℐ_(S) = ΔA_(S) − ΔA_(diag)

is a useful diagnostic representation of the interaction innovation. It
does not define 𝒞_(S) as ordinary addition. The native composition must
retain Exact Write types, histories, cross terms and completion.

Separation changes the completed write

For the same two constituents, consider two relational states.

Distant state

The two source-conditioned writes are carried separately. Their active
overlap and continuing reciprocal history are reduced, and a route
returning to the universal floor supplies the proposed disconnection
condition.

Nearby orbiting state

Each source continually changes the accumulation environment of the
other. Those changes alter the matter-free substrate between them, the
local W8/X1/W9 lifecycle and Theta carrier/intersection activity. The
pair therefore generates a continuing relational history and becomes a
new composite source.

The distinction is not “two sources versus more sources.” It is:

(w₁, w₂, R_(distant), H_(distant)) ≠ (w₁, w₂, R_(orbit), H_(orbit))

Many body amplification

For many particles, the number of possible relations grows faster than
the constituent count. Pair, cycle, face and higher closure information
can therefore become load-bearing even when every individual particle
write is small.

A schematic cluster display is

ΔA_(S) ∼ ∑_(i)A_(i) + ∑_(i < j)ℐ_(ij) + ∑_(i < j < k)ℐ_(ijk) + ⋯

but the Exact Write composition owns the actual algebra. The display
only shows what a spreadsheet-style constituent sum omits: interaction
terms whose values depend on the completed relational state.

This gives Nonzero Amplification two inseparable aspects:

1.  each constituent contributes a nonzero write; and

2.  coherent interaction among those writes generates additional
    completed information.

ATOM3D consequence

ATOM3D has frequently represented candidate assemblies through counts,
independent relation rows and separately scored receipt features. The
current Li-6 action-frame frontier exposes the limit of that
representation: complete W9P custody retains each relation but supplies
no relation-to-frame identity, leaving 13,824 reciprocal frame
assignments.

Relational Nonzero Amplification changes the unit evaluated by a
successor. The unit should be the whole active relation network, with
each relation read in the context of all co-active writes:

(e, f_(e), R_(S), H_(S), ΔA_(S), Θ_(S)) ↦ ℐ_(e)(S)

Here e is a relation, f_e its action frame and ℐ_(e)(S) its
context-dependent innovation inside the completed assembly. The same
isolated edge placed in a different network need not produce the same
innovation.

The direct successor is therefore an interaction-aware composite-write
receipt, not another isolated edge selector. Candidate Li-6 assemblies
can then be compared by exact completed closure while preserving the
current bars against geometry fitting and opaque selection.

RH consequence

The corresponding RH distinction is between eventwise inventory and
completed coupling. If W is assembled from event writes W_i, then

W^(*)W = ∑_(i)W_(i)^(*)W_(i) + ∑_(i ≠ j)W_(i)^(*)W_(j)

The off-diagonal products carry relational information. Current RH work
already retains completed Gamma-prime-Robin coupling where eventwise
positivity is insufficient, and its support extensions are expressed
through transported carriers and entrant Schur innovations.

Relational Nonzero Amplification supplies a shared candidate origin for
those cross terms: they are the mathematical receipt of coexisting
source writes interacting inside one completed substrate history.

Technical continuation

1.  Recover 𝒞_(S) from Complete Exact Write and current SLC receipts.

2.  Compile whole-network interaction innovations without reducing
    relations to independent rows.

3.  Compare identical inventories under distant, nearby, orbiting and
    relation-deleted configurations.

4.  Determine which interaction data selects or reduces ATOM3D action
    frames.

5.  Express the resulting operator as a completed Gram or Schur
    innovation.

6.  Cross-walk that operator to the RH source-append architecture.

campaign was executed for this edition.

- Nonzero amplification quantum galactic bridge.

- Relational nonzero amplification interaction closure.

Chapter XXVIII Computational Interfaces Across the Three Volumes

The expanded computational record gives a concrete meaning to the
handoff between the volumes. Volume I supplies substrate fields,
propagation, clocks and physical records. Volume II supplies finite
material sources, preparations, interactions and their receiving
observables. Volume III supplies exact representations, executable
transformations and retained results that can be passed to the next
calculation. The interfaces are mathematical objects with declared maps,
so a useful result can travel without changing what its symbols count or
measure. [V3C01,V3C02,V3C03]

What crosses an interface

A sufficient computational handoff can be written schematically as
ℬ = (𝒮, μ, 𝒪, 𝒞, ℛ, 𝒟)
Here 𝒮 identifies the source and preparation, μ its state law or metric,
𝒪 the operator, 𝒞 its clock convention, ℛ the requested receiver and 𝒟
the retained dependencies. This tuple is an editorial organization of
the interfaces developed in the preceding chapters. The particular
calculation supplies its actual entries; it is not an additional
physical law.

For a joint spin table, 𝒮 is the signed integer Hamiltonian with ordered
ports, μ is the labelled configuration count and ℛ might be a
rational-field DOS or a crossing query. There need be no dynamical clock
in that static request. For a Gibbs response, μ is the original Gibbs
inner product, 𝒪 = N(I − P) and the retained history scale is N/ξ
uniform site attempts. For a signed-Write trajectory, the source path
produces calibrated elapsed intervals received by the full carrier
dynamics. Chapters VI and XVIII give the corresponding implemented
constructions. [V3S01,V3S13,V3S14,V3S09]

This organization also explains how two calculations can share a native
matrix operation while retaining different meanings. A Gram matrix may
count states, measure a Gibbs residual, retain a completed arithmetic
form or encode a receiver’s observable directions. Its entries and
metric establish the correspondence. Matching dimensions alone do not
supply it.

Volume I source time retained records and actual readouts

The signed-Write integral supplies an explicit source-to-clock map. It
receives the actual phase state and path, retains orientation and
waiting, and sends a certified duration interval into the unchanged
receiver. Different source orders with equal endpoints then produce
different complete histories. Native dependency preservation carries
that distinction through fresh sessions and later readouts.
[V3S08,V3S09]

The common-receiver and directional reconstruction work develops the
complementary direction: determine what history can be recovered from a
declared observation set. A receiver with signed phase and axis
occupancy resolves a larger class than either partial observation. A
source construction and its inverse therefore meet through the actual
measured or computed record, with preparation, direction and sampling
specified by the task. The application chapters retain those complete
observation contracts.

The prime-event construction supplies another direct clock interface.
Its microscopic Ising gate accumulates all-spin and output-spin activity
along retained source paths. The exact output quotient has a simple
two-state generator in its own activity coordinate, while actual
sampling occurs at source-dependent stopping times. Chapter XXV develops
how the hidden state and those stopping times remain observable. That
source clock, the integer candidate index and a physical seconds
assignment retain separate roles. [V3C04]

The resulting computational contribution to Volume I is specific: an
identified source can produce a duration, ordered record or response
function that a field or clock construction receives through a declared
map. This lets accumulation, propagation and clock questions use the
completed computational operands directly while keeping their own
physical definitions. [V3C01]

Volume II exact phase sources and complete material receivers

The phase-to-binary chart gives an immediate source correspondence. Its
energy factor and aligned coordinate make the joint Ising table an
executable phase-response object. Ordered boundaries retain which
contact receives which sign. Conditional spectra and source constants
remain available for thermal assembly; a ground-matched auxiliary
interaction retains its own thermal factor. [V3S02,V3S03,V3C02]

The later response work adds a second interface. Complete retained and
hidden source functions generate Grams, Dirichlet forms and action
residuals. A receiver carries the full signed mixed entries and the
background terms that belong to that physical source. The latest edge
calculation demonstrates a new source observable entering a sharper
lower response through an explicit Schur extension. The source, clock
and earlier maxima remain identifiable throughout. [V3S14,V3S15]

The applied matter chapters show the same discipline in actual
configurations: retain circulation ties, phase signs, incoming
preparation and post-contact memory, then ask the declared readout
question. Exact source selection, distinct-report selection and physical
energy comparison are different operations. Each has its completed
result and actual receiving interface. This gives Volume II reusable
computational methods for source construction, history and response,
with the material inventory preserved. [V3C02]

Volume III several useful kinds of exact object

The volume now develops several related return types. A full integer DOS
retains the number of configurations at each energy. A joint table adds
magnetization and ordered boundary information. A layered ground
certificate retains the minimum, its exact degeneracy, a witness and
exhaustive accounting. A certified matrix operation returns enclosures
and residual bounds. A retained method trace supplies its
operation/dependency graph. A prime or factor certificate answers its
specified arithmetic question. Their shared computational infrastructure
makes them composable when a receiving map is supplied.
[V3S01,V3S05,V3S06]

This distinction is productive when choosing a method. A thermal
response needs the energy distribution or a justified source
integration. A ground-energy question can use a minimum-preserving
reduction. An inverse-history question needs the observations and
ambiguity classes left by the actual receiver. An arithmetic transition
estimate needs its signed source and completed complementary terms. A
source-clock calculation needs the original path and clock map. The
complete object requested by the next step determines which retained
result can serve it.

The record carried into the next three volume pass

The manuscript preserves the original twelve-chapter foundation and
develops later completed work in separate chapters with an exact source
register. Historical revisions remain attached to their results. Later
installations, qualified methods and completed calculations have their
own dated sources. Proposed successors and stopped or partial campaigns
retain those states rather than being folded into completed totals.

That organization provides a usable starting point for the planned
review of all three volumes. The next pass can follow a concrete chain:
identify a receiving question, locate its existing source operands,
determine the missing map or calculation, and select an applicable exact
method. The present expansion supplies the completed work and its
interfaces; the comparative selection of new research targets belongs to
that subsequent review.

Completed objects remain available to the next calculation

The N5000 construction retains 1,530,259,992 primary joint records
across six complete signed graphs, with exact integer multiplicities for
every energy, magnetization and retained boundary in the specified
sources. The N2000 physical analysis uses its own exact counts to derive
residual entropy, excitation gaps and controlled thermal response. The
prime-event program turns a source-level diagnosis into a prospective
gate change and measures the improvement from one to forty-seven exact
billion-index trajectories out of sixty-four per profile. Its later
retained path reaches 1,039,515,000,000 decisions without an incorrect
event before its intentional stop. Exact exchange reaches length
thirteen, accounts for 67,108,864 states and composes with thirty-two
local-matter families using the completed factors.

These results share a practical mathematical consequence. The completed
calculation preserves the operands needed by a later operation:
conditional integer counts for a field query, retained history for an
inverse, source factors for a new matter spectrum, or the hidden-state
law for a clock-sensitive response. Their constructions and validations
establish the finite results presented here. Their explicit interfaces
make those results reusable across the three volumes.

Appendix B Revision summary

This revision expands the attached 107-page edition while retaining its
useful research exposition. The change is organized around the completed
mathematical and computational objects.

A full N5000 chapter. Adds the six-source construction, all primary
record totals, packet and gateway factorization, two injective
encodings, modular integer reconstruction, conditional closure and
moment checks, measured production, archive and source-recovery chain,
reusable responses and the separately normalized thermal and
occupied-window results.

A full N2000 physics chapter. Develops the attachment’s exact ground
states, degeneracies, commensurate residual-entropy derivation,
excitation gaps, field thresholds, bin-sensitive finite entropy and
controlled low-temperature heat capacities. Its source size and later
physical-readout provenance remain distinct from N5000.

A full prime-event reliability chapter. Explains the event-created
sieve, seven-spin source, hidden-spin elimination, finite-state
three-reading dynamics, first-error replay, prospective balance, matched
and fresh ensembles, Poisson and ideal-census analysis, and explicit
budget and temporal-confirmation comparisons. The later continuation and
clocks receive a separate chapter.

Executable history and exact matter. Adds the explicit equal-observation
witness, sufficient-observation criterion, reversible reconstruction and
weave. Gives exact exchange and symbolic matter a complete chapter from
source quartics through Bethe checks, length-13 spectra, reusable
composition, fusion and compatible collective interaction.

Starbreaker and arithmetic. Restores the closed-cycle and complete
two-channel receiver results, signed Li6 construction and native
production counts. Adds the original signed Möbius energy, its
history-sensitive modes and cancellation, finite continuation
certificates and the precisely conditional RH implication.

An editorial pass through the full baseline. Removes the duplicated
Chapter I overview and derivation while retaining its additional
controls, architecture and source labels. Replaces vague classification
formulas with the actual nearby result, comparison and scope. Preserves
negative outcomes and numerical successors in their relevant contexts.

Consistent presentation. Uses one chapter hierarchy, editable
mathematical equations, readable single-column pages, source keys and a
linked contents list. Definitions introduce the principal new formulas.
No flowcharts are included.

Scope of verification for this edition

The revision used source reading, bounded arithmetic and transcription
checks, source manifests, document structure checks and rendered-page
inspection. It did not rerun historical scientific campaigns or alter
installed research software, live sources, original volumes or sealed
records. The N5000 record total, prime-gate balance and budget
arithmetic, exact history witness, finite-state multiplicity totals and
selected N2000 entropy and tail-bound values were checked as editorial
arithmetic. Such checks catch transcription errors; they do not
establish novelty or replace the scientific validations reported by each
source.

The editable DOCX and PDF represent the same revised manuscript. The
companion evidence archive retains the chapter source text, source
manifests and editorial check records. Research bulk datasets remain at
the canonical locations named by their own archives.
